namespace Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabbedMdi
{
	public class UltraTabbedMdiManagerRole : ComponentRole
	{
		private UltraTabbedMdiManager mdiManager;

		protected override ISynchronizeInvoke SynchronizingObject => mdiManager.MdiParent;

		public UltraTabbedMdiManagerRole(UltraTabbedMdiManager mdiManager)
			: base((Component)(object)mdiManager, "UltraTabbedMdiManager")
		{
			this.mdiManager = mdiManager;
			((ComponentRole)this).PropertiesCacheCount = 4;
		}

		protected override string[] GetRoleNames()
		{
			return StyleUtils.GetRoleNames();
		}

		protected override void OnStyleChanged()
		{
			mdiManager.OnAppStyleChanged();
		}
	}

	internal class StyleUtils
	{
		internal enum Role
		{
			TabItemVertLeft,
			TabItemVertRight,
			TabItemHorzTop,
			TabItemHorzBottom,
			TabItemAreaVertLeft,
			TabItemAreaVertRight,
			TabItemAreaHorzTop,
			TabItemAreaHorzBottom,
			TabClientAreaVert,
			TabClientAreaHorz,
			SplitterBar,
			SplitterBarVert,
			SplitterBarHorz,
			CloseButton,
			TabItemCloseButton,
			SelectedTabItemCloseButton,
			HeaderAreaCloseButton,
			TabListButton
		}

		internal enum CustomProperty
		{
			TabStyle = 0,
			HotTrack = 1,
			ViewStyle = 2,
			LastValue = 2
		}

		internal enum CachedProperty
		{
			BorderSplitter = 3,
			LastValue = 3
		}

		internal enum TabGroupCachedProperty
		{
			TabStyle,
			TabButtonStyle,
			ButtonStyle
		}

		internal const string UltraTabbedMdiManagerRoleName = "UltraTabbedMdiManager";

		internal const int ROLES_STEP = 5;

		internal const int ROLES_ROLE_ENUM = 0;

		internal const int ROLES_ROLE_NAME = 1;

		internal const int ROLES_BASE_ROLE_NAME = 2;

		internal const int ROLES_STATES = 3;

		internal const int ROLES_SUPPORTED_PROPS = 4;

		internal const int PROPS_STEP = 6;

		internal const int PROPS_PROP_NAME = 0;

		internal const int PROPS_PROP_TYPE = 1;

		internal const int PROPS_PROP_DEFAULT = 2;

		internal const int PROPS_PROP_CONVERTER = 3;

		internal const int PROPS_PROP_CONSTRAINT = 4;

		internal const int PROPS_PROP_DESC = 5;

		internal static readonly int TabGroupCachePropertyCount;

		static StyleUtils()
		{
			TabGroupCachePropertyCount = Enum.GetValues(typeof(TabGroupCachedProperty)).Length;
		}

		private StyleUtils()
		{
		}

		internal static object CacheBorderStylePropertyValue(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, CachedProperty prop, Role role, UIElementBorderStyle controlVal, UIElementBorderStyle resolveVal)
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UIElementBorderStyle roleBorderStyle = GetRoleBorderStyle(manager, role);
			return CachePropertyValue(manager, prop, roleBorderStyle, controlVal, (object)(UIElementBorderStyle)0, resolveVal);
		}

		internal static object CacheButtonStylePropertyValue(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, CachedProperty prop, Role role, UIElementButtonStyle controlVal, UIElementButtonStyle resolveVal)
		{
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0027: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UIElementButtonStyle buttonStyle = GetRole((IUltraControl)(object)manager, role).GetButtonStyle(((UltraStylableComponent)manager).ComponentRole);
			return CachePropertyValue(manager, prop, buttonStyle, controlVal, (object)(UIElementButtonStyle)0, resolveVal);
		}

		internal static object CacheButtonStylePropertyValue(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, TabGroupCachedProperty cacheProp, Role roleId, object controlVal, object fallbackVal)
		{
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			object defVal = (object)(UIElementButtonStyle)0;
			object styleVal = GetRoleButtonStyle(tabGroup.Manager, roleId);
			return CachePropertyValue(tabGroup, cacheProp, styleVal, controlVal, defVal, fallbackVal);
		}

		internal static object CacheUnresolvedPropertyValue(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, CustomProperty prop, object defVal)
		{
			return CachePropertyValue(manager, prop, defVal, defVal, defVal);
		}

		internal static object CacheUnresolvedPropertyValue(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, CachedProperty prop, object styleVal, object defVal)
		{
			return CachePropertyValue(manager, prop, styleVal, defVal, defVal, defVal);
		}

		internal static object CachePropertyValue(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, CachedProperty prop, object styleValue, object controlVal, object defVal, object resolveVal)
		{
			ComponentRole componentRole = GetComponentRole((ISupportAppStyling)(object)manager);
			if (componentRole != null)
			{
				return componentRole.CachePropertyValue(styleValue, controlVal, defVal, resolveVal, (int)prop);
			}
			return ResolveValueHelper(controlVal, defVal, resolveVal);
		}

		private static object ResolveValueHelper(object val, object defVal, object resolveVal)
		{
			if (defVal != resolveVal && object.Equals(val, defVal))
			{
				return resolveVal;
			}
			return val;
		}

		internal static object CachePropertyValue(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, CustomProperty prop, object styleValue, object controlVal, object defVal, object resolveVal)
		{
			ComponentRole componentRole = GetComponentRole((ISupportAppStyling)(object)manager);
			if (componentRole != null)
			{
				return componentRole.CachePropertyValue(styleValue, controlVal, defVal, resolveVal, (int)prop);
			}
			return ResolveValueHelper(controlVal, defVal, resolveVal);
		}

		internal static object CachePropertyValue(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, CustomProperty prop, object controlVal, object defVal, object resolveVal)
		{
			ComponentRole componentRole = GetComponentRole((ISupportAppStyling)(object)manager);
			if (componentRole != null)
			{
				string customPropertyName = GetCustomPropertyName(prop);
				object obj = null;
				if (componentRole.HasProperty(customPropertyName))
				{
					obj = componentRole.GetProperty(customPropertyName);
				}
				return componentRole.CachePropertyValue(obj, controlVal, defVal, resolveVal, (int)prop);
			}
			return ResolveValueHelper(controlVal, defVal, resolveVal);
		}

		internal static object CachePropertyValue(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, TabGroupCachedProperty prop, CustomProperty property, object controlVal, object defVal, object fallbackVal)
		{
			object styleVal = null;
			ComponentRole val = ((tabGroup.Manager != null) ? ((UltraStylableComponent)tabGroup.Manager).ComponentRole : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				string customPropertyName = GetCustomPropertyName(property);
				if (val.HasProperty(customPropertyName))
				{
					styleVal = val.GetProperty(customPropertyName);
				}
			}
			return CachePropertyValue(tabGroup, prop, styleVal, controlVal, defVal, fallbackVal);
		}

		internal static object CachePropertyValue(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, TabGroupCachedProperty prop, object styleVal, object controlVal, object defVal, object fallbackVal)
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			object obj = StyleUtilities.CalculateResolvedValue(GetResolutionOrder(tabGroup.Manager), styleVal, controlVal, defVal, fallbackVal);
			tabGroup.PropertyCache.CachePropertyValue((int)prop, obj);
			return obj;
		}

		[Conditional("DEBUG")]
		internal static void OutputUnsetCachedProps(ComponentRole role, int indexBeingSet)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			object obj = default(object);
			for (int i = 0; i < role.PropertiesCacheCount; i++)
			{
				if (i != indexBeingSet && !role.GetCachedProperty(i, ref obj))
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(",");
					}
					object value = ((i <= 2) ? ((object)(CustomProperty)i) : ((object)(CachedProperty)i));
					stringBuilder.Append(value);
				}
			}
			_ = stringBuilder.Length;
			_ = 0;
		}

		internal static UIElementBorderStyle CalculateBorderStyle(MdiTab tab, UIRole role, CachedProperty property, UIElementBorderStyle controlVal, UIElementBorderStyle fallback)
		{
			//IL_0038: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0056: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = tab.Manager;
			if (!GetCachedPropertyVal(manager, property, out var val))
			{
				val = CachePropertyValue(manager, property, role.BorderStyle, null, (object)(UIElementBorderStyle)0, (object)(UIElementBorderStyle)0);
			}
			return (UIElementBorderStyle)StyleUtilities.CalculateResolvedValue(((UltraStylableComponent)manager).ComponentRole.ResolutionOrder, val, (object)controlVal, (object)(UIElementBorderStyle)0, (object)fallback);
		}

		internal static UIElementButtonStyle CalculateButtonStyle(MdiTab tab, UIRole role, CachedProperty property, UIElementButtonStyle controlVal, UIElementButtonStyle fallback)
		{
			//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0044: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0050: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = tab.Manager;
			if (!GetCachedPropertyVal(manager, property, out var val))
			{
				val = CachePropertyValue(manager, property, role.GetButtonStyle(((UltraStylableComponent)manager).ComponentRole), null, (object)(UIElementButtonStyle)0, (object)(UIElementButtonStyle)0);
			}
			return (UIElementButtonStyle)StyleUtilities.CalculateResolvedValue(((UltraStylableComponent)manager).ComponentRole.ResolutionOrder, val, (object)controlVal, (object)(UIElementButtonStyle)0, (object)fallback);
		}

		internal static void DirtyCachedPropertyVals(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager)
		{
			ComponentRole componentRole = GetComponentRole((ISupportAppStyling)(object)manager);
			if (componentRole != null)
			{
				componentRole.ClearCachedPropertyValues();
			}
			if (manager == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in manager.TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
			{
				nestedTabGroup.PropertyCache.ClearCachedPropertyValues();
			}
		}

		internal static UIElementButtonStyle GetButtonStyle(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager)
		{
			//IL_0013: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (manager == null || ((UltraStylableComponent)manager).ComponentRole == null)
			{
				return (UIElementButtonStyle)0;
			}
			return ((UltraStylableComponent)manager).ComponentRole.ButtonStyle;
		}

		internal static bool GetCachedPropertyVal(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, TabGroupCachedProperty prop, out object val)
		{
			return tabGroup.PropertyCache.GetCachedProperty((int)prop, ref val);
		}

		internal static bool GetCachedPropertyVal(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, CustomProperty prop, out object val)
		{
			return GetCachedPropertyVal(manager, (int)prop, out val);
		}

		internal static bool GetCachedPropertyVal(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, CachedProperty prop, out object val)
		{
			return GetCachedPropertyVal(manager, (int)prop, out val);
		}

		private static bool GetCachedPropertyVal(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, int prop, out object val)
		{
			ComponentRole componentRole = GetComponentRole((ISupportAppStyling)(object)manager);
			if (componentRole != null)
			{
				return componentRole.GetCachedProperty(prop, ref val);
			}
			val = null;
			return false;
		}

		internal static string[] GetRoleNames()
		{
			object[] roleInfo = GetRoleInfo();
			string[] array = new string[roleInfo.Length / 5];
			for (int i = 0; i < roleInfo.Length; i += 5)
			{
				string text = (string)roleInfo[i + 1];
				array[i / 5] = text;
			}
			return array;
		}

		internal static AppStyleProperty[] GetCustomProperties()
		{
			//IL_0031: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0038: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_003d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0044: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0055: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			object[] customPropertiesInfo = GetCustomPropertiesInfo();
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			for (int i = 0; i < customPropertiesInfo.Length; i += 6)
			{
				string text = (string)customPropertiesInfo[i];
				Type type = (Type)customPropertiesInfo[1 + i];
				object defaultValue = customPropertiesInfo[2 + i];
				AppStylePropertyConverter val = (AppStylePropertyConverter)customPropertiesInfo[3 + i];
				ValueConstraint constraint = (ValueConstraint)customPropertiesInfo[4 + i];
				string name = (string)customPropertiesInfo[5 + i];
				AppStyleProperty val2 = new AppStyleProperty(text, type, val);
				val2.DefaultValue = defaultValue;
				val2.Constraint = constraint;
				val2.Description = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(name);
				arrayList.Add(val2);
			}
			return (AppStyleProperty[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(AppStyleProperty));
		}

		internal static UIRoleDefinition[] GetRoleDefinitions()
		{
			//IL_0042: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0047: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			//IL_0071: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

				{
					val2 |= array[j];

				arrayList.Add((object)new UIRoleDefinition(text, text2, val2, val));
			}
			return (UIRoleDefinition[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(UIRoleDefinition));
		}

		internal static RoleState GetRoleState(MdiTab.AppearanceResolutionPhase phase)
		{
			//IL_0029: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0033: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0039: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0038: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			return (RoleState)(phase switch
			{
				MdiTab.AppearanceResolutionPhase.Tab => 1, 
				MdiTab.AppearanceResolutionPhase.Selected => 2, 
				MdiTab.AppearanceResolutionPhase.Active => 16, 
				MdiTab.AppearanceResolutionPhase.HotTrack => 4, 
				MdiTab.AppearanceResolutionPhase.Final => 1, 
				_ => 1, 
			});
		}

		internal static object GetViewStyle(ISupportAppStyling stylableObj, CustomProperty viewStyleProp)
		{
			//IL_0036: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0060: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			ComponentRole componentRole = stylableObj.ComponentRole;
			object obj = null;
			if (componentRole != null)
			{
				string customPropertyName = GetCustomPropertyName(viewStyleProp);
				if (componentRole.HasProperty(customPropertyName))
				{
					obj = componentRole.GetProperty(customPropertyName);
				}
				if (obj == null || object.Equals(obj, ViewStyle.Default))
				{
					ViewStyle viewStyle = componentRole.ViewStyle;
					switch (viewStyle - 1)
					{
					case 3:
						obj = ViewStyle.Office2003;
						break;
					case 4:
						obj = ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005;
						break;
					case 0:
						obj = ViewStyle.Standard;
						break;
					case 5:
					case 6:
						obj = ViewStyle.Office2007;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return obj;
		}
	}

			ComponentRoleDefinition val = new ComponentRoleDefinition("UltraTabbedMdiManager", typeof(UltraTabbedMdiManager));
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList(StyleUtils.GetRoleNames());

			arrayList.Add("ScrollBar");
			arrayList.Add("ScrollBarTrackVertical");
			arrayList.Add("ScrollBarTrackHorizontal");

			arrayList.Add("ScrollBarThumbHorizontal");
			val.RelatedRoles = (string[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(string));
			val.UsesToolTips = true;
			val.CustomProperties = StyleUtils.GetCustomProperties();
			val.SupportedProperties = (SupportedComponentProperties)60;
			return (ComponentRoleDefinition[])(object)new ComponentRoleDefinition[1] { val };
		}
	}
	internal class DragDropHelper
	{
		private UltraTabbedMdiManager manager;

		private MdiTabGroup tabGroup;

		private DateTime scrollStartTime = DateTime.MaxValue;

		private DragScrollDirection scrollDirection = (DragScrollDirection)2;

		private DateTime lastScrollTime = DateTime.MaxValue;

		private static readonly TimeSpan InitialScrollDelay = new TimeSpan(0, 0, 0, 0, 500);

		private static readonly TimeSpan ScrollModeDelay = new TimeSpan(0, 0, 0, 0, 100);

		private TimeSpan scrollDelay = InitialScrollDelay;

		private const int ScrollThreshold = -20;

		private ITabItem dragOverTab;

		private DateTime tabEnterTime = DateTime.MaxValue;

		private static readonly TimeSpan InitialActivateDelay = new TimeSpan(0, 0, 0, 1, 0);

		private static readonly TimeSpan ActivateModeDelay = new TimeSpan(0, 0, 0, 0, 100);

		private TimeSpan activateDelay = InitialActivateDelay;

		internal DragDropHelper(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager)
		{
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			this.manager = manager;
		}

		internal void DragEnter(MdiTabGroupControl tabGroupControl, DragEventArgs e)
		{
			ProcessDragOver(tabGroupControl, e);
		}

		internal void DragDrop(MdiTabGroupControl tabGroupControl, DragEventArgs e)
		{
			ResetScrollInfo();
			ResetActivateInfo(resetDelayToInitial: true);
			tabGroup = null;
		}

		internal void DragOver(MdiTabGroupControl tabGroupControl, DragEventArgs e)
		{
			ProcessDragOver(tabGroupControl, e);
		}

		internal void DragLeave(MdiTabGroupControl tabGroupControl)
		{
			ResetScrollInfo();
			ResetActivateInfo(resetDelayToInitial: true);
			tabGroup = null;
		}

		private void ProcessDragOver(MdiTabGroupControl tabGroupControl, DragEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_005d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0062: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0084: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_008c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (tabGroupControl == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			tabGroup = tabGroupControl.TabGroup;
			if (tabGroup == null || ((Control)(object)tabGroupControl).IsDisposed || ((Control)(object)tabGroupControl).Disposing)
			{
				ResetScrollInfo();
				ResetActivateInfo(resetDelayToInitial: true);
				return;
			}
			Point p = new Point(e.X, e.Y);
			Point point = ((Control)(object)tabGroupControl).PointToClient(p);
			TabHitTestInfo hitInfo = tabGroup.TabManager.HitTest(point);
			if (!((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).RowAreaRect.Contains(point))
			{
				ResetScrollInfo();
				ResetActivateInfo(resetDelayToInitial: true);
			}
			else
			{
				ProcessDragScroll(hitInfo);
				ProcessTabActivate(hitInfo);
			}
		}

		private void ProcessTabActivate(TabHitTestInfo hitInfo)
		{
			if (((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).Tab == null)
			{
				ResetActivateInfo(resetDelayToInitial: false);
				return;
			}
			DateTime now = DateTime.Now;
			if (((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).Tab != dragOverTab)
			{
				dragOverTab = ((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).Tab;
				tabEnterTime = now;
				return;
			}
			TimeSpan timeSpan = now.Subtract(tabEnterTime);
			if (timeSpan > activateDelay && (!(lastScrollTime != DateTime.MaxValue) || !(now.Subtract(lastScrollTime) < new TimeSpan(0, 0, 0, 0, 200))))
			{
				ITabItem firstDisplayedTab = (ITabItem)(object)tabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab;
				tabGroup.SelectedTab = dragOverTab as MdiTab;
				if ((object)firstDisplayedTab != tabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab)
				{
					scrollStartTime = now;
					activateDelay = new TimeSpan(0, 0, 0, 0, 500);
					dragOverTab = null;
				}
				else
				{
					activateDelay = ActivateModeDelay;
				}
			}
		}

		private bool ProcessDragScroll(TabHitTestInfo hitInfo)
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0044: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0070: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0076: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_007c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0083: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if ((int)((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).ScrollDirection == 0 || ((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).Distance < -20)
			{
				ResetScrollInfo();
				return false;
			}
			DateTime now = DateTime.Now;
			if (scrollDirection != ((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).ScrollDirection)
			{
				scrollDelay = InitialScrollDelay;
				scrollDirection = ((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).ScrollDirection;
				scrollStartTime = now;
				return false;
			}
			TimeSpan timeSpan = now.Subtract(scrollStartTime);
			if (timeSpan >= scrollDelay)
			{
				ScrollType scrollType = (ScrollType)((int)((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).ScrollDirection == 1);
				tabGroup.Scroll(scrollType);
				scrollStartTime = now;
				lastScrollTime = now;
				scrollDelay = ScrollModeDelay;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void ResetActivateInfo(bool resetDelayToInitial)
		{
			dragOverTab = null;
			tabEnterTime = DateTime.MaxValue;
			if (resetDelayToInitial)
			{
				activateDelay = InitialActivateDelay;
			}
		}

		private void ResetScrollInfo()
		{
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			scrollStartTime = DateTime.MaxValue;
			scrollDirection = (DragScrollDirection)0;
			lastScrollTime = DateTime.MaxValue;
			scrollDelay = InitialScrollDelay;
		}
	}

	public class MdiTabGroupResizingEventArgs : CancelEventArgs
	{
		private MdiTabGroupsCollection tabGroups;

		private int[] newExtents;

		public MdiTabGroupsCollection TabGroups => tabGroups;

		public MdiTabGroupResizingEventArgs(MdiTabGroupsCollection tabGroups, int[] newExtents)
		{
			if (newExtents == null || tabGroups == null || tabGroups.Count != newExtents.Length)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_0"));
			}
			this.tabGroups = tabGroups;
			this.newExtents = newExtents;
		}

		public int GetNewExtent(int index)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index > TabGroups.Count - 1)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_1"), index, Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_2"));
			}
			return newExtents[index];
		}

		public int GetNewExtent(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			int index = TabGroups.IndexOf(tabGroup);
			return GetNewExtent(index);
		}

		public int[] GetNewExtents()
		{
			int[] array = new int[newExtents.Length];
			newExtents.CopyTo(array, 0);
			return array;
		}
	}

	internal class MdiChildSettings
	{
		private Form form;

		private bool isInitialized;

		private UltraTabbedMdiManager manager;

		private bool captionSettingsWereCleared;

		private bool controlBox;

		private FormBorderStyle formBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.Sizable;

		private bool maximizeBox;

		private bool minimizeBox;

		private bool helpButton;

		private Size maximumSize = Size.Empty;

		private Size minimumSize = Size.Empty;

		private Size size = Size.Empty;

		private Point location = Point.Empty;

		private SizeGripStyle sizeGripStyle;

		private FormWindowState windowState;

		private Size autoScrollMinSize = Size.Empty;

		internal bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;

		internal bool IsRoundedForm
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0026: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_002c: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				if (manager != null && manager.ToolbarsManager != null)
				{
					return (int)manager.ToolbarsManager.GetFormDisplayStyle(form) != 3;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal MdiChildSettings(Form form, UltraTabbedMdiManager manager)
		{
			this.form = form;
			this.manager = manager;
		}

		internal void Initialize()
		{
			if (isInitialized)
			{
				return;
			}
			controlBox = form.ControlBox;
			formBorderStyle = form.FormBorderStyle;
			maximizeBox = form.MaximizeBox;
			minimizeBox = form.MinimizeBox;
			helpButton = form.HelpButton;
			maximumSize = form.MaximumSize;
			minimumSize = form.MinimumSize;
			autoScrollMinSize = form.AutoScrollMinSize;
			this.size = form.Size;
			location = form.Location;
			if (form.WindowState != FormWindowState.Normal && form.IsHandleCreated)
			{
				PermissionSet permissionSet = new PermissionSet(null);
				permissionSet.AddPermission(new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode));
				permissionSet.AddPermission(new UIPermission(UIPermissionWindow.SafeTopLevelWindows));
				try
				{
					permissionSet.Assert();
					permissionSet.Demand();
					NativeWindowMethods.WINDOWPLACEMENT lpwndpl = default(NativeWindowMethods.WINDOWPLACEMENT);
					if (NativeWindowMethods.GetWindowPlacementApi(form.Handle, ref lpwndpl))
					{
						Rectangle rect = lpwndpl.rcNormalPosition.Rect;
						location = rect.Location;
						this.size = rect.Size;
					}
					CodeAccessPermission.RevertAssert();
					CodeAccessPermission.RevertAssert();
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}
			sizeGripStyle = form.SizeGripStyle;
			windowState = form.WindowState;
			isInitialized = true;
			form.SuspendLayout();
			if (form.StartPosition != FormStartPosition.Manual)
			{
				form.StartPosition = FormStartPosition.Manual;
			}
			if (!minimumSize.IsEmpty)
			{
				form.MinimumSize = Size.Empty;
				Size size = minimumSize;
				size.Width -= form.Width - form.ClientSize.Width;
				size.Height -= form.Height - form.ClientSize.Height;
				size.Width = Math.Max(0, size.Width);
				size.Height = Math.Max(0, size.Height);
				if (size.Height > 0 || size.Width > 0)
				{
					Size size2;
					if (autoScrollMinSize.IsEmpty)
					{
						size2 = size;
					}
					else
					{
						int width = Math.Min(size.Width, autoScrollMinSize.Width);
						int height = Math.Min(size.Height, autoScrollMinSize.Height);
						size2 = new Size(width, height);
					}
					form.AutoScrollMinSize = size2;
				}
			}
			if (!IsRoundedForm)
			{
				captionSettingsWereCleared = true;
				form.FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.None;
				form.HelpButton = false;
				form.MaximizeBox = false;
				form.MinimizeBox = false;
			}
			else
			{
				captionSettingsWereCleared = false;
			}
			form.SizeGripStyle = SizeGripStyle.Hide;
			if (form.WindowState != FormWindowState.Normal)
			{
				form.WindowState = FormWindowState.Normal;
			}
			form.ResumeLayout(performLayout: true);
		}

		internal void Restore()
		{
			if (isInitialized && !form.IsDisposed && !form.Disposing)
			{
				isInitialized = false;
				form.SuspendLayout();
				if (captionSettingsWereCleared)
				{
					captionSettingsWereCleared = false;
					form.ControlBox = controlBox;
					form.FormBorderStyle = formBorderStyle;
					form.MaximizeBox = maximizeBox;
					form.MinimizeBox = minimizeBox;
					form.HelpButton = helpButton;
				}
				form.MaximumSize = maximumSize;
				form.MinimumSize = minimumSize;
				form.Size = size;
				form.SizeGripStyle = sizeGripStyle;
				if (!minimumSize.IsEmpty)
				{
					form.AutoScrollMinSize = autoScrollMinSize;
				}
				form.Location = location;
				form.WindowState = windowState;
				form.ResumeLayout(performLayout: true);
			}
		}
	}
	[SuppressUnmanagedCodeSecurity]
	[SecurityCritical]
	internal class MdiClientSubclasser : NativeWindow, IMdiClientWindow
	{
		private const int WM_MDICASCADE = 551;

		private const int WM_MDIICONARRANGE = 552;

		private const int WM_MDIRESTORE = 547;

		private const int WM_MDITILE = 550;

		private const int WM_MDIMAXIMIZE = 549;

		private const int WM_MDIACTIVATE = 546;

		private const int WM_MDINEXT = 548;

		private const int WM_ERASEBKGND = 20;

		private const int WM_PARENTNOTIFY = 528;

		private const int WM_CREATE = 1;

		private const int WM_NCPAINT = 133;

		private const int WM_NCCALCSIZE = 131;

		private UltraTabbedMdiManager manager;

		private Rectangle displayRect = Rectangle.Empty;

		private bool IsManagerEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				if (manager != null)
				{
					return ((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)manager).Enabled;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private Rectangle DisplayRectangle => displayRect;

		bool IMdiClientWindow.IsActive => manager != null;

		IntPtr IMdiClientWindow.Handle => base.Handle;

		internal MdiClientSubclasser(UltraTabbedMdiManager mdiManager)
		{
			manager = mdiManager;
			UpdateBorderStyle();
		}

		protected override void WndProc(ref Message m)
		{
			//IL_030b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0310: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UltraTabbedMdiManager ultraTabbedMdiManager = manager;
			if (ultraTabbedMdiManager == null)
			{
				base.WndProc(ref m);
				return;
			}
			switch (m.Msg)
			{
			case 547:
			case 549:
			case 550:
			case 551:
			case 552:
				if (((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)ultraTabbedMdiManager).Enabled)
				{
					m.Result = IntPtr.Zero;
					return;
				}
				break;
			case 548:
			{
				Form form = null;
				if (m.WParam == IntPtr.Zero)
				{
					Control control = Control.FromChildHandle(base.Handle);
					if (control is Form)
					{
						form = ((Form)control).ActiveMdiChild;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					form = Control.FromHandle(m.WParam) as Form;
				}
				bool forward = m.LParam == IntPtr.Zero;
				Form form2 = GetNextWindow(form, forward);
				if (ultraTabbedMdiManager.TabNavigationMode == MdiTabNavigationMode.VisibleOrder)
				{
					form2?.Activate();
					m.Result = IntPtr.Zero;
					return;
				}
				bool fireSelectedEvent = false;
				if (!SendActivationNotification(form2, form, ref fireSelectedEvent))
				{
					break;
				}
				if (AllowMdiChildActivate(form2, fireSelectedEvent))
				{
					ultraTabbedMdiManager.SubclasserActivatingMdiChild = true;
					try
					{
						base.WndProc(ref m);
						return;
					}
					finally
					{
						ultraTabbedMdiManager.SubclasserActivatingMdiChild = false;
						ultraTabbedMdiManager.AfterSubclasserFormActivation(form2, form, fireSelectedEvent);
					}
				}
				m.Result = IntPtr.Zero;
				return;
			}
			case 546:
			{
				Form form3 = Control.FromHandle(m.WParam) as Form;
				bool fireSelectedEvent2 = false;
				if (!SendActivationNotification(form3, null, ref fireSelectedEvent2))
				{
					break;
				}
				if (AllowMdiChildActivate(form3, fireSelectedEvent2))
				{
					ultraTabbedMdiManager.SubclasserActivatingMdiChild = true;
					try
					{
						base.WndProc(ref m);
						return;
					}
					finally
					{
						ultraTabbedMdiManager.SubclasserActivatingMdiChild = false;
						ultraTabbedMdiManager.AfterSubclasserFormActivation(form3, null, fireSelectedEvent2);
					}
				}
				m.Result = IntPtr.Zero;
				return;
			}
			case 133:
			{
				base.WndProc(ref m);
				if (!IsManagerEnabled)
				{
					return;
				}
				IntPtr intPtr = IntPtr.Zero;
				Graphics graphics = null;
				IntPtr hWnd = m.HWnd;
				try
				{
					Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle(Point.Empty, DisplayRectangle.Size);
					if (m.WParam == IntPtr.Zero || m.WParam.ToInt64() == 1)
					{
						intPtr = NativeWindowMethods.GetWindowDC(hWnd);
					}
					else
					{
						intPtr = NativeWindowMethods.GetDCEx(hWnd, m.WParam, 129);
						if (intPtr == IntPtr.Zero)
						{
							intPtr = NativeWindowMethods.GetWindowDC(m.HWnd);
						}
					}
					if (intPtr != IntPtr.Zero)
					{
						SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
						if (Utilities.CanAssert)
						{
							securityPermission.Assert();
						}
						graphics = Graphics.FromHdc(intPtr);
						CodeAccessPermission.RevertAssert();
						DrawBorder(graphics, rectangle, rectangle);
						m.Result = IntPtr.Zero;
					}
					return;
				}
				finally
				{
					graphics?.Dispose();
					if (intPtr != IntPtr.Zero)
					{
						NativeWindowMethods.ReleaseDC(hWnd, intPtr);
					}
				}
			}
			case 131:
				if (IsManagerEnabled && m.WParam.ToInt64() == 1)
				{
					NativeWindowMethods.NCCALCSIZE_PARAMS nCCALCSIZE_PARAMS = (NativeWindowMethods.NCCALCSIZE_PARAMS)m.GetLParam(typeof(NativeWindowMethods.NCCALCSIZE_PARAMS));
					UIElementBorderWidths borderWidth = GetBorderWidth();
					displayRect = nCCALCSIZE_PARAMS.rectProposed.Rect;
					nCCALCSIZE_PARAMS.rectProposed.left += ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref borderWidth)).Left;
					nCCALCSIZE_PARAMS.rectProposed.right -= ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref borderWidth)).Right;
					nCCALCSIZE_PARAMS.rectProposed.top += ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref borderWidth)).Top;
					nCCALCSIZE_PARAMS.rectProposed.bottom -= ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref borderWidth)).Bottom;
					Marshal.StructureToPtr((object)nCCALCSIZE_PARAMS, m.LParam, fDeleteOld: false);
				}
				break;
			case 20:
				base.WndProc(ref m);
				if (IsManagerEnabled)
				{
					m.Result = new IntPtr(1);
				}
				return;
			case 528:
				if ((m.WParam.ToInt32() & 0xFFFF) == 1)
				{
					manager.MdiClientCreateNotification();
				}
				break;
			}
			base.WndProc(ref m);
		}

		private bool SendActivationNotification(Form form, Form previousForm, ref bool fireSelectedEvent)
		{
			if (manager == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return manager.ShouldSubclasserNotifyFormActivation(form, previousForm, ref fireSelectedEvent);
		}

		private bool AllowMdiChildActivate(Form form, bool fireTabSelecting)
		{
			if (manager != null)
			{
				return manager.ShouldSubclasserAllowFormActivation(form, fireTabSelecting);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private Form GetNextWindow(Form mdiChild, bool forward)
		{
			if (mdiChild == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (manager != null && manager.TabNavigationMode == MdiTabNavigationMode.VisibleOrder)
			{
				MdiTab mdiTab = manager.TabFromForm(mdiChild);
				MdiTab mdiTab2 = null;
				if (mdiTab != null)
				{
					mdiTab2 = mdiTab.GetNextPreviousTab(wrap: true, forward);
				}
				return mdiTab2?.Form;
			}
			MdiClient mdiClient = Control.FromHandle(base.Handle) as MdiClient;
			int childIndex = mdiClient.Controls.GetChildIndex(mdiChild, throwException: false);
			if (childIndex < 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			int count = mdiClient.Controls.Count;
			Control control = null;
			if (forward)
			{
				for (int i = childIndex + 1; i < count * 2; i++)
				{
					Control control2 = mdiClient.Controls[i % count];
					if (control2.CanSelect)
					{
						control = control2;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				for (int num = childIndex - 1 + count; num > childIndex; num--)
				{
					Control control3 = mdiClient.Controls[num % count];
					if (control3.CanSelect)
					{
						control = control3;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return control as Form;
		}

		private void Unhook()
		{
			manager = null;
			UpdateBorderStyle();
		}

		private void DrawBorder(Graphics g, Rectangle rect, Rectangle clipRect)
		{
			if (!IsManagerEnabled)
			{
				return;
			}
			Color color = manager.BorderColor;
			Pen pen = null;
			switch (manager.BorderStyle)
			{
			case MdiClientBorderStyle.Inset:
				if (color.IsEmpty)
				{
					color = SystemColors.Control;
				}
				if (color == SystemColors.Control)
				{
					DrawBorderHelper(g, rect, clipRect, Border3DSide.Left | Border3DSide.Top, SystemColors.ControlDark, ref pen);
					DrawBorderHelper(g, rect, clipRect, Border3DSide.Right | Border3DSide.Bottom, SystemColors.ControlLightLight, ref pen);
					rect.Inflate(-1, -1);
					DrawBorderHelper(g, rect, clipRect, Border3DSide.Left | Border3DSide.Top, SystemColors.ControlDarkDark, ref pen);
					DrawBorderHelper(g, rect, clipRect, Border3DSide.Right | Border3DSide.Bottom, SystemColors.ControlLight, ref pen);
				}
				else
				{
					DrawBorderHelper(g, rect, clipRect, Border3DSide.Left | Border3DSide.Top, DrawUtility.Dark(color), ref pen);
					DrawBorderHelper(g, rect, clipRect, Border3DSide.Right | Border3DSide.Bottom, DrawUtility.LightLight(color), ref pen);
					rect.Inflate(-1, -1);
					DrawBorderHelper(g, rect, clipRect, Border3DSide.Left | Border3DSide.Top, DrawUtility.DarkDark(color), ref pen);
					DrawBorderHelper(g, rect, clipRect, Border3DSide.Right | Border3DSide.Bottom, DrawUtility.Light(color), ref pen);
				}
				break;
			case MdiClientBorderStyle.Solid:
				if (color.IsEmpty)
				{
					color = SystemColors.ControlDark;
				}
				DrawBorderHelper(g, rect, clipRect, Border3DSide.All, color, ref pen);
				break;
			}
			pen?.Dispose();
		}

		private void DrawBorderHelper(Graphics g, Rectangle rect, Rectangle invalidRect, Border3DSide sides, Color color, ref Pen pen)
		{
			if (sides == (Border3DSide)0)
			{
				return;
			}
			Rectangle rectangle = rect;
			rectangle.Width--;
			rectangle.Height--;
			if (rectangle.Height > 0 && rectangle.Width > 0)
			{
				if (pen == null)
				{
					pen = Utilities.CreatePen(color);
				}
				else if (pen.Color != color)
				{
					pen.Color = Utilities.GetNonSystemColor(color);
				}
				if ((sides & Border3DSide.Left) != 0 && invalidRect.Left <= rectangle.Left)
				{
					g.DrawLine(pen, rectangle.Left, rectangle.Bottom, rectangle.Left, rectangle.Top);
				}
				if ((sides & Border3DSide.Right) != 0 && invalidRect.Right >= rectangle.Right - 1)
				{
					g.DrawLine(pen, rectangle.Right, rectangle.Top, rectangle.Right, rectangle.Bottom);
				}
				if ((sides & Border3DSide.Top) != 0 && invalidRect.Top <= rectangle.Top)
				{
					g.DrawLine(pen, rectangle.Left, rectangle.Top, rectangle.Right, rectangle.Top);
				}
				if ((sides & Border3DSide.Bottom) != 0 && invalidRect.Bottom >= rectangle.Bottom - 1)
				{
					g.DrawLine(pen, rectangle.Right, rectangle.Bottom, rectangle.Left, rectangle.Bottom);
				}
			}
		}

		private UIElementBorderWidths GetBorderWidth()
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0082: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UIElementBorderWidths result = default(UIElementBorderWidths);
			if (IsManagerEnabled)
			{
				switch (manager.BorderStyle)
				{
				case MdiClientBorderStyle.Inset:
				{
					int num = (((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref result)).Bottom = 2);
					int num3 = (((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref result)).Top = num);
					int left = (((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref result)).Right = num3);
					((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref result)).Left = left;
					break;
				}
				case MdiClientBorderStyle.Solid:
				{
					int num = (((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref result)).Bottom = 1);
					int num3 = (((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref result)).Top = num);
					int left = (((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref result)).Right = num3);
					((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref result)).Left = left;
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override void OnHandleChange()
		{
			base.OnHandleChange();
			if (IsManagerEnabled)
			{
				UpdateBorderStyle();
			}
		}

		private void UpdateBorderStyle()
		{
			if (!IsManagerEnabled)
			{
				NativeWindowMethods.UpdateBorderStyle(base.Handle, BorderStyle.Fixed3D);
			}
			else
			{
				NativeWindowMethods.UpdateBorderStyle(base.Handle, BorderStyle.None);
			}
		}

		void IMdiClientWindow.BorderColorChanged()
		{
			if (!IsManagerEnabled)
			{
				return;
			}
			SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
			try
			{
				if (Utilities.CanAssert)
				{
					securityPermission.Assert();
				}
				NativeWindowMethods.RedrawWindowApi(base.Handle, IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero, 1089u);
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
		}

		void IMdiClientWindow.VerifyBorderStyle()
		{
			UpdateBorderStyle();
		}

		void IMdiClientWindow.Unsubclass()
		{
			Unhook();
		}

		bool IMdiClientWindow.Activate(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, MdiClient mdiClient)
		{
			if (mdiClient == null || manager == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (this.manager == manager && mdiClient.Handle == base.Handle)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (base.Handle != mdiClient.Handle)
			{
				return false;
			}
			this.manager = manager;
			UpdateBorderStyle();
			return true;
		}
	}
	internal interface IMdiClientWindow
	{
		bool IsActive { get; }

		IntPtr Handle { get; }

		void Unsubclass();

		void VerifyBorderStyle();

		void BorderColorChanged();

		bool Activate(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, MdiClient mdiClient);
	}
	[SuppressUnmanagedCodeSecurity]
	[SecurityCritical]
	internal class MdiParentSubclasser : NativeWindow, IMdiParentWindow
	{
		private const int WM_NEXTMENU = 531;

		private UltraTabbedMdiManager manager;

		private bool IsManagerEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				if (manager != null)
				{
					return ((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)manager).Enabled;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		bool IMdiParentWindow.IsActive => manager != null;

		IntPtr IMdiParentWindow.Handle => base.Handle;

		internal MdiParentSubclasser(UltraTabbedMdiManager mdiManager)
		{
			manager = mdiManager;
		}

		protected override void WndProc(ref Message m)
		{
			UltraTabbedMdiManager ultraTabbedMdiManager = manager;
			if (ultraTabbedMdiManager == null)
			{
				base.WndProc(ref m);
				return;
			}
			int msg = m.Msg;
			if (msg == 531 && ((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)ultraTabbedMdiManager).Enabled && ultraTabbedMdiManager.MdiParent != null && ultraTabbedMdiManager.MdiParent.ActiveMdiChild != null)
			{
				Form activeMdiChild = ultraTabbedMdiManager.MdiParent.ActiveMdiChild;
				if (activeMdiChild != null && activeMdiChild.IsHandleCreated)
				{
					Message message = m;
					base.WndProc(ref m);
					IntPtr intPtr = activeMdiChild.Handle;
					NativeWindowMethods.MDINEXTMENU mDINEXTMENU = (NativeWindowMethods.MDINEXTMENU)Marshal.PtrToStructure(m.LParam, typeof(NativeWindowMethods.MDINEXTMENU));
					if (mDINEXTMENU.hwndNext == intPtr && mDINEXTMENU.hmenuNext != IntPtr.Zero && NativeWindowMethods.GetSystemMenu(intPtr, revert: false) == mDINEXTMENU.hmenuNext)
					{
						mDINEXTMENU.hmenuIn = mDINEXTMENU.hmenuNext;
						mDINEXTMENU.hwndNext = IntPtr.Zero;
						Marshal.StructureToPtr((object)mDINEXTMENU, m.LParam, fDeleteOld: true);
						base.WndProc(ref m);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			base.WndProc(ref m);
		}

		private void Unhook()
		{
			manager = null;
		}

		void IMdiParentWindow.Unsubclass()
		{
			Unhook();
		}

		bool IMdiParentWindow.Activate(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, Form mdiParent)
		{
			if (mdiParent == null || manager == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (this.manager == manager && mdiParent.Handle == base.Handle)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (base.Handle != mdiParent.Handle)
			{
				return false;
			}
			this.manager = manager;
			return true;
		}
	}
	internal interface IMdiParentWindow
	{
		bool IsActive { get; }

		IntPtr Handle { get; }

		void Unsubclass();

		bool Activate(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, Form mdiParent);
	}
	public class TabbedMdiEventManager : EventManagerBase
	{
		private UltraTabbedMdiManager manager;

		private static int[] beforeEventIndexes;

		private static int[] afterEventIndexes;

		private int[] AfterEventIndexes
		{
			get
			{
				if (afterEventIndexes == null)
				{
					afterEventIndexes = new int[8] { 7, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20, 22 };
				}
				return afterEventIndexes;
			}
		}

		private int[] BeforeEventIndexes
		{
			get
			{
				if (beforeEventIndexes == null)
				{
					beforeEventIndexes = new int[8] { 6, 8, 11, 13, 15, 17, 19, 21 };
				}
				return beforeEventIndexes;
			}
		}

		public TabbedMdiEventManager(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager)
			: base(Enum.GetValues(typeof(TabbedMdiEventIds)).Length)
		{
			if (manager == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_3"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_4"));
			}
			this.manager = manager;
		}

		public bool InProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds eventid)
		{
			return InProgress((int)eventid);
		}

		public bool IsEnabled(TabbedMdiEventIds eventid)
		{
			return IsEnabled((int)eventid);
		}

		public void SetEnabled(TabbedMdiEventIds eventid, bool enabled)
		{
			SetEnabled((int)eventid, enabled);
		}

		public bool IsEnabled(TabbedMdiEventGroups group)
		{
			return group switch
			{
				TabbedMdiEventGroups.AllEvents => base.AllEventsEnabled, 
				TabbedMdiEventGroups.BeforeEvents => IsEnabled(BeforeEventIndexes), 
				TabbedMdiEventGroups.AfterEvents => IsEnabled(AfterEventIndexes), 
				_ => false, 
			};
		}

		public void SetEnabled(TabbedMdiEventGroups group, bool enabled)
		{
			switch (group)
			{
			case TabbedMdiEventGroups.AllEvents:
				base.AllEventsEnabled = enabled;
				break;
			case TabbedMdiEventGroups.BeforeEvents:
				SetEnabled(BeforeEventIndexes, enabled);
				break;
			case TabbedMdiEventGroups.AfterEvents:
				SetEnabled(AfterEventIndexes, enabled);
				break;
			}
		}

		internal void IncrementInProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds eventid)
		{
			IncrementInProgress((int)eventid);
		}

		internal void DecrementInProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds eventid)
		{
			DecrementInProgress((int)eventid);
		}

		internal bool CanFireEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds eventid)
		{
			return IsEnabled(eventid);
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableObjectConverter))]
	public class MdiTab : KeyedSubObjectBase, ISerializable, IDeserializationCallback, ITabItem
	{
		protected internal enum AppearanceResolutionPhase
		{
			Active,
			Selected,
			HotTrack,
			Tab,
			Final
		}

		private class TabClosedHelper
		{
			private MdiTab tab;

			private UltraTabbedMdiManager manager;

			private Form form;

			internal TabClosedHelper(MdiTab tab, UltraTabbedMdiManager manager, Form form)
			{
				this.tab = tab;
				this.manager = manager;
				this.form = form;
				this.form.Disposed += OnMdiChildDisposed;
			}

			private void OnMdiChildDisposed(object sender, EventArgs e)
			{
				form.Disposed -= OnMdiChildDisposed;
				manager.InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabClosed, new MdiTabClosedEventArgs(tab, MdiTabCloseReason.FormClosed));
			}
		}

		private MdiTabSettings settings;

		private MdiTabSettingsResolved settingsResolved;

		private Form form;

		private object persistedInfo;

		private string text;

		private string tooltip;

		private object tabItemTag;

		private object owner;

		private MdiChildSettings formSettings;

		private Icon icon;

		private Image iconImage;

		private Stream iconStream;

		private int cachedIndex = -1;

		private bool isClosingTab;

		private AccessibleObject accessibilityObject;

		private MdiTabCloseReason currentCloseReason = MdiTabCloseReason.CloseMethod;

		private int mdiChildIndex = -1;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public MdiTabGroup TabGroup => owner as MdiTabGroup;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public MdiTabSettings Settings
		{
			get
			{
				if (settings == null)
				{
					settings = new MdiTabSettings(this);
					settings.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				}
				return settings;
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public bool HasSettings => settings != null;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public MdiTabSettingsResolved SettingsResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (settingsResolved == null)
				{
					settingsResolved = new MdiTabSettingsResolved(this);
				}
				return settingsResolved;
			}
		}

		[ReadOnly(true)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public Form Form => form;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public UltraTabbedMdiManager Manager
		{
			get
			{
				if (owner is MdiTabGroup)
				{
					return ((MdiTabGroup)owner).Manager;
				}
				return owner as UltraTabbedMdiManager;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public bool IsFormActive
		{
			get
			{
				UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = Manager;
				if (manager != null)
				{
					return this == manager.ActiveTab;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public bool IsFormEnabled => Form?.Enabled ?? false;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public bool IsFormVisible
		{
			get
			{
				Form form = Form;
				if (form != null)
				{
					if (form.Visible)
					{
						return !form.IsDisposed;
					}
					return false;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Visible)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public object PersistedInfo
		{
			get
			{
				return persistedInfo;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != persistedInfo)
				{
					persistedInfo = value;
					NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.PersistedInfo);
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public bool IsSelected
		{
			get
			{
				MdiTabGroup tabGroup = TabGroup;
				if (tabGroup == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return tabGroup.SelectedTab == this;
			}
		}

		public string Text
		{
			get
			{
				return text;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(value == text))
				{
					text = value;
					TabGroup?.DirtyTabItem(this, invalidate: true, textChanged: true, imageChanged: false);
					NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Text);
				}
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public string TextResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (Text != null)
				{
					return Text;
				}
				string text = null;
				if (IsCustomTab)
				{
					text = CustomMdiTab.Text;
				}
				if (text != null)
				{
					return text;
				}
				if (Form != null)
				{
					return Form.Text;
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}
		}

		public string ToolTip
		{
			get
			{
				return tooltip;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(tooltip == value))
				{
					tooltip = value;
					NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ToolTip);
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public string ToolTipResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (ToolTip != null)
				{
					return ToolTip;
				}
				if (IsCustomTab)
				{
					return CustomMdiTab.ToolTip;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public UIElement UIElement => Manager?.GetUIElement(this);

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public bool IsCustomTab => CustomMdiTab != null;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public int Index
		{
			get
			{
				MdiTabsBaseCollection mdiTabsBaseCollection = null;
				if (TabGroup != null)
				{
					mdiTabsBaseCollection = TabGroup.Tabs;
				}
				else if (Manager != null)
				{
					mdiTabsBaseCollection = Manager.HiddenTabs;
				}
				if (mdiTabsBaseCollection == null)
				{
					cachedIndex = -1;
				}
				else if (cachedIndex < 0 || cachedIndex >= mdiTabsBaseCollection.Count || mdiTabsBaseCollection[cachedIndex] != this)
				{
					cachedIndex = mdiTabsBaseCollection.IndexOf(this);
				}
				return cachedIndex;
			}
		}

		internal Form MdiChild
		{
			get
			{
				return form;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != form)
				{
					UnhookForm();
					form = value;
					if (form == null)
					{
						formSettings = null;
					}
					HookForm();
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool IsFormInitialized
		{
			get
			{
				if (formSettings != null)
				{
					return formSettings.IsInitialized;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal ICustomMdiTab CustomMdiTab
		{
			get
			{
				Form obj = Form;
				return (ICustomMdiTab)(object)((obj is ICustomMdiTab) ? obj : null);
			}
		}

		internal bool CanActivate
		{
			get
			{
				if (form != null && form.Visible && !form.Disposing)
				{
					return !form.IsDisposed;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal int MdiChildIndex => mdiChildIndex;

		protected internal UIRole UIRole
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0045: Expected I4, but got Unknown
				StyleUtils.Role role;
				if (TabGroup == null)
				{
					role = StyleUtils.Role.TabItemHorzTop;
				}
				else
				{
					TabOrientation tabOrientation = TabGroup.SettingsResolved.TabOrientation;
					switch (tabOrientation - 1)
					{
					default:
						role = StyleUtils.Role.TabItemHorzTop;
						break;
					case 2:
					case 3:
						role = StyleUtils.Role.TabItemHorzBottom;
						break;
					case 4:
					case 5:
						role = StyleUtils.Role.TabItemVertLeft;
						break;
					case 6:
					case 7:
						role = StyleUtils.Role.TabItemVertRight;
						break;
					}
				}
				return StyleUtils.GetRole((IUltraControl)(object)Manager, role);
			}
		}

		private bool IsHotTrackTab
		{
			get
			{
				if (TabGroup != null)
				{
					return TabGroup.HotTrackTab == this;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool DoesFormHaveIcon
		{
			get
			{
				if (form != null)
				{
					return form.Icon != null;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal Image FormIcon
		{
			get
			{
				CreateFormIcon();
				return iconImage;
			}
		}

		private bool IsVertical
		{
			get
			{
				if (TabGroup != null)
				{
					return TabGroup.IsVertical;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		string ITabItem.ToolTipText => ToolTipResolved;

		bool ITabItem.Visible => IsFormVisible;

		object ITabItem.Tag
		{
			get
			{
				return tabItemTag;
			}
			set
			{
				tabItemTag = value;
			}
		}

		bool ITabItem.AllowMoving => SettingsResolved.AllowDrag != MdiTabDragStyle.None;

		bool ITabItem.Enabled => IsFormEnabled;

		Size ITabItem.ImageSize
		{
			get
			{
				UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = Manager;
				if (manager != null)
				{
					MdiTabGroup tabGroup = TabGroup;
					Size imageSize = manager.ImageSize;
					if (IsCustomTab && CustomMdiTab.HasTabImage)
					{
						return imageSize;
					}
					if (HasSettings && HasTabImage(Settings))
					{
						return imageSize;
					}
					if (tabGroup != null && tabGroup.HasTabSettings && HasTabImage(tabGroup.TabSettings))
					{
						return imageSize;
					}
					if (manager.HasTabSettings && HasTabImage(manager.TabSettings))
					{
						return imageSize;
					}
					if (SettingsResolved.DisplayFormIcon && DoesFormHaveIcon)
					{
						return imageSize;
					}
				}
				return Size.Empty;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public AccessibleObject AccessibilityObject
		{
			get
			{
				if (accessibilityObject == null)
				{
					MdiTabGroup tabGroup = TabGroup;
					if (tabGroup != null)
					{
						accessibilityObject = tabGroup.CreateTabAccessibilityInstance(this);
					}
				}
				return accessibilityObject;
			}
		}

		protected internal MdiTab(Form form)
		{
			MdiChild = form;
		}

		protected MdiTab(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			SerializationInfoEnumerator enumerator = info.GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				SerializationEntry current = enumerator.Current;
				switch (current.Name)
				{
				case "Key":
					Key = (string)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(string), Key);
					break;
				case "Settings":
					settings = (MdiTabSettings)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(MdiTabSettings), null);
					break;
				case "PersistedInfo":
					persistedInfo = Utils.DeserializeObjectProperty(current);
					break;
				case "ToolTip":
					tooltip = (string)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(string), tooltip);
					break;
				case "Text":
					text = (string)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(string), text);
					break;
				case "Tag":
					DeserializeTag(current);
					break;
				case "MdiChildIndex":
					mdiChildIndex = (int)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(int), -1);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		protected bool ShouldSerializeSettings()
		{
			if (settings != null)
			{
				return settings.ShouldSerialize();
			}
			return false;
		}

		public virtual void Activate()
		{
			if (Form != null && Manager != null)
			{
				bool isSelected = IsSelected;
				Manager.ActivateMdiChild(this, !isSelected, !isSelected, allowCancel: true, activate: true);
			}
		}

		public virtual void Close()
		{
			if (Form == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			isClosingTab = true;
			try
			{
				UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = Manager;
				if (manager != null)
				{
					MdiTabClosingEventArgs e = new MdiTabClosingEventArgs(this, currentCloseReason);
					manager.InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabClosing, e);
					if (e.Cancel)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				bool flag = false;
				if (IsCustomTab)
				{
					CustomMdiTab.Close();
				}
				else
				{
					switch (SettingsResolved.TabCloseAction)
					{
					default:
						flag = true;
						if (Form != null)
						{
							Form.Close();
						}
						break;
					case MdiTabCloseAction.Hide:
						if (Form != null)
						{
							Form.Hide();
						}
						break;
					case MdiTabCloseAction.None:
						break;
					}
				}
				if (manager != null && (Form == null || Form.IsDisposed || !flag))
				{
					MdiTabClosedEventArgs e2 = new MdiTabClosedEventArgs(this, currentCloseReason);
					manager.InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabClosed, e2);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				isClosingTab = false;
			}
		}

		public virtual void EnsureTabInView()
		{
			if (TabGroup != null)
			{
				TabGroup.TabManager.EnsureTabItemInView((ITabItem)(object)this);
			}
		}

		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			string textResolved = TextResolved;
			bool flag = Key != null && Key.Length > 0;
			bool flag2 = textResolved != null && textResolved.Length > 0;
			if (flag && flag2)
			{
				return string.Format(null, "MdiTab Key='{0}', Text='{1}'", new object[2] { Key, TextResolved });
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				return string.Format(null, "MdiTab Key='{0}'", new object[1] { Key });
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				return string.Format(null, "MdiTab Text='{0}'", new object[1] { TextResolved });
			}
			return "MdiTab";
		}

		protected void ResolveAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps, TabState tabState)

			//IL_012c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

		protected static void ResolveAppearancePhase(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps, AppearanceResolutionPhase phase, MdiTabSettings settings)
		{
			switch (phase)
			{
			case AppearanceResolutionPhase.Active:
				if (settings != null && settings.HasActiveTabAppearance)
				{
					settings.ActiveTabAppearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
				break;
			case AppearanceResolutionPhase.HotTrack:
				if (settings != null && settings.HasHotTrackTabAppearance)
				{
					settings.HotTrackTabAppearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
				break;
			case AppearanceResolutionPhase.Selected:
				if (settings != null && settings.HasSelectedTabAppearance)
				{
					settings.SelectedTabAppearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
				break;
			case AppearanceResolutionPhase.Tab:
				if (settings != null && settings.HasTabAppearance)
				{
					settings.TabAppearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
				break;
			case AppearanceResolutionPhase.Final:
			{
				UltraTabbedMdiManager ultraTabbedMdiManager = settings?.Manager;
				if (ultraTabbedMdiManager != null && ultraTabbedMdiManager.HasAppearance)
				{
					ultraTabbedMdiManager.Appearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			if (HasSettings && propChangeInfo.Source == settings)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Settings, propChangeInfo);
			}
			base.OnSubObjectPropChanged(propChangeInfo);
		}

		protected override void OnDispose()
		{
			DisposeIconImage();
			if (settings != null)
			{
				settings.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				settings.Dispose();
				settings = null;
			}
			base.OnDispose();
		}

		private void HookForm()
		{
			if (form != null)
			{
				form.EnabledChanged += OnMdiChildEnabledChanged;
				form.VisibleChanged += OnMdiChildVisibleChanged;
				form.TextChanged += OnMdiChildTextChanged;
				form.LocationChanged += OnMdiChildLocationChanged;
				form.SizeChanged += OnMdiChildSizeChanged;
				form.Closing += OnMdiChildClosing;
				form.Closed += OnMdiChildClosed;
				form.Enter += OnMdiChildEnter;
			}
		}

		private void UnhookForm()
		{
			if (form != null)
			{
				form.EnabledChanged -= OnMdiChildEnabledChanged;
				form.VisibleChanged -= OnMdiChildVisibleChanged;
				form.TextChanged -= OnMdiChildTextChanged;
				form.LocationChanged -= OnMdiChildLocationChanged;
				form.SizeChanged -= OnMdiChildSizeChanged;
				form.Closing -= OnMdiChildClosing;
				form.Closed -= OnMdiChildClosed;
				form.Enter -= OnMdiChildEnter;
				RestoreTabFormSettings();
				DisposeIconImage();
			}
		}

		private void OnMdiChildEnabledChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			TabGroup?.DirtyTabItem(this, invalidate: true, textChanged: false, imageChanged: false);
		}

		private void OnMdiChildVisibleChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			Manager?.MdiChildVisibilityChanged(this);
		}

		private void OnMdiChildTextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			TabGroup?.DirtyTabItem(this, invalidate: true, textChanged: true, imageChanged: false);
		}

		private void OnMdiChildLocationChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			Manager?.ResetFormPosition(this);
		}

		private void OnMdiChildSizeChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			Manager?.ResetFormPosition(this);
		}

		private void OnMdiChildClosing(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
		{
			if (isClosingTab)
			{
				return;
			}
			UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = Manager;
			if (manager != null)
			{
				MdiTabClosingEventArgs e2 = new MdiTabClosingEventArgs(this, MdiTabCloseReason.FormClosed);
				manager.InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabClosing, e2);
				if (e2.Cancel)
				{
					e.Cancel = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnMdiChildClosed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (isClosingTab)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Form != null && Form.IsDisposed)
			{
				UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = Manager;
				if (manager != null)
				{
					MdiTabClosedEventArgs e2 = new MdiTabClosedEventArgs(this, MdiTabCloseReason.FormClosed);
					manager.InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabClosed, e2);
				}
			}
			else if (Manager != null)
			{
				new TabClosedHelper(this, Manager, sender as Form);
			}
		}

		private void OnMdiChildEnter(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			Form form = sender as Form;
			if (CanActivate && form.ContainsFocus && form.IsMdiChild && Manager != null)
			{
				Form mdiParent = Utilities.GetMdiParent(form);
				if (mdiParent.ActiveMdiChild != null && form != mdiParent.ActiveMdiChild)
				{
					Manager.ActivateMdiChild(this, invokeTabSelecting: true, invokeTabSelected: true, allowCancel: false, activate: true);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void ResolveAppearanceHelper(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps, AppearanceResolutionPhase phase, MdiTabSettings tabSettings, MdiTabSettings groupSettings, MdiTabSettings managerSettings, MdiTabGroup tabGroup, TabState tabState, UIRole role, ResolutionOrderInfo order)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0078: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0069: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			RoleState roleState = StyleUtils.GetRoleState(phase);
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref order)).UseControlInfo && tabSettings != null)
			{
				ResolveAppearancePhase(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, phase, tabSettings);
			}
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref order)).UseStyleBefore && role != null)
			{
				role.ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, roleState);
			}
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref order)).UseControlInfo)
			{
				if (groupSettings != null)
				{
					ResolveAppearancePhase(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, phase, groupSettings);
				}
				if (managerSettings != null)
				{
					ResolveAppearancePhase(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, phase, managerSettings);
				}
			}
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref order)).UseStyleAfter && role != null)
			{
				role.ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, roleState);
			}
			tabGroup?.ResolveDefaultTabPhaseAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, phase, tabState);
		}

		private bool HasTabImage(MdiTabSettings tabSettings)
		{
			//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0046: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0076: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_008e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (tabSettings == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ResolutionOrderInfo resolutionOrderInfo = StyleUtils.GetResolutionOrderInfo(Manager);
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrderInfo)).UseControlInfo)
			{
				if (IsCustomTab && CustomMdiTab.HasTabImage)
				{
					return true;
				}
				AppearancePropFlags val = (AppearancePropFlags)256;
				if (tabSettings.HasActiveTabAppearance && tabSettings.ActiveTabAppearance.HasPropertyBeenSet(val))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (tabSettings.HasSelectedTabAppearance && tabSettings.SelectedTabAppearance.HasPropertyBeenSet(val))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (tabSettings.HasHotTrackTabAppearance && tabSettings.HotTrackTabAppearance.HasPropertyBeenSet(val))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (tabSettings.HasTabAppearance && tabSettings.TabAppearance.HasPropertyBeenSet(val))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrderInfo)).UseStyleBefore || ((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrderInfo)).UseStyleAfter)
			{
				UIRole uIRole = UIRole;
				if (uIRole != null && (uIRole.HasAppearancePropertyBeenSet((RoleState)1, (AppearancePropFlags)256) || uIRole.HasAppearancePropertyBeenSet((RoleState)16, (AppearancePropFlags)256) || uIRole.HasAppearancePropertyBeenSet((RoleState)4, (AppearancePropFlags)256) || uIRole.HasAppearancePropertyBeenSet((RoleState)2, (AppearancePropFlags)256)))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal void DisposeIconImage()
		{
			icon = null;
			if (iconImage != null)
			{
				iconImage.Dispose();
				iconImage = null;
			}
			if (iconStream != null)
			{
				iconStream.Close();
				iconStream = null;
			}
		}

		private void CreateFormIcon()
		{
			Form form = this.form;
			if (form == null || form.Icon == null || form.Disposing || form.IsDisposed)
			{
				DisposeIconImage();
			}
			else
			{
				if (form.Icon == this.icon)
				{
					return;
				}
				DisposeIconImage();
				this.icon = form.Icon;
				Icon icon = form.Icon;
				Size size = ((Manager != null) ? Manager.ImageSize : UltraTabbedMdiManager.ImageSizeDefault);
				try
				{
					if (size.Width > 0 && size.Height > 0)
					{
						icon = new Icon(icon, size);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				try
				{
					iconImage = icon.ToBitmap();
				}
				finally
				{
					if (icon != form.Icon)
					{
						icon.Dispose();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private static int GetMdiChildIndex(Form mdiChild)
		{
			if (mdiChild != null && mdiChild.IsMdiChild)
			{
				Form[] mdiChildren = mdiChild.MdiParent.MdiChildren;
				int i = 0;
				for (int num = mdiChildren.Length; i < num; i++)
				{
					Form form = mdiChildren[i];
					if (form != null && form == mdiChild)
					{
						return i;
					}
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		internal void Close(MdiTabCloseReason closeReason)
		{
			MdiTabCloseReason mdiTabCloseReason = currentCloseReason;
			try
			{
				currentCloseReason = closeReason;
				Close();
			}
			finally
			{
				currentCloseReason = mdiTabCloseReason;
			}
		}

		internal MdiTab GetNextPreviousTab(bool wrap, bool next)
		{
			MdiTab result = null;
			MdiTabGroup tabGroup = TabGroup;
			if (tabGroup != null)
			{
				int num = Index + (next ? 1 : (-1));
				if (num >= 0 && num < tabGroup.Tabs.Count)
				{
					result = tabGroup.Tabs[num];
				}
				else if (next)
				{
					tabGroup = tabGroup.NextLeafTabGroup;
					if (tabGroup == null && wrap && Manager != null)
					{
						tabGroup = UltraTabbedMdiManager.GetFirstLeafTabGroup(Manager.TabGroups);
					}
					if (tabGroup != null)
					{
						result = tabGroup.Tabs[0];
					}
				}
				else
				{
					tabGroup = tabGroup.PreviousLeafTabGroup;
					if (tabGroup == null && wrap && Manager != null)
					{
						tabGroup = UltraTabbedMdiManager.GetLastLeafTabGroup(Manager.TabGroups);
					}
					if (tabGroup != null)
					{
						result = tabGroup.Tabs[tabGroup.Tabs.Count - 1];
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal static AppearanceData ResolveTabFontAppearance(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, TabState tabState, MdiTabSettings groupSettings, MdiTabSettings managerSettings)
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0038: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0030: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0032: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0050: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0046: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0048: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0092: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0094: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00bb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			AppearanceData appearance = default(AppearanceData);
			AppearancePropFlags requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)65011720;
			UIRole uIRoleTabItemDefault = tabGroup.UIRoleTabItemDefault;
			ResolutionOrderInfo resolutionOrderInfo = StyleUtils.GetResolutionOrderInfo(tabGroup.Manager);
			if ((tabState & 4) != 0)
			{
				ResolveAppearanceHelper(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, AppearanceResolutionPhase.Active, null, groupSettings, managerSettings, tabGroup, tabState, uIRoleTabItemDefault, resolutionOrderInfo);
			}
			if ((tabState & 2) != 0)
			{
				ResolveAppearanceHelper(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, AppearanceResolutionPhase.HotTrack, null, groupSettings, managerSettings, tabGroup, tabState, uIRoleTabItemDefault, resolutionOrderInfo);
			}
			if ((tabState & 1) != 0)
			{
				ResolveAppearanceHelper(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, AppearanceResolutionPhase.Selected, null, groupSettings, managerSettings, tabGroup, tabState, uIRoleTabItemDefault, resolutionOrderInfo);
			}
			ResolveAppearanceHelper(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, AppearanceResolutionPhase.Tab, null, groupSettings, managerSettings, tabGroup, tabState, uIRoleTabItemDefault, resolutionOrderInfo);
			bool useStyleAfter = (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrderInfo)).UseStyleBefore = false);
			((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrderInfo)).UseStyleAfter = useStyleAfter;
			ResolveAppearanceHelper(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, AppearanceResolutionPhase.Final, null, groupSettings, managerSettings, tabGroup, tabState, uIRoleTabItemDefault, resolutionOrderInfo);
			UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = tabGroup.Manager;
			if (manager.HasAppearance)
			{
				manager.Appearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
			return appearance;
		}

		internal void InitializeOwner(object owner)
		{
			if (!(owner is MdiTabGroup))
			{
				accessibilityObject = null;
			}
			this.owner = owner;
		}

		internal void InitializeTabFormSettings()
		{
			if (IsFormInitialized)
			{
				return;
			}
			Form mdiChild = MdiChild;
			if (mdiChild != null)
			{
				if (formSettings == null)
				{
					formSettings = new MdiChildSettings(mdiChild, Manager);
				}
				formSettings.Initialize();
			}
		}

		internal void RestoreTabFormSettings()
		{
			if (IsFormInitialized && (Manager == null || !Manager.IsDisposing))
			{
				formSettings.Restore();
			}
		}

		internal bool IsDescendantOf(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			for (MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = TabGroup; mdiTabGroup != null; mdiTabGroup = mdiTabGroup.Parent)
			{
				if (mdiTabGroup == tabGroup)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal Image GetResolvedImage()
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			AppearanceData appearance = default(AppearanceData);
			AppearancePropFlags requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)256;
			ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (TabState)0);
			return ((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).GetImage(Manager.ImageList);
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.SerializationFormatter)]
		void ISerializable.GetObjectData(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			if (ShouldSerializeKey())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "Key", Key);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeSettings())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "Settings", Settings);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializePersistedInfo())
			{
				Utils.SerializeObjectProperty(info, "PersistedInfo", PersistedInfo);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeToolTip())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "ToolTip", ToolTip);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeText())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "Text", Text);
			}
			int num = GetMdiChildIndex(form);
			Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "MdiChildIndex", num);
			if (ShouldSerializeTag())
			{
				SerializeTag(info);
			}
			GetObjectData(info, context);
		}

		void IDeserializationCallback.OnDeserialization(object sender)
		{
			if (settings != null)
			{
				settings.InitializeOwner(this);
				settings.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			}
			OnDeserialization(sender);
		}

		void ITabItem.ResolveAppearance(TabState state, ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			//IL_0003: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, state);
		}
	}
