					{
						Type typeFromHandle = typeof(MdiTabGroup);
						noDragCursorStream = typeFromHandle.Assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(typeFromHandle, "CursorNoDragMono.cur");
						noDragCursor = new Cursor(noDragCursorStream);
					}
					return noDragCursor;
				}
			}

			public override bool IsDragging
			{
				get
				{
					if (!((TabDragManager)this).IsDragging)
					{
						return isProcessingDrop;
					}
					return true;
				}
			}

			protected virtual bool AllowScroll => true;

			protected virtual int ScrollDistanceMinimum => -20;

			protected virtual int ScrollDistanceMaximum => 0;

			private MdiTabDragStyle TabDragStyle
			{
				get
				{
					if (!(((TabDragManager)this).Tab is MdiTab { Manager: not null } mdiTab))
					{
						return MdiTabDragStyle.None;
					}
					MdiTabDragStyle allowDrag = mdiTab.SettingsResolved.AllowDrag;
					if (allowDrag == MdiTabDragStyle.WithinAndAcrossGroups && mdiTab.Manager.IsActiveTabGroupMaximized)
					{
						return MdiTabDragStyle.WithinGroup;
					}
					return allowDrag;
				}
			}

			internal bool UseReversibleFrame
			{
				get
				{
					if (!canUseReversibleFrame.HasValue)
					{
						canUseReversibleFrame = !Utilities.IsWin10OrLater;
					}
					return canUseReversibleFrame.Value;
				}
			}

			private OutlineForm DragForm
			{
				get
				{
					if (dragForm == null || dragForm.Disposing)
					{
						dragForm = new OutlineForm();
						dragForm.StartPosition = FormStartPosition.Manual;
						dragForm.FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.None;
						dragForm.ShowInTaskbar = false;
						InitializeDragForm();
						dragForm.ForceHandleCreation();
					}
					return dragForm;
				}
			}

			public MdiTabDragManager(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, TabManager tabManager)
				: base(tabManager)
			{
				if (tabGroup == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_13"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_14"));
				}
				owningTabGroup = tabGroup;
			}

			protected virtual int GetScrollInterval(int distance)
			{
				return 500;
			}

			protected override bool InitiateDrag(Point tabOffset, ITabItem tabToDrag, UIElement tabElement)
			{
				UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = TabGroup.Manager;
				if (manager == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (TabGroup.Tabs.Count == 1 && manager.TabGroups.Count == 1)
				{
					return false;
				}
				CancelableMdiTabEventArgs e = new CancelableMdiTabEventArgs(tabToDrag as MdiTab);
				manager.InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabDragging, e);
				if (e.Cancel)
				{
					return false;
				}
				HookMessageFilter();
				originalSrcTabGroup = ((MdiTab)(object)tabToDrag).TabGroup;
				originalTabIndex = tabToDrag.Index;
				originalFirstDisplayedTab = TabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab;
				lastScreenPt = new Point(int.MinValue, int.MinValue);
				manager.OnDragTabStart();
				return true;
			}

			public override void ProcessMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
			{
				//IL_0051: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_02a0: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_02a5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_02aa: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = TabGroup.Manager;
				if (manager == null)
				{
					TabGroup.TabManager.CancelDrag();
					return;
				}
				Point point = new Point(e.X, e.Y);
				Point point2 = ((TabDragManager)this).DragSourceControl.PointToScreen(point);
				if (point2 == lastScreenPt)
				{
					return;
				}
				scrollDirection = (DragScrollDirection)0;
				lastScreenPt = point2;
				Rectangle mdiClientScreenRect = manager.MdiClientScreenRect;
				if (!mdiClientScreenRect.Contains(point2))
				{
					if (((TabDragManager)this).Tab is MdiTab && TabDragStyle == MdiTabDragStyle.WithinAndAcrossGroups)
					{
						int num = mdiClientScreenRect.Top - point2.Y;
						int num2 = point2.Y - mdiClientScreenRect.Bottom;
						int num3 = mdiClientScreenRect.Left - point2.X;
						int num4 = point2.X - mdiClientScreenRect.Right;
						if (num < 0)
						{
							num = int.MaxValue;
						}
						if (num2 < 0)
						{
							num2 = int.MaxValue;
						}
						if (num3 < 0)
						{
							num3 = int.MaxValue;
						}
						if (num4 < 0)
						{
							num4 = int.MaxValue;
						}
						TabGroupDropArea tabGroupDropArea = TabGroupDropArea.Middle;
						if (manager.CanCreateRootVerticalGroup && Math.Min(num3, num4) < 24)
						{
							tabGroupDropArea = ((num3 >= num4) ? TabGroupDropArea.RightEdge : TabGroupDropArea.LeftEdge);
						}
						if (manager.CanCreateRootHorizontalGroup && Math.Min(num, num2) < 24 && (tabGroupDropArea == TabGroupDropArea.Middle || Math.Min(num, num2) < Math.Min(num3, num4)))
						{
							tabGroupDropArea = ((num < num2) ? TabGroupDropArea.TopEdge : TabGroupDropArea.BottomEdge);
						}
						if (tabGroupDropArea != TabGroupDropArea.Middle)
						{
							MdiTabDropAction mdiTabDropAction = ((tabGroupDropArea != TabGroupDropArea.LeftEdge && tabGroupDropArea != TabGroupDropArea.RightEdge) ? MdiTabDropAction.NewHorizontalGroup : MdiTabDropAction.NewVerticalGroup);
							if (InvokeDragOver(mdiTabDropAction, point2, null, null, tabGroupDropArea))
							{
								Rectangle screenRect = mdiClientScreenRect;
								if (mdiTabDropAction == MdiTabDropAction.NewVerticalGroup)
								{
									screenRect.Width = mdiClientScreenRect.Width / (manager.CalculateMaxTabGroupCols() + 1);
									if (tabGroupDropArea == TabGroupDropArea.RightEdge)
									{
										screenRect.X = mdiClientScreenRect.Right - screenRect.Width;
									}
								}
								else
								{
									screenRect.Height = mdiClientScreenRect.Height / (manager.CalculateMaxTabGroupRows() + 1);
									if (tabGroupDropArea == TabGroupDropArea.BottomEdge)
									{
										screenRect.Y = mdiClientScreenRect.Bottom - screenRect.Height;
									}
								}
								ShowDropRect(screenRect);
								return;
							}
						}
					}
					ProcessInvalidDropLocation();
					return;
				}
				MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = manager.TabGroupFromPoint(point2);
				if (mdiTabGroup == null)
				{
					ProcessInvalidDropLocation();
					return;
				}
				switch (GetTabGroupArea(mdiTabGroup, point2))
				{
				case TabGroupArea.None:
					ProcessContextMenuDrop(mdiTabGroup);
					break;
				case TabGroupArea.TabGroupControl:
				{
					MdiTab mdiTab2 = ((TabDragManager)this).Tab as MdiTab;
					if (mdiTabGroup != TabGroup && (TabDragStyle != MdiTabDragStyle.WithinAndAcrossGroups || !mdiTabGroup.SettingsResolved.AllowDrop))
					{
						ProcessContextMenuDrop(mdiTabGroup);
						break;
					}
					Point point3 = ((mdiTabGroup == TabGroup) ? point : manager.PointToClient(mdiTabGroup, point2));
					TabHitTestInfo hitInfo = mdiTabGroup.TabManager.HitTest(point3);
					ProcessTabRowAreaDrop(mdiTabGroup, hitInfo, point3);
					break;
				}
				default:
					ProcessTabGroupClientArea(mdiTabGroup);
					break;
				}
			}

			protected override void DragEnded()
			{
				((TabDragManager)this).DragEnded();
				ShowDropRect(Rectangle.Empty);
				if (dragCursor != null)
				{
					dragCursor.Dispose();
					dragCursor = null;
				}
				if (dragCursorStream != null)
				{
					dragCursorStream.Close();
					dragCursorStream = null;
				}
				if (noDragCursor != null)
				{
					noDragCursor.Dispose();
					noDragCursor = null;
				}
				if (noDragCursorStream != null)
				{
					noDragCursorStream.Close();
					noDragCursorStream = null;
				}
				originalIndexes = null;
				originalSrcTabGroup = null;
				originalTabIndex = 0;
				originalFirstDisplayedTab = null;
				dropAction = MdiTabDropAction.None;
				dropTabGroup = null;
				dropNewGroupArea = TabGroupDropArea.Middle;
				UnhookMessageFilter();
				if (TabGroup.Manager != null)
				{
					TabGroup.Manager.OnDragTabEnd();
				}
			}

			public override void ProcessDrop()
			{
				//IL_019e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0196: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_01a6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_018e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_01b3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_01b9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				isProcessingDrop = true;
				try
				{
					MdiTab mdiTab = ((TabDragManager)this).Tab as MdiTab;
					MdiTabDropAction mdiTabDropAction = dropAction;
					MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = dropTabGroup;
					int tabIndex = dropTabIndex;
					int num = dropTabIndex;
					Point screenPt = lastScreenPt;
					int index = ((ITabItem)originalSrcTabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab).Index;
					MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup2 = originalSrcTabGroup;
					bool flag = (object)originalSrcTabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab == ((TabDragManager)this).Tab;
					int num2 = originalTabIndex;
					TabGroupDropArea tabGroupDropArea = dropNewGroupArea;
					int index2 = ((TabDragManager)this).Tab.Index;
					RestoreTabPosition();
					int index3 = ((ITabItem)mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab).Index;
					bool flag2 = index3 != index;
					UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = TabGroup.Manager;
					((TabDragManager)this).DragEnded();
					if (manager != null)
					{
						switch (mdiTabDropAction)
						{
						case MdiTabDropAction.ContextMenu:
							if (((ITabItem)mdiTab).Index != index2)
							{
								manager.MoveToGroup(mdiTab, mdiTabGroup2, index2);
								if ((flag || flag2) && mdiTabGroup2.ParentCollection != null && mdiTabGroup2.Tabs.Count > index && mdiTabGroup2.Tabs.Contains(mdiTab) && mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab != null && ((ITabItem)mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab).Index != index)
								{
									mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab = mdiTabGroup2.Tabs[index];
								}
								if (mdiTab.CanActivate)
								{
									mdiTab.Activate();
								}
							}
							manager.ShowDragDropContextMenu(mdiTab, screenPt);
							break;
						case MdiTabDropAction.NewHorizontalGroup:
						case MdiTabDropAction.NewVerticalGroup:
						{
							Orientation orientation;
							RelativePosition relativePosition;
							switch (tabGroupDropArea)
							{
							case TabGroupDropArea.BottomEdge:
								orientation = Orientation.Horizontal;
								relativePosition = (RelativePosition)1;
								break;
							case TabGroupDropArea.TopEdge:
								orientation = Orientation.Horizontal;
								relativePosition = (RelativePosition)0;
								break;
							case TabGroupDropArea.LeftEdge:
								orientation = Orientation.Vertical;
								relativePosition = (RelativePosition)0;
								break;
							case TabGroupDropArea.RightEdge:
								orientation = Orientation.Vertical;
								relativePosition = (RelativePosition)1;
								break;
							default:
								orientation = mdiTabGroup.ParentOrientation;
								relativePosition = (RelativePosition)1;
								break;
							}
							manager.MoveToNewGroup(mdiTab, mdiTabGroup, relativePosition, orientation);
							if ((flag || flag2) && mdiTabGroup2.ParentCollection != null && mdiTabGroup2.Tabs.Count > index && mdiTabGroup2.Tabs.Contains(mdiTab) && mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab != null && ((ITabItem)mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab).Index != index)
							{
								mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab = mdiTabGroup2.Tabs[index];
							}
							if (mdiTab.CanActivate)
							{
								mdiTab.Activate();
							}
							break;
						}
						case MdiTabDropAction.None:
							if (num2 != index2)
							{
								manager.MoveToGroup(mdiTab, mdiTabGroup2, index2);
								if ((flag || flag2) && mdiTabGroup2.ParentCollection != null && mdiTabGroup2.Tabs.Count > index && mdiTabGroup2.Tabs.Contains(mdiTab) && mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab != null && ((ITabItem)mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab).Index != index)
								{
									mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab = mdiTabGroup2.Tabs[index];
								}
								if (mdiTab.CanActivate)
								{
									mdiTab.Activate();
								}
							}
							break;
						case MdiTabDropAction.TabGroup:
							if (num != num2 || mdiTabGroup != mdiTabGroup2)
							{
								manager.MoveToGroup(mdiTab, mdiTabGroup, tabIndex);
							}
							if ((flag || flag2) && mdiTabGroup2.ParentCollection != null && mdiTabGroup2.Tabs.Count > index && mdiTabGroup2.Tabs.Contains(mdiTab) && mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab != null && ((ITabItem)mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab).Index != index)
							{
								mdiTabGroup2.FirstDisplayedTab = mdiTabGroup2.Tabs[index];
							}
							if (mdiTab.CanActivate)
							{
								mdiTab.Activate();
							}
							break;
						}
					}
					if (manager != null)
					{
						MdiTabDroppedEventArgs e = new MdiTabDroppedEventArgs(mdiTab);
						manager.InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabDropped, e);
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					isProcessingDrop = false;
				}
			}

			public override void CancelDrag()
			{
				if (!isProcessingDrop)
				{
					if (((TabDragManager)this).IsDragging)
					{
						RestoreTabPosition();
					}
					((TabDragManager)this).CancelDrag();
				}
			}

			protected override void OnScrollTimerTick()
			{
				//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0019: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001f: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				//IL_0025: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0031: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				if (((TabDragManager)this).IsDragging && dropAction == MdiTabDropAction.TabGroup && (int)scrollDirection != 0)
				{
					ScrollType val = (ScrollType)((int)scrollDirection != 2);
					dropTabGroup.TabManager.ScrollTabs(val, 1);
				}
			}

			private void ProcessTabGroupClientArea(MdiTabGroup destTabGroup)
			{
				UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = TabGroup.Manager;
				if (manager == null || (!manager.CanCreateNewGroup && TabGroup.Tabs.Count > 1))
				{
					ProcessContextMenuDrop(destTabGroup);
					return;
				}
				bool flag = manager.CanCreateVerticalGroup;
				bool flag2 = manager.CanCreateHorizontalGroup;
				Rectangle tabGroupDropRect = manager.TabGroupManager.GetTabGroupDropRect(destTabGroup);
				Point point = manager.MdiParent.PointToClient(lastScreenPt);
				if (destTabGroup == originalSrcTabGroup && destTabGroup.Tabs.Count == 1)
				{
					flag = false;
					flag2 = false;
				}
				else if (((TabDragManager)this).Tab is MdiTab && TabDragStyle != MdiTabDragStyle.WithinAndAcrossGroups)
				{
					flag = false;
					flag2 = false;
				}
				int num = point.Y - tabGroupDropRect.Top;
				int num2 = tabGroupDropRect.Bottom - point.Y;
				int num3 = point.X - tabGroupDropRect.Left;
				int num4 = tabGroupDropRect.Right - point.X;
				TabGroupDropArea tabGroupDropArea = TabGroupDropArea.Middle;
				if (flag && Math.Min(num3, num4) < 24)
				{
					tabGroupDropArea = ((num3 >= num4) ? TabGroupDropArea.RightEdge : TabGroupDropArea.LeftEdge);
				}
				if (flag2 && Math.Min(num, num2) < 24 && (tabGroupDropArea == TabGroupDropArea.Middle || Math.Min(num, num2) < Math.Min(num3, num4)))
				{
					tabGroupDropArea = ((num < num2) ? TabGroupDropArea.TopEdge : TabGroupDropArea.BottomEdge);
				}
				switch (tabGroupDropArea)
				{
				case TabGroupDropArea.LeftEdge:
				case TabGroupDropArea.RightEdge:
				{
					if (!InvokeDragOver(MdiTabDropAction.NewVerticalGroup, lastScreenPt, destTabGroup, null, tabGroupDropArea))
					{
						ShowDropRect(Rectangle.Empty);
						break;
					}
					Rectangle rectangle2;
					if (destTabGroup.ParentOrientation == Orientation.Vertical)
					{
						rectangle2 = manager.TabGroupManager.GetTabGroupArea(destTabGroup);
					}
					else
					{
						rectangle2 = manager.TabGroupManager.GetTabGroupFormRect(destTabGroup);
						Rectangle tabGroupControlRectangle2 = manager.TabGroupManager.GetTabGroupControlRectangle(destTabGroup);
						if (!tabGroupControlRectangle2.IsEmpty)
						{
							rectangle2 = Rectangle.Union(rectangle2, tabGroupControlRectangle2);
						}
					}
					Rectangle screenRect2 = manager.MdiParent.RectangleToScreen(rectangle2);
					if (tabGroupDropArea == TabGroupDropArea.RightEdge)
					{
						screenRect2.X += screenRect2.Width / 2;
					}
					screenRect2.Width -= screenRect2.Width / 2;
					ShowDropRect(screenRect2);
					break;
				}
				case TabGroupDropArea.TopEdge:
				case TabGroupDropArea.BottomEdge:
				{
					if (!InvokeDragOver(MdiTabDropAction.NewHorizontalGroup, lastScreenPt, destTabGroup, null, tabGroupDropArea))
					{
						ShowDropRect(Rectangle.Empty);
						break;
					}
					Rectangle rectangle;
					if (destTabGroup.ParentOrientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
					{
						rectangle = manager.TabGroupManager.GetTabGroupArea(destTabGroup);
					}
					else
					{
						rectangle = manager.TabGroupManager.GetTabGroupFormRect(destTabGroup);
						Rectangle tabGroupControlRectangle = manager.TabGroupManager.GetTabGroupControlRectangle(destTabGroup);
						if (!tabGroupControlRectangle.IsEmpty)
						{
							rectangle = Rectangle.Union(rectangle, tabGroupControlRectangle);
						}
					}
					Rectangle screenRect = manager.MdiParent.RectangleToScreen(rectangle);
					if (tabGroupDropArea == TabGroupDropArea.BottomEdge)
					{
						screenRect.Y += screenRect.Height / 2;
					}
					screenRect.Height -= screenRect.Height / 2;
					ShowDropRect(screenRect);
					break;
				}
				default:
					ProcessContextMenuDrop(destTabGroup);
					break;
				}
			}

			private void ProcessInvalidDropLocation()
			{
				ShowDropRect(Rectangle.Empty);
				InvokeDragOver(MdiTabDropAction.None, lastScreenPt, null, null);
			}

			private void ProcessContextMenuDrop(MdiTabGroup containingTabGroup)
			{
				ShowDropRect(Rectangle.Empty);
				InvokeDragOver(MdiTabDropAction.ContextMenu, lastScreenPt, containingTabGroup, null);
			}

								}
								else
								{
									rect.X -= tabItemSize.Width;
								}
								break;
							default:
								if (interTabSpacingResolved > 0)
								{
									rect.X += tabItemSize.Width + interTabSpacingResolved;
								}
								else
								{
									rect.X += tabItemSize.Width;
								}
								break;
							}
							if (rect.Contains(tabGroupPt))
							{
								num3 = index + 1;
							}
						}
					}
					else if (destTabGroup.TabManager.GetPreviousVisibleTabItem((ITabItem)(object)mdiTab2, false) is MdiTab mdiTab4)
					{
						Size tabItemSize2 = destTabGroup.TabManager.GetTabItemSize((ITabItem)(object)mdiTab4);
						int interTabSpacingResolved2 = destTabGroup.TabManager.InterTabSpacingResolved;
						TabOrientation tabOrientation = destTabGroup.SettingsResolved.TabOrientation;
						switch (tabOrientation - 1)
						{
						case 5:
						case 7:
							if (interTabSpacingResolved2 > 0)
							{
								rect.Y += tabItemSize2.Height + interTabSpacingResolved2;
							}
							else
							{
								rect.Y += tabItemSize2.Height;
							}
							break;
						case 4:
						case 6:
							if (interTabSpacingResolved2 > 0)
							{
								rect.Y -= tabItemSize2.Height + interTabSpacingResolved2;
							}
							else
							{
								rect.Y -= tabItemSize2.Height;
							}
							break;
						default:
							if (interTabSpacingResolved2 > 0)
							{
								rect.X -= tabItemSize2.Width + interTabSpacingResolved2;
							}
							else
							{
								rect.X -= tabItemSize2.Width;
							}
							break;
						case 1:
						case 3:
							if (interTabSpacingResolved2 > 0)
							{
								rect.X += tabItemSize2.Width + interTabSpacingResolved2;
							}
							else
							{
								rect.X += tabItemSize2.Width;
							}
							break;
						}
						if (rect.Contains(tabGroupPt))
						{
							num3 = index - 1;
						}
					}
				}
				else if ((index2 > index && num2 < num) || (index2 > index && num2 > num))
				{
					Rectangle rect2 = ((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).Element.Rect;
					Size tabItemSize3 = destTabGroup.TabManager.GetTabItemSize((ITabItem)(object)mdiTab2);
					int interTabSpacingResolved3 = destTabGroup.TabManager.InterTabSpacingResolved;
					TabOrientation tabOrientation = destTabGroup.SettingsResolved.TabOrientation;
					Rectangle rectangle;
					switch (tabOrientation - 1)
					{
					case 4:
					case 6:
						rectangle = new Rectangle(rect2.X, rect2.Bottom - tabItemSize3.Height, rect2.Width, tabItemSize3.Height);
						break;
					case 5:
					case 7:
						rectangle = new Rectangle(rect2.X, rect2.Y, rect2.Width, tabItemSize3.Height);
						break;
					default:
						rectangle = new Rectangle(rect2.Right - tabItemSize3.Width, rect2.Y, tabItemSize3.Width, rect2.Height);
						break;
					case 1:
					case 3:
						rectangle = new Rectangle(rect2.X, rect2.Y, tabItemSize3.Width, rect2.Height);
						break;
					}
					if (rectangle.Contains(tabGroupPt))
					{
						num3 = index2;
					}
					else if (index != index2 - 1)
					{
						num3 = index2 - 1;
					}
				}
				else if ((index2 < index && num2 > num) || (index2 < index && num2 < num))
				{
					Rectangle rect3 = ((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).Element.Rect;
					Size tabItemSize4 = destTabGroup.TabManager.GetTabItemSize((ITabItem)(object)mdiTab2);
					int interTabSpacingResolved4 = destTabGroup.TabManager.InterTabSpacingResolved;
					TabOrientation tabOrientation = destTabGroup.SettingsResolved.TabOrientation;
					Rectangle rectangle2;
					switch (tabOrientation - 1)
					{
					case 4:
					case 6:
						rectangle2 = new Rectangle(rect3.X, rect3.Y, rect3.Width, tabItemSize4.Height);
						break;
					case 5:
					case 7:
						rectangle2 = new Rectangle(rect3.X, rect3.Bottom - tabItemSize4.Height, rect3.Width, tabItemSize4.Height);
						break;
					default:
						rectangle2 = new Rectangle(rect3.X, rect3.Y, tabItemSize4.Width, rect3.Height);
						break;
					case 1:
					case 3:
						rectangle2 = new Rectangle(rect3.Right - tabItemSize4.Width, rect3.Y, tabItemSize4.Width, rect3.Height);
						break;
					}
					if (rectangle2.Contains(tabGroupPt))
					{
						num3 = index2;
					}
					else if (index != index2 + 1)
					{
						num3 = index2 + 1;
					}
				}
				if (!AllowScroll || (int)((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).ScrollDirection == 0 || ((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).Distance >= ScrollDistanceMaximum || ((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).Distance <= ScrollDistanceMinimum)
				{
					if (((TabDragManager)this).HasScrollTimer && ((TabDragManager)this).ScrollTimer.Enabled)
					{
						((TabDragManager)this).ScrollTimer.Enabled = false;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					int scrollInterval = GetScrollInterval(((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).Distance);
					if (!((TabDragManager)this).HasScrollTimer || !((TabDragManager)this).ScrollTimer.Enabled || ((TabDragManager)this).ScrollTimer.Interval != scrollInterval)
					{
						if (scrollInterval >= 0)
						{
							((TabDragManager)this).ScrollTimer.Interval = 500;
							((TabDragManager)this).ScrollTimer.Enabled = true;
						}
						else if (((TabDragManager)this).ScrollTimer.Enabled)
						{
							((TabDragManager)this).ScrollTimer.Enabled = false;
						}
					}
					scrollDirection = ((TabHitTestInfo)(ref hitInfo)).ScrollDirection;
				}
				if (num3 == -1 && dropAction == MdiTabDropAction.TabGroup)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (InvokeDragOver(MdiTabDropAction.TabGroup, lastScreenPt, destTabGroup, Cursors.Arrow))
				{
					if (num3 != -1)
					{
						destTabGroup.Tabs.Reposition(mdiTab2, num3);
					}
					if (destTabGroup.Tabs[index3] != destTabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab)
					{
						if (destTabGroup.Tabs[index3] == null)
						{
							destTabGroup.TabManager.FirstDisplayedTabItem = destTabGroup.TabManager.LastVisibleTabItem;
						}
						else
						{
							destTabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab = destTabGroup.Tabs[index3];
						}
					}
					if (num3 == -1)
					{
						dropTabIndex = index;
					}
					else
					{
						dropTabIndex = num3;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					dropTabIndex = index;
				}
			}

			private bool InvokeDragOver(MdiTabDropAction dropAction, Point screenPt, MdiTabGroup tabGroup, Cursor defaultCursor)
			{
				return InvokeDragOver(dropAction, screenPt, tabGroup, defaultCursor, TabGroupDropArea.Middle);
			}

			private bool InvokeDragOver(MdiTabDropAction dropAction, Point screenPt, MdiTabGroup tabGroup, Cursor defaultCursor, TabGroupDropArea newTabGroupDropArea)
			{
				//IL_0065: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0082: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				this.dropAction = dropAction;
				dropTabGroup = tabGroup;
				dropNewGroupArea = newTabGroupDropArea;
				UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = TabGroup.Manager;
				if (manager == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (dropAction == MdiTabDropAction.None)
				{
					Cursor cursor = defaultCursor;
					if (cursor == null)
					{
						cursor = NoDragCursor;
					}
					if (Cursor.Current != cursor)
					{
						Cursor.Current = cursor;
					}
					return false;
				}
				RelativePosition relativePosition = (RelativePosition)((newTabGroupDropArea != TabGroupDropArea.LeftEdge && newTabGroupDropArea != TabGroupDropArea.TopEdge) ? 1 : 0);
				Orientation orientation = ((newTabGroupDropArea == TabGroupDropArea.LeftEdge || newTabGroupDropArea == TabGroupDropArea.RightEdge) ? Orientation.Vertical : Orientation.Horizontal);
				MdiTabDragOverEventArgs e = new MdiTabDragOverEventArgs(((TabDragManager)this).Tab as MdiTab, screenPt, dropAction, tabGroup, relativePosition, orientation);
				manager.InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabDragOver, e);
				Cursor cursor2 = ((e.DragCursor != null) ? e.DragCursor : defaultCursor);
				if (cursor2 == null)
				{
					cursor2 = (e.AllowDrop ? DragCursor : NoDragCursor);
				}
				if (Cursor.Current != cursor2)
				{
					Cursor.Current = cursor2;
				}
				if (!e.AllowDrop)
				{
					this.dropAction = MdiTabDropAction.None;
				}
				return e.AllowDrop;
			}

			private void CacheOriginalIndexes(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
			{
				if (originalIndexes == null)
				{
					originalIndexes = new HybridDictionary(TabGroup.Manager.TabGroups.Count);
				}
				if (originalIndexes[tabGroup] != null)
				{
					return;
				}
				HybridDictionary hybridDictionary = new HybridDictionary(tabGroup.Tabs.Count);
				originalIndexes.Add(tabGroup, hybridDictionary);
				foreach (MdiTab tab in tabGroup.Tabs)
				{
					hybridDictionary.Add(tab, ((ITabItem)tab).Index);
				}
			}

			private int GetOriginalTabIndex(MdiTab tab, MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
			{
				HybridDictionary hybridDictionary = originalIndexes[tabGroup] as HybridDictionary;
				if (hybridDictionary[tab] != null)
				{
					return (int)hybridDictionary[tab];
				}
				return int.MaxValue;
			}

			private TabGroupArea GetTabGroupArea(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, Point screenPt)
			{
				UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = TabGroup.Manager;
				if (manager == null || manager.MdiParent == null)
				{
					return TabGroupArea.None;
				}
				Point pt = manager.MdiParent.PointToClient(screenPt);
				TabGroupInfoManager tabGroupManager = manager.TabGroupManager;
				if (tabGroupManager.GetTabGroupControlRectangle(tabGroup).Contains(pt))
				{
					return TabGroupArea.TabGroupControl;
				}
				if (tabGroupManager.GetSplitterControlRectangle(tabGroup).Contains(pt))
				{
					return TabGroupArea.Splitter;
				}
				if (tabGroupManager.GetTabGroupFormRect(tabGroup).Contains(pt))
				{
					return TabGroupArea.Form;
				}
				return TabGroupArea.None;
			}

			private void HookMessageFilter()
			{
				if (messageFilterAdded)
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
					if (Utilities.CanAssert)
					{
						securityPermission.Assert();
					}
					Utilities.AddMessageFilter((IMessageFilter)this);
					messageFilterAdded = true;
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}

			private void UnhookMessageFilter()
			{
				if (!messageFilterAdded)
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
					if (Utilities.CanAssert)
					{
						securityPermission.Assert();
					}
					Utilities.RemoveMessageFilter((IMessageFilter)this);
					messageFilterAdded = false;
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}

			private void InitializeDragForm()
			{
				if (dragForm == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					dragForm.Opacity = 1.0;
					dragForm.TransparencyKey = dragForm.BackColor;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}

			private void RestoreTabPosition()
			{
				if (originalSrcTabGroup != null)
				{
					int index = ((ITabItem)originalSrcTabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab).Index;
					if (originalTabIndex != ((TabDragManager)this).Tab.Index)
					{
						originalSrcTabGroup.Tabs.Reposition(((TabDragManager)this).Tab as MdiTab, originalTabIndex);
					}
					if (originalSrcTabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab != null && ((ITabItem)originalSrcTabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab).Index != index)
					{
						originalSrcTabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab = originalSrcTabGroup.Tabs[index];
					}
				}
			}

			private void ShowDropRect(Rectangle screenRect)
			{
				if (screenRect == previousDropRect)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (UseReversibleFrame)
				{
					if (!previousDropRect.IsEmpty)
					{
						NativeWindowMethods.DrawReversibleFrame(previousDropRect);
					}
					if (!screenRect.IsEmpty)
					{
						NativeWindowMethods.DrawReversibleFrame(screenRect);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					bool isEmpty = screenRect.IsEmpty;
					OutlineForm outlineForm = DragForm;
					if (isEmpty)
					{
						outlineForm.Hide();
					}
					else if (!dragForm.Visible || dragForm.Bounds != screenRect)
					{
						NativeWindowMethods.ShowNoActivate(dragForm, screenRect, topmost: true);
					}
				}
				previousDropRect = screenRect;
			}

			[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode)]
			bool IMessageFilter.PreFilterMessage(ref Message m)
			{
				if (!((TabDragManager)this).IsDragging)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (m.Msg >= 256 && m.Msg <= 264)
				{
					if (m.Msg == 256 && m.WParam.ToInt64() == 27)
					{
						((TabDragManager)this).TabManager.CancelDrag();
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

			Font = 8
		}

		public int Extent
		{
			get
			{
				return extent;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value < 0)
				{
					throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", value, Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_65"));
				}
				if (Manager == null)
				{
					extent = value;
				}
				else
				{
					Manager.UpdateTabGroupExtent(this, value);
				}
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Extent);
			}
		}

		public MdiTab FirstDisplayedTab
		{
			get
			{
				if (!HasTabs)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return TabManager.FirstDisplayedTabItem as MdiTab;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!Tabs.Contains(value))
				{
					throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_8"), value, Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_9"));
				}
				TabManager.FirstDisplayedTabItem = (ITabItem)(object)value;
			}
		}

		public MdiTab SelectedTab
		{
			get
			{
				if (!HasTabs)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return TabManager.SelectedTabItem as MdiTab;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_8"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_10"));
				}
				if (!Tabs.Contains(value))
				{
					throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_8"), value, Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_11"));
				}
				TabManager.SelectedTabItem = (ITabItem)(object)value;
			}
		}

		internal bool HasTabsResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (HasTabs)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (HasTabGroups)
				{
					foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup in TabGroups)
					{
						if (tabGroup.HasTabsResolved)
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		protected internal TabManager TabManager
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0031: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_003b: Expected O, but got Unknown
				//IL_0048: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0052: Expected O, but got Unknown
				//IL_005f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0069: Expected O, but got Unknown
				//IL_0076: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0080: Expected O, but got Unknown
				//IL_008d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0097: Expected O, but got Unknown
				//IL_00a4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_00ae: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (tabManager == null || ((DisposableObject)(object)tabManager).Disposed)
				{
					tabManager = CreateTabManager();
					tabManager.SelectedTabItemChanging += new SelectedTabItemChangingEventHandler(OnTabManagerSelectedTabItemChanging);
					tabManager.SelectedTabItemChanged += new SelectedTabItemChangedEventHandler(OnTabManagerSelectedTabItemChanged);
					tabManager.TabScrolling += new TabScrollingEventHandler(OnTabManagerTabScrolling);
					tabManager.TabScrolled += new TabScrolledEventHandler(OnTabManagerTabScrolled);
					tabManager.TabItemMoving += new TabItemMovingEventHandler(OnTabManagerTabMoving);
					tabManager.TabItemMoved += new TabItemMovedEventHandler(OnTabManagerTabMoved);
				}
				return tabManager;
			}
		}

		protected internal bool HasTabManager => tabManager != null;

		private bool ContainsActiveTab => ((Manager == null) ? null : Manager.ActiveTab)?.IsDescendantOf(this) ?? false;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[RefreshProperties(RefreshProperties.Repaint)]
		public int ClientExtent
		{
			get
			{
				int num = Extent;
				if (Manager != null && Manager.TabGroupManager.NeedsSplitter(this))
				{
					num -= Manager.SplitterWidthResolved;
				}
				return num;
			}
			set
			{
				if (Manager != null && Manager.TabGroupManager.NeedsSplitter(this))
				{
					value += Manager.SplitterWidthResolved;
				}
				Extent = value;
			}
		}

		internal MdiTab SerializedFirstDisplayedTab
		{
			get
			{
				if (HasTabs)
				{
					int num = serializedFirstDisplayedTabIndex;
					if (num >= 0 && num < Tabs.Count)
					{
						return Tabs[num];
					}
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		internal MdiTab SerializedSelectedTab
		{
			get
			{
				if (HasTabs)
				{
					int num = serializedSelectedTabIndex;
					if (num >= 0 && num < Tabs.Count)
					{
						return Tabs[num];
					}
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public int Index
		{
			get
			{
				MdiTabGroupsCollection parentCollection = ParentCollection;
				if (parentCollection == null)
				{
					cachedIndex = -1;
				}
				else if (cachedIndex < 0 || cachedIndex >= parentCollection.Count || parentCollection[cachedIndex] != this)
				{
					cachedIndex = parentCollection.IndexOf(this);
				}
				return cachedIndex;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public MdiTabGroup Parent => parentGroup;

		internal MdiTabGroupsCollection ParentCollection
		{
			get
			{
				if (Parent != null)
				{
					return Parent.TabGroups;
				}
				if (Manager != null)
				{
					return Manager.TabGroups;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		internal Orientation ParentOrientation
		{
			get
			{
				if (Parent != null)
				{
					return Parent.TabGroupOrientation;
				}
				if (Manager != null)
				{
					return Manager.Orientation;
				}
				return Orientation.Horizontal;
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public bool HasTabGroups
		{
			get
			{
				if (tabGroups != null)
				{
					return tabGroups.Count > 0;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public Orientation TabGroupOrientation

		{

				int num = 0;
				while (parent != null)
				{
					num++;
					parent = parent.Parent;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

						{
							if (((ITabItem)tab).Visible)

								num++;

						return num;

			}
		}

		protected internal MdiTabGroup(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager)
		{

			//IL_0025: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (manager == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_3"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_7"));

		protected MdiTabGroup(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0025: Expected O, but got Unknown
			SerializationInfoEnumerator enumerator = info.GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				SerializationEntry current = enumerator.Current;
				switch (current.Name)
				{
				case "Key":
					Key = (string)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(string), Key);
					break;
				case "Settings":
					settings = (MdiTabGroupSettings)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(MdiTabGroupSettings), null);
					break;
				case "TabSettings":
					tabSettings = (MdiTabSettings)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(MdiTabSettings), null);
					break;
				case "Tabs":
					tabs = (MdiTabsCollection)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(MdiTabsCollection), null);
					break;
				case "Extent":
					extent = (int)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(int), extent);
					break;
				case "Tag":
					DeserializeTag(current);
					break;
				case "FirstDisplayTabIndex":
					serializedFirstDisplayedTabIndex = (int)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(int), serializedFirstDisplayedTabIndex);
					break;
				case "SelectedTabIndex":
					serializedSelectedTabIndex = (int)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(int), serializedSelectedTabIndex);
					break;
				case "TabGroups":
					tabGroups = (MdiTabGroupsCollection)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(MdiTabGroupsCollection), null);
					break;
				case "ActiveTabGroupIndex":
					serializedActiveTabGroupIndex = (int)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(int), -1);
					break;
				case "PreMaximizedExtent":
					preMaximizeExtent = (int)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(int), preMaximizeExtent);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		protected bool ShouldSerializeSettings()
		{
			if (settings != null)

			}
			return false;
		}

		public void ResetSettings()
		{

		}

		protected bool ShouldSerializeExtent()
		{
			return extent > 0;
		}

		protected bool ShouldSerializeTabs()
		{
			return HasTabs;
		}

		public void ResetTabs()
		{
			if (!HasTabs)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MdiTab tab in Tabs)
			{
				tab.Reset();
			}
		}

		protected bool ShouldSerializeTabGroups()
		{
			if (!HasTabGroups)
			{
				return false;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup in TabGroups)
			{
				if (tabGroup.ShouldSerialize())
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void ResetTabGroups()
		{
			if (!HasTabGroups)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup in TabGroups)
			{
				tabGroup.Reset();
			}
		}

		public void MoveAllTabs(MdiTabGroupPosition relation)
		{
			UltraTabbedMdiManager ultraTabbedMdiManager = Manager;
			if (ultraTabbedMdiManager == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_12"));
			}
			ultraTabbedMdiManager.MoveAllTabs(this, relation);
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			ResetKey();
			ResetSettings();
			ResetTabs();
			ResetTabSettings();
			ResetTag();
			ResetTabGroups();
		}

		public bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (!ShouldSerializeExtent() && !ShouldSerializeKey() && !ShouldSerializeSettings() && !ShouldSerializeTabSettings() && !ShouldSerializeTabs() && !ShouldSerializeTabGroups())
			{
				return ShouldSerializeTag();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void ResolveTabAreaAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00c7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cc: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cd: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cf: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0101: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			UltraTabbedMdiManager ultraTabbedMdiManager = Manager;
			ResolutionOrderInfo resolutionOrderInfo = StyleUtils.GetResolutionOrderInfo(ultraTabbedMdiManager);
			UIRole uIRoleTabArea = UIRoleTabArea;
			if (uIRoleTabArea != null && ((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrderInfo)).UseStyleBefore)
			{
				uIRoleTabArea.ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (RoleState)1);
			}
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrderInfo)).UseControlInfo)
			{
				if (HasSettings && Settings.HasTabAreaAppearance)
				{
					Settings.TabAreaAppearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
				if (ultraTabbedMdiManager != null && ultraTabbedMdiManager.HasTabGroupSettings && ultraTabbedMdiManager.TabGroupSettings.HasTabAreaAppearance)
				{
					ultraTabbedMdiManager.TabGroupSettings.TabAreaAppearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
			}
			if (uIRoleTabArea != null && ((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrderInfo)).UseStyleAfter)
			{
				uIRoleTabArea.ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (RoleState)1);
			}
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrderInfo)).UseControlInfo && ultraTabbedMdiManager != null && ultraTabbedMdiManager.HasAppearance)
			{
				ultraTabbedMdiManager.Appearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
			if ((long)requestedProps == 0L)
			{
				return;
			}
			TabStyle tabStyle = SettingsResolved.TabStyle;
			switch (tabStyle - 1)
			{
			case 3:
			case 4:
				if (Utilities.ShouldSetAppearanceProperty(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (AppearancePropFlags)1))
				{
					Color control = SystemColors.Control;
					float luminance = Math.Min(control.GetBrightness() * 1.0627451f, 1f);
					control = XPItemColor.ColorFromHLS(control.GetHue(), luminance, control.GetSaturation());
					((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColor = control;
					requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEuL);
				}
				break;
			case 10:
				if (Utilities.ShouldSetAppearanceProperty(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (AppearancePropFlags)1))
				{
					((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColor = Office2007ColorTable.Colors.TabHeaderAreaBackColor;
					requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEuL);
				}
				break;
			}
			requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFC1FFFF7uL);
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			bool flag = Key != null && Key.Length > 0;
			int count = Tabs.Count;
			if (flag)
			{
				return string.Format(null, "MdiTabGroup Key='{0}', Tab Count={1}", new object[2] { Key, count });
			}
			return string.Format(null, "MdiTabGroup Tab Count={0}", new object[1] { count });
		}

		protected override void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			if (HasSettings && propChangeInfo.Source == settings)
			{
				propertyCache.ClearCachedPropertyValues();
				NotifyTabManager(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Settings, propChangeInfo);
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Settings, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (HasTabSettings && propChangeInfo.Source == tabSettings)
			{
				NotifyTabManager(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabSettings, propChangeInfo);
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabSettings, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (tabs != null && Tabs == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				NotifyTabManager(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Tabs, propChangeInfo);
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Tabs, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (tabGroups != null && tabGroups == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabGroups, propChangeInfo);
			}
			base.OnSubObjectPropChanged(propChangeInfo);
		}

		protected virtual TabManager CreateTabManager()
		{
			return (TabManager)(object)new MdiTabGroupManager(this);
		}

		protected override void OnDispose()
		{
			DisposeTabManager();
			if (settings != null)
			{
				settings.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				settings.Dispose();
				settings = null;
			}
			base.OnDispose();
		}

		protected internal void DirtyTabManager(TabManagerChangeType changeType)
		{
			if (changeType == TabManagerChangeType.None)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = (changeType & TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate) != 0;
			bool flag2 = (changeType & TabManagerChangeType.Collection) != 0;
			bool flag3 = (changeType & TabManagerChangeType.AllMetrics) != 0;
			if (HasTabManager)
			{
				if ((changeType & TabManagerChangeType.Font) != TabManagerChangeType.None)
				{
					TabManager.DirtyFont();
				}
				if (flag || flag2 || flag3)
				{
					TabManager.Dirty(flag, flag2, flag3);
				}
			}
			if ((flag2 || flag3) && Manager != null)
			{
				Manager.DirtyGroupPosition(this);
			}
		}

		internal void DirtyTabItem(MdiTab tab, bool invalidate, bool textChanged, bool imageChanged)
		{
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0027: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002a: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_002c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (!HasTabManager)
			{
				return;
			}
			TabManager.DirtyTabItem((ITabItem)(object)tab, invalidate, textChanged, imageChanged);
			if (textChanged)
			{
				TextOrientation textOrientationResolved = TabManager.TextOrientationResolved;
				if ((int)textOrientationResolved == 2 || (int)textOrientationResolved == 4)
				{
					DirtyTabManager(TabManagerChangeType.Collection | TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate);
				}
				else
				{
					Manager.DirtyGroupPosition(this);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void InitializeSelectedTab(MdiTab tab)
		{
			isInitializingSelectedTab = true;
			try
			{
				TabManager.SelectedTabItem = (ITabItem)(object)tab;
				TabManager.Dirty(true, false, false);
			}
			finally
			{
				isInitializingSelectedTab = false;
			}
		}

		internal void InitializeExtent(int extent)
		{
			if (this.extent != extent)
			{
				this.extent = extent;
				DirtyTabManager(GetTabChangeType(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Extent));
			}
		}

		internal void InitializeTabbedMdiManager(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager)
		{
			this.manager = manager;
			if (HasTabGroups)
			{
				foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup in TabGroups)
				{
					tabGroup.InitializeTabbedMdiManager(manager);
				}
			}
			if (this.manager == null)
			{
				DisposeTabManager();
			}
		}

		internal void InitializeParentTabGroup(MdiTabGroup parentGroup)
		{
			this.parentGroup = parentGroup;
		}

		internal static TabManagerChangeType GetTabChangeType(TabbedMdiPropertyIds property)
		{
			switch (property)
			{
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.CloseButtonAppearance:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ScrollButtonAppearance:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ScrollTrackAppearance:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabAreaAppearance:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabListButtonAppearance:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.CloseButtonAlignment:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.HotTrackCloseButtonAppearance:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.PressedCloseButtonAppearance:
				return TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate;
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ActiveTabAppearance:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Appearance:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.HotTrackTabAppearance:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.SelectedTabAppearance:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabAppearance:
				return TabManagerChangeType.AllMetrics | TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate | TabManagerChangeType.Font;
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.AllowClose:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ButtonStyle:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.HotTrack:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ImageTransparentColor:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ScrollArrowStyle:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ShowPartialTabs:
				return TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate;
			default:
				return TabManagerChangeType.None;
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Settings:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabGroupSettings:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabSettings:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Text:
				return TabManagerChangeType.None;
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.DisplayFormIcon:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ImageList:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ImageSize:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MaxTabWidth:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MinTabWidth:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabButtonStyle:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabHeight:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabPadding:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabOrientation:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabSizing:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabStyle:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabWidth:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TextOrientation:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.UseMnemonics:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.SpaceAfterTabs:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.SpaceBeforeTabs:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.CloseButtonLocation:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.CloseButtonVisibility:
				return TabManagerChangeType.AllMetrics | TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate;
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Extent:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.InterTabSpacing:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Orientation:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ScrollButtons:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ScrollButtonTypes:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ScrollTrackExtent:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ShowButtonSeparators:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ShowCloseButton:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.SplitterWidth:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabsPerRow:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabAreaMargins:
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ShowTabListButton:
				return TabManagerChangeType.Collection | TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate;
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Tabs:
				return TabManagerChangeType.Collection | TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate;
			case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MdiTab:
				return TabManagerChangeType.Collection | TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate;
			}
		}

		internal static TabManagerChangeType GetTabChangeType(TabbedMdiPropertyIds propertyChanged, PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			//IL_00dc: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e1: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00fe: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			//IL_00fe: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0104: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (propChangeInfo == null)
			{
				return TabManagerChangeType.None;
			}
			if (propChangeInfo.Source is MdiTabGroupSettings || propChangeInfo.Source is MdiTabSettings || propChangeInfo.Source is MdiTabsCollection)
			{
				if (!(propChangeInfo.PropId is TabbedMdiPropertyIds))
				{
					return TabManagerChangeType.None;
				}
				TabbedMdiPropertyIds tabbedMdiPropertyIds = (TabbedMdiPropertyIds)(object)propChangeInfo.PropId;
				if (propChangeInfo.Trigger == null)
				{
					return GetTabChangeType(tabbedMdiPropertyIds);
				}
				return GetTabChangeType(tabbedMdiPropertyIds, propChangeInfo.Trigger);
			}
			if (propChangeInfo.Source is AppearanceBase)
			{
				switch (propertyChanged)
				{
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.CloseButtonAppearance:
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ScrollButtonAppearance:
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabListButtonAppearance:
					return GetTabChangeType(propertyChanged);
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ActiveTabAppearance:
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Appearance:
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.HotTrackTabAppearance:
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.SelectedTabAppearance:
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabAppearance:
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabAreaAppearance:
					if (IsFontDataChange(propChangeInfo))
					{
						return TabManagerChangeType.AllMetrics | TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate | TabManagerChangeType.Font;
					}
					if (propChangeInfo.PropId is AppearancePropIds)
					{
						AppearancePropIds val = (AppearancePropIds)(object)propChangeInfo.PropId;
						switch (val - 5)
						{
						default:
							if ((int)val != 1001)
							{
								break;
							}
							goto case 0;
						case 0:
						case 1:
						case 2:
						case 3:
						case 4:
							return TabManagerChangeType.AllMetrics | TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate;
						}
					}
					return TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate;
				}
			}
			else if (propChangeInfo.Source is Margins)
			{
				return GetTabChangeType(propertyChanged);
			}
			return TabManagerChangeType.None;
		}

		internal void AdjustAppearancePropertiesForTabOrientation(Color topColor, Color bottomColor, ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003c: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			//IL_003f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0058: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0050: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0069: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			GradientStyle backGradientStyle = (GradientStyle)1;
			Color backColor = topColor;
			Color backColor2 = bottomColor;
			TabOrientation tabOrientation = SettingsResolved.TabOrientation;
			switch ((int)tabOrientation)
			{
			case 0:
			case 1:
			case 2:
				backGradientStyle = (GradientStyle)2;
				backColor = topColor;
				backColor2 = bottomColor;
				break;
			case 3:
			case 4:
				if (!backColor2.IsEmpty)
				{
					backGradientStyle = (GradientStyle)2;
					backColor = bottomColor;
					backColor2 = topColor;
				}
				break;
			case 5:
			case 6:
				backGradientStyle = (GradientStyle)3;
				backColor = topColor;
				backColor2 = bottomColor;
				break;
			case 7:
			case 8:
				if (!backColor2.IsEmpty)
				{
					backGradientStyle = (GradientStyle)3;
					backColor = bottomColor;
					backColor2 = topColor;
				}
				break;
			}
			if (((ulong)requestedProps & 1uL) == 1 && !((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)1))
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColor = backColor;
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEuL);
			}
			if (((ulong)requestedProps & 0x4000000uL) == 67108864 && !((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)67108864))
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColor2 = backColor2;

		private static bool IsFontDataChange(PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			//IL_004a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0050: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			//IL_005a: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (propChangeInfo == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (propChangeInfo.PropId is FontDataPropIds)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (propChangeInfo.Source is AppearanceBase && propChangeInfo.Trigger != null)
			{
				return IsFontDataChange(propChangeInfo.Trigger);
			}
			if (!(propChangeInfo.PropId is AppearancePropIds))
			{

			}
			AppearancePropIds val = (AppearancePropIds)(object)propChangeInfo.PropId;
			if ((int)val != 4)

			}

		}

		private void NotifyTabManager(TabbedMdiPropertyIds propertyChanged, PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			if (propChangeInfo == null || !(propChangeInfo.PropId is TabbedMdiPropertyIds) || propChangeInfo.Source == tabGroups)
			{
				return;
			}
			TabbedMdiPropertyIds tabbedMdiPropertyIds = (TabbedMdiPropertyIds)(object)propChangeInfo.PropId;
			if (propChangeInfo.Source != tabs || propChangeInfo.Trigger == null || !ProcessTabChangeNotification(propChangeInfo.Trigger))
			{
				if (propChangeInfo.Trigger == null)
				{
					DirtyTabManager(GetTabChangeType(tabbedMdiPropertyIds));
				}
				else if (propChangeInfo.Source != tabs)
				{
					DirtyTabManager(GetTabChangeType(tabbedMdiPropertyIds, propChangeInfo.Trigger));
				}
			}
		}

		private bool ProcessTabChangeNotification(PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			//IL_0061: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0066: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0067: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0069: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0083: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			//IL_0083: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0089: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (!(propChangeInfo.Source is MdiTab))
			{
				return false;
			}
			MdiTab mdiTab = propChangeInfo.Source as MdiTab;
			PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo2 = propChangeInfo.FindTrigger(null);
			if (propChangeInfo2.PropId is TabbedMdiPropertyIds && (TabbedMdiPropertyIds)(object)propChangeInfo2.PropId == TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Text)
			{
				DirtyTabItem(mdiTab, invalidate: true, textChanged: true, imageChanged: false);
			}
			else if (propChangeInfo2.PropId is AppearancePropIds)
			{
				AppearancePropIds val = (AppearancePropIds)(object)propChangeInfo2.PropId;
				switch (val - 5)
				{
				default:
					if ((int)val == 1001)
					{
						DirtyTabItem(mdiTab, invalidate: true, textChanged: true, imageChanged: true);
					}
					else
					{
						DirtyTabItem(mdiTab, invalidate: true, textChanged: false, imageChanged: false);
					}
					break;
				case 0:
				case 1:
					DirtyTabItem(mdiTab, invalidate: true, textChanged: true, imageChanged: false);
					break;
				case 2:
				case 3:
				case 4:
					DirtyTabItem(mdiTab, invalidate: true, textChanged: false, imageChanged: true);
					break;
				}
			}
			else if (propChangeInfo2.PropId is TabbedMdiPropertyIds)
			{
				TabbedMdiPropertyIds tabbedMdiPropertyIds = (TabbedMdiPropertyIds)(object)propChangeInfo2.PropId;
				switch (tabbedMdiPropertyIds)
				{
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.DisplayFormIcon:
					DirtyTabItem(mdiTab, invalidate: true, textChanged: false, imageChanged: true);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.AllowClose:
					if (mdiTab == SelectedTab)
					{
						DirtyTabManager(TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate);
					}
					break;
				default:
				{
					TabManagerChangeType tabChangeType = GetTabChangeType(tabbedMdiPropertyIds);
					bool flag = (tabChangeType & TabManagerChangeType.AllMetrics) != 0;
					bool flag2 = (tabChangeType & TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate) != 0;
					if (flag || flag2)
					{
						DirtyTabItem(mdiTab, flag2, flag, flag);
					}
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void DisposeTabManager()
		{
			//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0022: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_002f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0039: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0046: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0050: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_005d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0067: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0074: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_008b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0095: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (tabManager != null)
			{
				tabManager.SelectedTabItemChanging -= new SelectedTabItemChangingEventHandler(OnTabManagerSelectedTabItemChanging);
				tabManager.SelectedTabItemChanged -= new SelectedTabItemChangedEventHandler(OnTabManagerSelectedTabItemChanged);
				tabManager.TabScrolling -= new TabScrollingEventHandler(OnTabManagerTabScrolling);
				tabManager.TabScrolled -= new TabScrolledEventHandler(OnTabManagerTabScrolled);
				tabManager.TabItemMoving -= new TabItemMovingEventHandler(OnTabManagerTabMoving);
				tabManager.TabItemMoved -= new TabItemMovedEventHandler(OnTabManagerTabMoved);
				((DisposableObject)(object)tabManager).Dispose();
				tabManager = null;
			}
		}

		private static MdiTabGroup GetLeafTabGroup(MdiTabGroup startTabGroup, bool previous)
		{
			if (!previous && startTabGroup.HasTabGroups)
			{
				return UltraTabbedMdiManager.GetFirstLeafTabGroup(startTabGroup.TabGroups);
			}
			return GetLeafTabGroupHelper(startTabGroup, previous);
		}

		private static MdiTabGroup GetLeafTabGroupHelper(MdiTabGroup startTabGroup, bool previous)
		{
			MdiTabGroupsCollection parentCollection = startTabGroup.ParentCollection;
			int num = ((!previous) ? 1 : (-1));
			int num2 = startTabGroup.Index + num;
			if (num2 >= 0 && num2 < parentCollection.Count)
			{
				MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = parentCollection[num2];
				if (!mdiTabGroup.HasTabGroups)
				{
					return mdiTabGroup;
				}
				if (previous)
				{
					return UltraTabbedMdiManager.GetLastLeafTabGroup(mdiTabGroup.TabGroups);
				}
				return UltraTabbedMdiManager.GetFirstLeafTabGroup(mdiTabGroup.TabGroups);
			}
			if (startTabGroup.Parent == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return GetLeafTabGroupHelper(startTabGroup.Parent, previous);
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.SerializationFormatter)]
		void ISerializable.GetObjectData(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			if (ShouldSerializeKey())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "Key", Key);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeSettings())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "Settings", Settings);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTabSettings())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "TabSettings", TabSettings);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTabs())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "Tabs", Tabs);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeExtent())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "Extent", Extent);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTag())
			{
				SerializeTag(info);
			}
			if (!HasTabGroups && FirstDisplayedTab != null)
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "FirstDisplayTabIndex", ((ITabItem)FirstDisplayedTab).Index);
			}
			if (!HasTabGroups && SelectedTab != null)
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "SelectedTabIndex", ((ITabItem)SelectedTab).Index);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTabGroups())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "TabGroups", TabGroups);
			}
			if (HasTabGroups && ContainsActiveTab)
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "ActiveTabGroupIndex", Manager.GetTabGroupIndex(Manager.ActiveTab, this));
			}
			if (Manager != null && Manager.MaximizedTabGroup != null)
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "PreMaximizedExtent", PreMaximizedExtent);
			}
			GetObjectData(info, context);
		}

		void IDeserializationCallback.OnDeserialization(object sender)
		{
			if (settings != null)
			{
				settings.InitializeOwner(this);
				settings.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			}
			if (tabSettings != null)
			{
				tabSettings.InitializeOwner(this);
				tabSettings.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			}
			if (tabs != null)
			{
				tabs.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				foreach (MdiTab tab in Tabs)
				{
					tab.InitializeOwner(this);
				}
			}
			if (tabGroups != null)
			{
				tabGroups.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup in TabGroups)
				{
					tabGroup.InitializeParentTabGroup(this);
				}
			}
			OnDeserialization(sender);
		}

		Font ITabProvider.CreateMetricsFont()
		{
			if (Manager != null)
			{
				return Manager.CreateTabMetricsFont(this);
			}
			return Control.DefaultFont.Clone() as Font;
		}

		TabState ITabProvider.GetTabItemState(ITabItem tab)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0013: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0016: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0068: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0051: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0052: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			TabState val = (TabState)0;
			if (tab != null)
			{
				if (TabManager.SelectedTabItem == tab)
				{
					val = (TabState)(val | 1);
				}
				if (!TabManager.IsDragging && TabManager.HotTrackTabItem == tab && Manager != null && !Manager.IsDraggingInternal && tab.HotTrack)
				{
					val = (TabState)(val | 2);
				}

		}

		}

	[Serializable]
	[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableCollectionConverter))]
	public class MdiTabGroupsCollection : KeyedSubObjectsCollectionBase, ISerializable, IDeserializationCallback
	{
		public class MdiTabGroupEnumerator : DisposableObjectEnumeratorBase
		{
			public MdiTabGroup Current => ((IEnumerator)this).Current as MdiTabGroup;

			public MdiTabGroupEnumerator(MdiTabGroupsCollection collection)
				: base(collection)
			{
			}
		}

		private class NestedMdiTabGroupEnumerator : IEnumerator
		{
			private MdiTabGroupsCollection rootTabGroups;

			private bool leafGroupsOnly;

			private MdiTabGroup currentGroup;

			private Stack parentCollectionStack;

			object IEnumerator.Current => currentGroup;

			internal NestedMdiTabGroupEnumerator(MdiTabGroupsCollection rootTabGroups, bool leafGroupsOnly)
			{
				this.rootTabGroups = rootTabGroups;
				this.leafGroupsOnly = leafGroupsOnly;
				parentCollectionStack = new Stack(3);
				((IEnumerator)this).Reset();
			}

			bool IEnumerator.MoveNext()
			{
				if (rootTabGroups.Count == 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
				MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = null;
				MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup2 = ((currentGroup == null) ? rootTabGroups[0] : currentGroup);
				if (currentGroup == null && (!leafGroupsOnly || mdiTabGroup2.HasTabs))
				{
					mdiTabGroup = mdiTabGroup2;
				}
				else if (mdiTabGroup2.HasTabGroups)
				{
					while (mdiTabGroup == null && mdiTabGroup2.HasTabGroups)
					{
						parentCollectionStack.Push(mdiTabGroup2.TabGroups);
						mdiTabGroup2 = mdiTabGroup2.TabGroups[0];
						if (!mdiTabGroup2.HasTabGroups || !leafGroupsOnly)
						{
							mdiTabGroup = mdiTabGroup2;
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					while (mdiTabGroup == null)
					{
						MdiTabGroupsCollection mdiTabGroupsCollection = parentCollectionStack.Peek() as MdiTabGroupsCollection;
						int num = ((mdiTabGroup2.Index < 0) ? mdiTabGroupsCollection.IndexOf(mdiTabGroup2) : mdiTabGroup2.Index);
						if (num < mdiTabGroupsCollection.Count - 1)
						{
							mdiTabGroup2 = mdiTabGroupsCollection[num + 1];
							if (!mdiTabGroup2.HasTabGroups || !leafGroupsOnly)
							{
								mdiTabGroup = mdiTabGroup2;
								continue;
							}
							while (mdiTabGroup2.HasTabGroups)
							{
								parentCollectionStack.Push(mdiTabGroup2.TabGroups);
								mdiTabGroup2 = mdiTabGroup2.TabGroups[0];
								if (!mdiTabGroup2.HasTabGroups || !leafGroupsOnly)
								{
									mdiTabGroup = mdiTabGroup2;
								}
							}
						}
						else
						{
							if (mdiTabGroupsCollection == rootTabGroups || mdiTabGroup2.Parent == null)
							{
								break;
							}
							mdiTabGroup2 = mdiTabGroup2.Parent;
							parentCollectionStack.Pop();
						}
					}
				}
				if (mdiTabGroup != null)
				{
					currentGroup = mdiTabGroup;
				}
				return mdiTabGroup != null;
			}

			void IEnumerator.Reset()
			{
				currentGroup = null;
				parentCollectionStack.Clear();
				parentCollectionStack.Push(rootTabGroups);
			}
		}

		private class NestedMdiTabGroupsCollection : IEnumerable
		{
			private bool groupsWithTabsOnly;

			private MdiTabGroupsCollection tabGroups;

			internal NestedMdiTabGroupsCollection(MdiTabGroupsCollection tabGroups, bool groupsWithTabsOnly)
			{
				this.tabGroups = tabGroups;
				this.groupsWithTabsOnly = groupsWithTabsOnly;
			}

			IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
			{
				return new NestedMdiTabGroupEnumerator(tabGroups, groupsWithTabsOnly);
			}
		}

		private int initialCapacity = 4;

		public MdiTabGroup this[int index] => GetItem(index) as MdiTabGroup;

		public MdiTabGroup this[string key] => GetItem(key) as MdiTabGroup;

		public override bool IsReadOnly => true;

		protected override int InitialCapacity => initialCapacity;

		public MdiTabGroupsCollection()
		{
		}

		public MdiTabGroupsCollection(int initialCapacity)
		{
			this.initialCapacity = initialCapacity;
		}

		public MdiTabGroupsCollection(MdiTabGroup[] tabGroups)
			: this()
		{
			if (tabGroups == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_15"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_16"));
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup in tabGroups)
			{
				if (mdiTabGroup == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_17"));
				}
			}
			AddRange(tabGroups);
		}

		protected MdiTabGroupsCollection(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			SerializationInfoEnumerator enumerator = info.GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				SerializationEntry current = enumerator.Current;
				if (current.Value is MdiTabGroup)
				{
					MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = (MdiTabGroup)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(MdiTabGroup), null);
					if (mdiTabGroup != null)
					{
						InternalAdd(mdiTabGroup);
					}
					continue;
				}
				string name = current.Name;
				if (!(name == "Count"))
				{
					if (name == "Tag")
					{
						DeserializeTag(current);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					initialCapacity = Math.Max(initialCapacity, (int)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(int), initialCapacity));
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChange)
		{
			if (propChange.Source is MdiTabGroup)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MdiTabGroup, propChange);
			}
			base.OnSubObjectPropChanged(propChange);
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.SerializationFormatter)]
		protected virtual void GetObjectData(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnDeserialization(object sender)
		{
		}

		internal void Add(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			Insert(Count, tabGroup);
		}

		internal void AddRange(MdiTabGroup[] tabGroups)
		{
			InsertRange(Count, tabGroups);
		}

		internal void Insert(int index, MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			tabGroup.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			InternalInsert(index, tabGroup);
			NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MdiTabGroup);
		}

		internal void InsertRange(int index, MdiTabGroup[] tabGroups)
		{
			foreach (MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup in tabGroups)
			{
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup2 in tabGroups)
			{
				mdiTabGroup2.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				InternalInsert(index, mdiTabGroup2);
				index++;
			}
			NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MdiTabGroup);
		}

		internal void Remove(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			tabGroup.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			InternalRemove(tabGroup);
			NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MdiTabGroup);
		}

		internal void Clear()
		{
			MdiTabGroupEnumerator mdiTabGroupEnumerator = GetEnumerator();
			try
			{
				while (mdiTabGroupEnumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					MdiTabGroup current = mdiTabGroupEnumerator.Current;
					current.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				IDisposable disposable = mdiTabGroupEnumerator as IDisposable;
				if (disposable != null)
				{
					disposable.Dispose();
				}
			}
			InternalClear();
			NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MdiTabGroup);
		}

		internal IEnumerable GetNestedTabGroups(bool leafGroupsOnly)
		{
			return new NestedMdiTabGroupsCollection(this, leafGroupsOnly);
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.SerializationFormatter)]
		void ISerializable.GetObjectData(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "Count", Count);
			for (int i = 0; i < Count; i++)
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, i.ToString(), this[i]);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTag())
			{
				SerializeTag(info);
			}
			GetObjectData(info, context);
		}

		void IDeserializationCallback.OnDeserialization(object sender)
		{
			MdiTabGroupEnumerator mdiTabGroupEnumerator = GetEnumerator();
			try
			{
				while (mdiTabGroupEnumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					MdiTabGroup current = mdiTabGroupEnumerator.Current;
					current.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				IDisposable disposable = mdiTabGroupEnumerator as IDisposable;
				if (disposable != null)
				{
					disposable.Dispose();
				}
			}
			OnDeserialization(sender);
		}
	}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Visible)]
		[DefaultValue(false)]
		public bool ForceSerialization
		{
			get
			{
				if (Manager != null && Manager.IsDesignMode && this == Manager.TabGroupSettings)
				{
					InheritanceAttribute inheritanceAttribute = Utilities.GetInheritanceAttribute((Component)(object)Manager);
					if (inheritanceAttribute != null && inheritanceAttribute.InheritanceLevel == InheritanceLevel.Inherited)
					{
						return !ShouldSerialize();
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
			set
			{
			}
		}

			{

			}
			return true;
		}

		public void Reset()

		protected override void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChange)
		{
			if (HasCloseButtonAppearance && propChange.Source == closeButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.CloseButtonAppearance, propChange);
			}
			else if (HasScrollButtonAppearance && propChange.Source == scrollButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ScrollButtonAppearance, propChange);
			}
			else if (HasTabAreaAppearance && propChange.Source == tabAreaAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabAreaAppearance, propChange);
			}
			else if (HasScrollTrackAppearance && propChange.Source == scrollTrackAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ScrollTrackAppearance, propChange);
			}
			else if (tabAreaMargins != null && propChange.Source == tabAreaMargins)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabAreaMargins, propChange);
			}
			else if (HasTabListButtonAppearance && propChange.Source == tabListButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabListButtonAppearance, propChange);
			}
			else if (HasHotTrackCloseButtonAppearance && propChange.Source == hotTrackCloseButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.HotTrackCloseButtonAppearance, propChange);
			}
			else if (HasPressedCloseButtonAppearance && propChange.Source == pressedCloseButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.PressedCloseButtonAppearance, propChange);
			}
			base.OnSubObjectPropChanged(propChange);
		}

		protected override void OnDispose()
		{
			owner = null;
			if (closeButtonAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				closeButtonAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				closeButtonAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				closeButtonAppearanceHolder = null;
			}
			if (scrollButtonAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				scrollButtonAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				scrollButtonAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				scrollButtonAppearanceHolder = null;
			}
			if (tabAreaAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				tabAreaAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				tabAreaAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				tabAreaAppearanceHolder = null;
			}
			if (scrollTrackAppearanceHolder != null)
			{

				scrollTrackAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				scrollTrackAppearanceHolder = null;
			}
			if (tabListButtonAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				tabListButtonAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				tabListButtonAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				tabListButtonAppearanceHolder = null;

			base.Dispose();

		public bool AllowDrop
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_002a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0030: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				//IL_0058: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_005e: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				if (tabGroup.HasSettings && tabGroup.Settings.ShouldSerializeAllowDrop())
				{
					return (int)tabGroup.Settings.AllowDrop == 1;
				}
				UltraTabbedMdiManager manager = Manager;
				if (manager != null && manager.HasTabGroupSettings && manager.TabGroupSettings.ShouldSerializeAllowDrop())
				{
					return (int)manager.TabGroupSettings.AllowDrop == 1;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		{
