		public MdiTabGroupSettingsResolved(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (tabGroup == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_13"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_24"));
			}
			this.tabGroup = tabGroup;
		}

		private static void MergeMargins(Margins destination, Margins source)
		{
			if (!destination.ShouldSerializeLeft() && source.ShouldSerializeLeft())
			{
				destination.Left = source.Left;
			}
			if (!destination.ShouldSerializeRight() && source.ShouldSerializeRight())
			{
				destination.Right = source.Right;
			}
			if (!destination.ShouldSerializeTop() && source.ShouldSerializeTop())
			{
				destination.Top = source.Top;
			}
			if (!destination.ShouldSerializeBottom() && source.ShouldSerializeBottom())
			{
				destination.Bottom = source.Bottom;
			}
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableCollectionConverter))]
	public abstract class MdiTabsBaseCollection : KeyedSubObjectsCollectionBase, ISerializable, IDeserializationCallback
	{
		public class MdiTabEnumerator : DisposableObjectEnumeratorBase
		{
			public MdiTab Current => ((IEnumerator)this).Current as MdiTab;

			public MdiTabEnumerator(MdiTabsBaseCollection collection)
				: base(collection)
			{
			}
		}

		private int initialCapacity = 4;

		internal IList InternalTabList => List;

		public MdiTab this[int index] => GetItem(index) as MdiTab;

		public MdiTab this[string key] => GetItem(key) as MdiTab;

		public override bool AllowDuplicateKeys => true;

		public override bool IsReadOnly => true;

		protected override int InitialCapacity => initialCapacity;

		protected MdiTabsBaseCollection()
		{
		}

		protected MdiTabsBaseCollection(int initialCapacity)
		{
			this.initialCapacity = initialCapacity;
		}

		protected MdiTabsBaseCollection(MdiTab[] tabs)
			: this()
		{
			if (tabs == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_25"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_26"));
			}
			foreach (MdiTab mdiTab in tabs)
			{
				if (mdiTab == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_17"));
				}
			}
			AddRange(tabs);
		}

		protected MdiTabsBaseCollection(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			SerializationInfoEnumerator enumerator = info.GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				SerializationEntry current = enumerator.Current;
				if (current.Value is MdiTab)
				{
					MdiTab mdiTab = (MdiTab)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(MdiTab), null);
					if (mdiTab != null)
					{
						InternalAdd(mdiTab);
					}
					continue;
				}
				string name = current.Name;
				if (!(name == "Count"))
				{
					if (name == "Tag")
					{
						DeserializeTag(current);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					initialCapacity = Math.Max(initialCapacity, (int)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(int), initialCapacity));
				}
			}
		}

		public bool Contains(MdiTab tab)
		{
			return Contains((IKeyedSubObject)tab);
		}

		public MdiTabEnumerator GetEnumerator()
		{
			return new MdiTabEnumerator(this);
		}

		protected override object[] CreateArray()
		{
			return new MdiTab[Count];
		}

		protected override void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChange)
		{
			if (propChange.Source is MdiTab)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MdiTab, propChange);
			}
			base.OnSubObjectPropChanged(propChange);
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.SerializationFormatter)]
		protected virtual void GetObjectData(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnDeserialization(object sender)
		{
		}

		internal void Add(MdiTab tab)
		{
			Insert(Count, tab);
		}

		internal void AddRange(MdiTab[] tabs)
		{
			InsertRange(Count, tabs);
		}

		internal void Insert(int index, MdiTab tab)
		{
			tab.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			if (index < 0)
			{
				index = 0;
			}
			else if (index > Count)
			{
				index = Count;
			}
			InternalInsert(index, tab);
			NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MdiTab);
		}

		internal void InsertRange(int index, MdiTab[] tabs)
		{
			foreach (MdiTab mdiTab in tabs)
			{
			}
			if (index < 0)
			{
				index = 0;
			}
			else if (index > Count)
			{
				index = Count;
			}
			foreach (MdiTab mdiTab2 in tabs)
			{
				mdiTab2.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				InternalInsert(index, mdiTab2);
				index++;
			}
			NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MdiTab);
		}

		internal void Remove(MdiTab tab)
		{
			tab.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			InternalRemove(tab);
			NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MdiTab);
		}

		internal void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			Remove(this[index]);
		}

		internal void Clear()
		{
			MdiTabEnumerator mdiTabEnumerator = GetEnumerator();
			try
			{
				while (mdiTabEnumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					MdiTab current = mdiTabEnumerator.Current;
					current.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				IDisposable disposable = mdiTabEnumerator as IDisposable;
				if (disposable != null)
				{
					disposable.Dispose();
				}
			}
			InternalClear();
			NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MdiTab);
		}

		internal void Reposition(MdiTab tab, int newIndex)
		{
			int num = IndexOf(tab);
			if (num != -1)
			{
				List.RemoveAt(num);
				List.Insert(newIndex, tab);
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.MdiTab);
			}
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.SerializationFormatter)]
		void ISerializable.GetObjectData(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "Count", Count);
			for (int i = 0; i < Count; i++)
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, i.ToString(), this[i]);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTag())
			{
				SerializeTag(info);
			}
			GetObjectData(info, context);
		}

		void IDeserializationCallback.OnDeserialization(object sender)
		{
			MdiTabEnumerator mdiTabEnumerator = GetEnumerator();
			try
			{
				while (mdiTabEnumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					MdiTab current = mdiTabEnumerator.Current;
					current.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				IDisposable disposable = mdiTabEnumerator as IDisposable;
				if (disposable != null)
				{
					disposable.Dispose();
				}
			}
			OnDeserialization(sender);
		}
	}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public UltraTabbedMdiManager Manager
		{
			get
			{
				if (owner is UltraTabbedMdiManager)
				{
					return owner as UltraTabbedMdiManager;
				}
				if (owner is MdiTabGroup)
				{
					return ((MdiTabGroup)owner).Manager;
				}
				if (owner is MdiTab)
				{
					MdiTab mdiTab = owner as MdiTab;
					if (mdiTab.TabGroup != null)
					{
						return mdiTab.TabGroup.Manager;
					}
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		protected MdiTabSettings(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			//IL_02be: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02c8: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_03aa: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_03b9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_03be: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_030a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0314: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_02e4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02ee: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_03d2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_03e1: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_03e6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0289: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0298: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_029d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0217: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0221: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0379: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0388: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_038d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0233: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0242: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0247: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			SerializationInfoEnumerator enumerator = info.GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				SerializationEntry current = enumerator.Current;
				switch (current.Name)
				{
				case "ActiveTabAppearance":
					activeTabAppearanceHolder = (AppearanceHolder)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(AppearanceHolder), activeTabAppearanceHolder);
					break;
				case "AllowClose":
					allowClose = (DefaultableBoolean)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(DefaultableBoolean), allowClose);
					break;
				case "AllowDrag":
					allowDrag = (MdiTabDragStyle)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(MdiTabDragStyle), allowDrag);
					break;
				case "HotTrack":
					hotTrack = (DefaultableBoolean)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(DefaultableBoolean), hotTrack);
					break;
				case "HotTrackTabAppearance":
					hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder = (AppearanceHolder)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(AppearanceHolder), hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder);
					break;
				case "SelectedTabAppearance":
					selectedTabAppearanceHolder = (AppearanceHolder)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(AppearanceHolder), selectedTabAppearanceHolder);
					break;
				case "TabAppearance":
					tabAppearanceHolder = (AppearanceHolder)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(AppearanceHolder), tabAppearanceHolder);
					break;
				case "TabCloseAction":
					tabCloseAction = (MdiTabCloseAction)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(MdiTabCloseAction), tabCloseAction);
					break;
				case "TabWidth":
					tabWidth = (int)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(int), tabWidth);
					break;
				case "DisplayFormIcon":
					displayFormIcon = (DefaultableBoolean)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(DefaultableBoolean), displayFormIcon);
					break;
				case "Tag":
					DeserializeTag(current);
					break;
				case "CloseButtonAlignment":
					closeButtonAlignment = (TabCloseButtonAlignment)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(TabCloseButtonAlignment), closeButtonAlignment);
					break;
				case "CloseButtonVisibility":
					closeButtonVisibility = (TabCloseButtonVisibility)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(TabCloseButtonVisibility), closeButtonVisibility);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void InitializeOwner(object owner)
		{

				_ = this.owner;
				this.owner = owner;

		internal void InitializeFrom(MdiTabSettings source)
		{
			//IL_00e0: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00f8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00fd: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_011c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0121: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0145: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_014a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0151: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0156: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			Reset();
			if (source != null)
			{
				if (source.HasActiveTabAppearance)
				{
					AppearanceBase activeTabAppearance = ActiveTabAppearance;
					activeTabAppearanceHolder.InitializeFrom(source.activeTabAppearanceHolder);
				}
				else if (HasActiveTabAppearance)
				{
					activeTabAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				}
				if (source.HasHotTrackTabAppearance)
				{
					AppearanceBase hotTrackTabAppearance = HotTrackTabAppearance;
					hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder.InitializeFrom(source.hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder);
				}
				else if (HasHotTrackTabAppearance)
				{
					hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				}
				if (source.HasSelectedTabAppearance)
				{
					AppearanceBase selectedTabAppearance = SelectedTabAppearance;
					selectedTabAppearanceHolder.InitializeFrom(source.selectedTabAppearanceHolder);
				}
				else if (HasSelectedTabAppearance)
				{
					selectedTabAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				}
				if (source.HasTabAppearance)
				{
					AppearanceBase tabAppearance = TabAppearance;
					tabAppearanceHolder.InitializeFrom(source.tabAppearanceHolder);
				}
				else if (HasTabAppearance)
				{
					tabAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				}
				allowClose = source.AllowClose;
				allowDrag = source.AllowDrag;
				hotTrack = source.HotTrack;
				tabWidth = source.TabWidth;
				tabCloseAction = source.TabCloseAction;
				displayFormIcon = source.displayFormIcon;
				if (source.ShouldSerializeTag())
				{
					tagValue = source.Tag;
				}
				else
				{
					tagValue = null;
				}
				closeButtonAlignment = source.closeButtonAlignment;
				closeButtonVisibility = source.closeButtonVisibility;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChange)
		{
			if (HasActiveTabAppearance && propChange.Source == activeTabAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ActiveTabAppearance, propChange);
			}
			else if (HasHotTrackTabAppearance && propChange.Source == hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.HotTrackTabAppearance, propChange);
			}
			else if (HasTabAppearance && propChange.Source == tabAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabAppearance, propChange);
			}
			else if (HasSelectedTabAppearance && propChange.Source == selectedTabAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.SelectedTabAppearance, propChange);
			}
			base.OnSubObjectPropChanged(propChange);
		}

		protected override void OnDispose()
		{
			owner = null;
			if (activeTabAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				activeTabAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				activeTabAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				activeTabAppearanceHolder = null;
			}
			if (hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder = null;
			}
			if (tabAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				tabAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				tabAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				tabAppearanceHolder = null;
			}
			if (selectedTabAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				selectedTabAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				selectedTabAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				selectedTabAppearanceHolder = null;
			}
			base.Dispose();
		}

		protected virtual void GetObjectData(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnDeserialization(object sender)
		{
		}

		void IDeserializationCallback.OnDeserialization(object sender)
		{
			if (activeTabAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				activeTabAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			}
			if (hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			}
			if (tabAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				tabAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			}
			if (selectedTabAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				selectedTabAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			}
			OnDeserialization(sender);
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.SerializationFormatter)]
		void ISerializable.GetObjectData(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			//IL_0028: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0064: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0082: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0136: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0154: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (ShouldSerializeActiveTabAppearance())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "ActiveTabAppearance", activeTabAppearanceHolder);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeAllowClose())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "AllowClose", allowClose);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeAllowDrag())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "AllowDrag", allowDrag);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeDisplayFormIcon())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "DisplayFormIcon", displayFormIcon);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeHotTrack())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "HotTrack", hotTrack);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeHotTrackTabAppearance())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "HotTrackTabAppearance", hotTrackTabAppearanceHolder);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeSelectedTabAppearance())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "SelectedTabAppearance", selectedTabAppearanceHolder);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTabAppearance())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "TabAppearance", tabAppearanceHolder);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTabCloseAction())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "TabCloseAction", tabCloseAction);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTabWidth())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "TabWidth", tabWidth);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTag())
			{
				SerializeTag(info);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeCloseButtonAlignment())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "CloseButtonAlignment", closeButtonAlignment);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeCloseButtonVisibility())
			{
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "CloseButtonVisibility", closeButtonVisibility);
			}
			GetObjectData(info, context);
		}

	public class MdiTabSettingsResolved
	{

		public bool AllowClose
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_002a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0030: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				//IL_0058: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_005e: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				//IL_008c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0092: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				if (tab.HasSettings && tab.Settings.ShouldSerializeAllowClose())
				{
					return (int)tab.Settings.AllowClose == 1;
				}
				MdiTabGroup tabGroup = TabGroup;
				if (tabGroup != null && tabGroup.HasTabSettings && tabGroup.TabSettings.ShouldSerializeAllowClose())
				{
					return (int)tabGroup.TabSettings.AllowClose == 1;
				}
				UltraTabbedMdiManager ultraTabbedMdiManager = tabGroup?.Manager;
				if (ultraTabbedMdiManager != null && ultraTabbedMdiManager.HasTabSettings && ultraTabbedMdiManager.TabSettings.ShouldSerializeAllowClose())
				{
					return (int)ultraTabbedMdiManager.TabSettings.AllowClose == 1;
				}
				if (tab.IsCustomTab)
				{
					return tab.CustomMdiTab.AllowClose;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public MdiTabDragStyle AllowDrag
		{
			get
			{
				if (tab.HasSettings && tab.Settings.ShouldSerializeAllowDrag())
				{
					return tab.Settings.AllowDrag;
				}
				MdiTabGroup tabGroup = TabGroup;
				if (tabGroup != null && tabGroup.HasTabSettings && tabGroup.TabSettings.ShouldSerializeAllowDrag())
				{
					return tabGroup.TabSettings.AllowDrag;
				}
				UltraTabbedMdiManager ultraTabbedMdiManager = tabGroup?.Manager;
				if (ultraTabbedMdiManager != null && ultraTabbedMdiManager.HasTabSettings && ultraTabbedMdiManager.TabSettings.ShouldSerializeAllowDrag())
				{
					return ultraTabbedMdiManager.TabSettings.AllowDrag;
				}
				if (ultraTabbedMdiManager != null && ultraTabbedMdiManager.IsActiveTabGroupMaximized)
				{
					return MdiTabDragStyle.WithinGroup;
				}
				return MdiTabDragStyle.WithinAndAcrossGroups;
			}
		}

		public MdiTabCloseAction TabCloseAction
		{
			get
			{
				if (tab.IsCustomTab)

				if (tabGroup != null && tabGroup.HasTabSettings && tabGroup.TabSettings.ShouldSerializeTabCloseAction())
				{
					return tabGroup.TabSettings.TabCloseAction;

				if (ultraTabbedMdiManager != null && ultraTabbedMdiManager.HasTabSettings && ultraTabbedMdiManager.TabSettings.ShouldSerializeTabCloseAction())

					return ultraTabbedMdiManager.TabSettings.TabCloseAction;
				}
				return MdiTabCloseAction.Close;
			}
		}

				return ultraTabbedMdiManager.TabSettings.CloseButtonVisibility;
			}
		}

		public MdiTabSettingsResolved(MdiTab tab)
		{
			if (tab == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_28"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_29"));
			}
			this.tab = tab;
		}
	}

		internal struct RECT
		{
			public int left;

			public int top;

			public int right;

			public int bottom;

			public Rectangle Rect => new Rectangle(left, top, right - left, bottom - top);

			public RECT(int left, int top, int right, int bottom)
			{
				this.left = left;
				this.top = top;
				this.right = right;
				this.bottom = bottom;
			}

			public static RECT FromXYWH(int x, int y, int width, int height)
			{
				return new RECT(x, y, x + width, y + height);
			}

			public static RECT FromRectangle(Rectangle rect)
			{
				return new RECT(rect.Left, rect.Top, rect.Right, rect.Bottom);
			}
		}

		private static IntPtr PatternBrush
		{
			get
			{
				if (patternBrush == IntPtr.Zero)
				{
					Application.ApplicationExit += OnApplicationExit;
					IntPtr intPtr = CreateBitmap(8, 8, 1, 1, new short[8] { 85, 170, 85, 170, 85, 170, 85, 170 });
					patternBrush = CreatePatternBrush(intPtr);
					DeleteObject(intPtr);
				}
				return patternBrush;
			}
		}

		{

			{
				return SetWindowLong32(hwnd, nIndex, dwNewLong);
			}
			return SetWindowLongPtr64(hwnd, nIndex, dwNewLong);
		}

		internal static void DrawReversibleFrame(Rectangle rect)
		{
			DrawReversibleFrame(rect, Rectangle.Empty);
		}

		internal static void DrawReversibleFrame(Rectangle rect, Rectangle previousRect)
		{
			int width = SystemInformation.FrameBorderSize.Width;
			DrawReversibleFrame(rect, width, previousRect);
		}

		internal static void DrawReversibleFrame(Rectangle rect, int width, Rectangle previousRect)
		{
			if (width == 0 || rect == previousRect)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				if (!previousRect.IsEmpty)
				{
					DrawReversibleFrameAPI(previousRect, width);
				}
				if (!rect.IsEmpty)
				{
					DrawReversibleFrameAPI(rect, width);
				}
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				if (!previousRect.IsEmpty)
				{
					Rectangle[] rects = GetRects(previousRect, width);
					Rectangle[] array = rects;
					foreach (Rectangle rectangle in array)
					{
						ControlPaint.FillReversibleRectangle(rectangle, SystemColors.Control);
					}
				}
				if (!rect.IsEmpty)
				{
					Rectangle[] rects2 = GetRects(rect, width);
					Rectangle[] array2 = rects2;
					foreach (Rectangle rectangle2 in array2)
					{
						ControlPaint.FillReversibleRectangle(rectangle2, SystemColors.Control);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal static IntPtr SetFocusApi(IntPtr hWnd)
		{
			return SetFocus(hWnd);
		}

		internal static bool SetForegroundWindowApi(IntPtr hWnd)
		{
			return SetForegroundWindow(hWnd);
		}

		internal static void SetRedraw(Control control, bool newValue, bool redraw)
		{
			if (control == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				IntPtr handle = control.Handle;
				SendMessageApi(handle, 11, newValue ? new IntPtr(1) : IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero);
				if (newValue && redraw)
				{
					RedrawWindowApi(handle, IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero, 1671u);
				}
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
		}

		private static bool SetWindowPosApi(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr hWndInsertAfter, int x, int y, int cx, int cy, int flags)
		{
			return SetWindowPos(hWnd, hWndInsertAfter, x, y, cx, cy, flags);
		}

		internal static void ShowNoActivate(Form form, Point location, bool topmost)
		{
			ShowNoActivate(form, new Rectangle(location, form.Size), topmost);
		}

		internal static void ShowNoActivate(Form form, Rectangle rect, bool topmost)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!form.IsHandleCreated)
				{
					form.CreateControl();
				}
				SetWindowPosApi(form.Handle, topmost ? HWND_TOPMOST : IntPtr.Zero, rect.X, rect.Y, rect.Width, rect.Height, 80);
				form.Visible = true;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				form.StartPosition = FormStartPosition.Manual;
				form.TopMost = topmost;
				form.Bounds = rect;
				form.Show();
				Form owner = Utilities.GetOwner(form);
				if (owner != null)
				{
					Utilities.FocusControl(owner);
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void UpdateBorderStyle(IntPtr hwnd, BorderStyle borderStyle)
		{
			if (hwnd == IntPtr.Zero)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				UpdateBorderStyleAPI(hwnd, borderStyle);
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
		}

		internal static Rectangle[] GetRects(Rectangle rect, int width)
		{
			if (rect.Width <= width * 2 || rect.Height <= width * 2)
			{
				return new Rectangle[1] { rect };
			}
			return new Rectangle[4]
			{
				new Rectangle(rect.X, rect.Y, rect.Width, width),
				new Rectangle(rect.Right - width, rect.Y + width, width, rect.Height - width * 2),
				new Rectangle(rect.X, rect.Bottom - width, rect.Width, width),
				new Rectangle(rect.X, rect.Y + width, width, rect.Height - width * 2)
			};
		}

		private static bool DrawReversibleFrameAPI(Rectangle[] rects)
		{
			if (rects == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			IntPtr zero = IntPtr.Zero;
			IntPtr hObject = IntPtr.Zero;
			IntPtr dC = GetDC(zero);
			if (dC == IntPtr.Zero)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
			}
			try
			{
				hObject = SelectObject(dC, PatternBrush);
				for (int i = 0; i < rects.Length; i++)
				{
					Rectangle rectangle = rects[i];
					PatBlt(dC, rectangle.X, rectangle.Y, rectangle.Width, rectangle.Height, 5898313);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				if (ex is OutOfMemoryException)
				{
					throw;
				}
				return false;
			}
			finally
			{
				SelectObject(dC, hObject);
				ReleaseDC(zero, dC);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static void DrawReversibleFrameAPI(Rectangle rect, int width)
		{
			Rectangle[] rects = GetRects(rect, width);
			DrawReversibleFrameAPI(rects);
		}

		private static void OnApplicationExit(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (patternBrush != IntPtr.Zero)
			{
				DeleteObject(patternBrush);
			}
		}

		internal static IntPtr SendMessageApi(IntPtr hWnd, int msg, IntPtr wparam, IntPtr lparam)
		{
			return SendMessage(hWnd, msg, wparam, lparam);
		}

		internal static bool RedrawWindowApi(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr rectUpdate, IntPtr hrgnUpdate, uint flags)
		{
			return RedrawWindow(hWnd, rectUpdate, hrgnUpdate, flags);
		}

		private static void AdjustStylesForBorder(ref IntPtr currentStyle, ref IntPtr currentExStyle, BorderStyle borderStyle)
		{
			switch (borderStyle)
			{
			case BorderStyle.None:
				currentExStyle = RemoveBits(currentExStyle, 512);
				currentStyle = RemoveBits(currentStyle, 8388608);
				break;
			case BorderStyle.Fixed3D:
				currentExStyle = AddBits(currentExStyle, 512);
				currentStyle = RemoveBits(currentStyle, 8388608);
				break;
			case BorderStyle.FixedSingle:
				currentExStyle = RemoveBits(currentExStyle, 512);
				currentStyle = AddBits(currentStyle, 8388608);
				break;
			}
		}

		private static void UpdateBorderStyleAPI(IntPtr handle, BorderStyle borderStyle)
		{
			IntPtr currentStyle = GetWindowLong(handle, -16);
			IntPtr currentExStyle = GetWindowLong(handle, -20);
			IntPtr intPtr = currentStyle;
			IntPtr intPtr2 = currentExStyle;
			AdjustStylesForBorder(ref currentStyle, ref currentExStyle, borderStyle);
			if (intPtr != currentStyle)
			{
				SetWindowLong(handle, -16, currentStyle);
			}
			if (intPtr2 != currentExStyle)
			{
				SetWindowLong(handle, -20, currentExStyle);
			}
			SetWindowPos(handle, IntPtr.Zero, 0, 0, 0, 0, SetWindowPosEnum.SWP_NOSIZE | SetWindowPosEnum.SWP_NOMOVE | SetWindowPosEnum.SWP_NOZORDER | SetWindowPosEnum.SWP_NOACTIVATE | SetWindowPosEnum.SWP_FRAMECHANGED | SetWindowPosEnum.SWP_NOOWNERZORDER);
		}

		internal static IntPtr AddBits(IntPtr ptr, int bitsToRemove)
		{
			if (IntPtr.Size == 4)
			{
				return new IntPtr(ptr.ToInt32() | bitsToRemove);
			}
			return new IntPtr(ptr.ToInt64() | (uint)bitsToRemove);
		}

		internal static IntPtr RemoveBits(IntPtr ptr, int bitsToRemove)
		{
			if (IntPtr.Size == 4)
			{
				return new IntPtr(ptr.ToInt32() & ~bitsToRemove);
			}
			return new IntPtr(ptr.ToInt64() & ~bitsToRemove);
		}
	}

	public enum SplitterBarEventIds

		SplitterDragging,
		SplitterMove,
		SplitterDragged
	}
	public class SplitterMoveEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		private Rectangle splitterRect = Rectangle.Empty;

		private Cursor dragCursor;

		private Point mousePosition = Point.Empty;

		private Point originalMousePosition = Point.Empty;

		public Rectangle SplitRectangle
		{
			get
			{
				return splitterRect;
			}
			set
			{
				splitterRect = value;
			}
		}

		public Cursor DragCursor
		{
			get
			{
				return dragCursor;
			}
			set
			{
				dragCursor = value;
			}
		}

		public Point MousePosition => mousePosition;

		public Point OriginalMousePosition => originalMousePosition;

		public SplitterMoveEventArgs(Rectangle splitterRect, Point mousePosition, Point originalMousePosition)
		{
			this.splitterRect = splitterRect;
			this.mousePosition = mousePosition;
			this.originalMousePosition = originalMousePosition;
		}
	}
	public class SplitterDraggedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		private Point mousePosition = Point.Empty;

		private Point originalMousePosition = Point.Empty;

		private bool cancelled;

		public bool Cancelled => cancelled;

		public Point MousePosition => mousePosition;

		public Point OriginalMousePosition => originalMousePosition;

		public SplitterDraggedEventArgs(Point mousePosition, Point originalMousePosition, bool cancelled)
		{
			this.mousePosition = mousePosition;
			this.cancelled = cancelled;
			this.originalMousePosition = originalMousePosition;
		}
	}

			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_SplitterBarControl_P_Orientation")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Behavior")]
		[DefaultValue(Orientation.Vertical)]
		public Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return orientation;
			}
			set
			{
				if (orientation != value)
				{
					if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(Orientation), value))
					{
						throw new InvalidEnumArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_8"), (int)value, typeof(Orientation));
					}
					orientation = value;
					DestroyCursor();
					((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)SplitterBarPropertyIds.Orientation);
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_SplitterBarControl_P_BorderStyle")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public UIElementBorderStyle BorderStyle
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				return borderStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_003f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_002c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_003c: Expected I4, but got Unknown
				if (borderStyle != value)
				{
					if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(UIElementBorderStyle), value))
					{
						throw new InvalidEnumArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_8"), (int)value, typeof(UIElementBorderStyle));
					}
					borderStyle = value;
					DirtyChildElements();
					((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)SplitterBarPropertyIds.BorderStyle);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override ControlUIElementBase ControlUIElement
		{
			get
			{
				if (controlUIElement == null)
				{
					if (ultraControl != null)
					{
						controlUIElement = new SplitterControlUIElement(this, ultraControl);
					}
					else
					{
						controlUIElement = new SplitterControlUIElement(this);
					}
				}
				return (ControlUIElementBase)(object)controlUIElement;
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Visible)]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_SplitterBarControl_P_Appearance")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		public AppearanceBase Appearance
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0013: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (appearanceHolder == null)
				{
					appearanceHolder = new AppearanceHolder();
					appearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged += SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				}
				return appearanceHolder.Appearance;
			}
			set
			{
				//IL_002c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0036: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (appearanceHolder == null || !appearanceHolder.HasAppearance || value != appearanceHolder.Appearance)
				{
					if (appearanceHolder == null)
					{
						appearanceHolder = new AppearanceHolder();
						appearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged += SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					}
					appearanceHolder.Appearance = value;
					((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)SplitterBarPropertyIds.Appearance);
				}
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public bool HasAppearance
		{
			get

				return false;
			}
		}

		}

					cursorStream = typeFromHandle.Assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(typeFromHandle, name);

					((UltraControlBase)this).Invalidate();
				}
				((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)SplitterBarPropertyIds.Appearance, propChangeInfo);

		}

		internal void ResolveAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			if (ResolveAppearanceCallback != null)
			{
				ResolveAppearanceCallback.Invoke(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
			if ((long)requestedProps == 0L)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (HasAppearance)
			{
				Appearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
			if ((long)requestedProps != 0L)
			{
				if (Utilities.ShouldSetAppearanceProperty(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (AppearancePropFlags)1))
				{
					((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColor = SystemColors.Control;
					requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEuL);
				}
				if (Utilities.ShouldSetAppearanceProperty(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (AppearancePropFlags)1073741824))
				{
					((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BorderColor3DBase = ((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColor;
					requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFBFFFFFFFuL);
				}
				if (Utilities.ShouldSetAppearanceProperty(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (AppearancePropFlags)4))
				{
					((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BorderColor = DrawUtility.Dark(((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColor);
					requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFBuL);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs pe)
		{
			//IL_0052: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0057: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0078: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (((UltraControlBase)this).IsUpdating)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (ultraControl != null)
			{
				IUltraControl obj = ultraControl;
				UltraComponentControlManagerBase val = (UltraComponentControlManagerBase)(object)((obj is UltraComponentControlManagerBase) ? obj : null);
				IUltraControl obj2 = ultraControl;
				UltraControlBase val2 = (UltraControlBase)(object)((obj2 is UltraControlBase) ? obj2 : null);
				if (val != null || val2 != null)
				{
					if ((val == null || !val.IsUpdating) && (val2 == null || !val2.IsUpdating))
					{
						AlphaBlendMode val3 = ((val != null) ? val.AlphaBlendMode : val2.AlphaBlendMode);
						((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).Draw(pe.Graphics, pe.ClipRectangle, !((Control)this).GetStyle(ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer), val3);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			((UltraControlBase)this).OnPaint(pe);
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				if (IsDragging)
				{
					EndDrag(cancel: true);
				}
				DestroyCursor();
			}
			((UltraControlBase)this).Dispose(disposing);
		}

		private void DestroyCursor()
		{
			if (currentCursor != null)
			{
				currentCursor.Dispose();
				currentCursor = null;
			}
			if (cursorStream != null)
			{
				cursorStream.Close();
				cursorStream = null;
			}
		}

		internal void RemoveIntersections()
		{
			if (HasIntersections)
			{
				((UltraControlBase)this).Invalidate();
				IntersectionList.Clear();
			}
		}

		internal void AddIntersection(Rectangle siblingSplitterRect)
		{
			Rectangle rectangle = Rectangle.Intersect(siblingSplitterRect, ((Control)this).Bounds);
			rectangle.Offset(-((Control)this).Left, -((Control)this).Top);
			IntersectionList.Add(rectangle);
			((UltraControlBase)this).Invalidate(rectangle);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSplitterDragging(CancelEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_30"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_31"));
			}
			((CancelEventHandler)((Component)this).Events[EventSplitterDragging])?.Invoke(this, e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSplitterDragged(SplitterDraggedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_30"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_32"));
			}
			((SplitterDraggedEventHandler)((Component)this).Events[EventSplitterDragged])?.Invoke(this, e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSplitterMove(SplitterMoveEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_30"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_32"));
			}
			((SplitterMoveEventHandler)((Component)this).Events[EventSplitterMove])?.Invoke(this, e);
		}

		internal void InvokeEvent(SplitterBarEventIds eventId, EventArgs e)
		{
			switch (eventId)
			{
			case SplitterBarEventIds.SplitterDragged:
				OnSplitterDragged(e as SplitterDraggedEventArgs);
				break;
			case SplitterBarEventIds.SplitterMove:
				OnSplitterMove(e as SplitterMoveEventArgs);
				break;
			case SplitterBarEventIds.SplitterDragging:
				OnSplitterDragging(e as CancelEventArgs);
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public class SplitterControlUIElement : ControlUIElementBase, IMessageFilter
	{
		private class RectangleComparer : IComparer
		{
			int IComparer.Compare(object x, object y)
			{
				Rectangle rectangle = (Rectangle)x;
				Rectangle rectangle2 = (Rectangle)y;
				if (rectangle.X < rectangle2.X)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (rectangle.X > rectangle2.X)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				if (rectangle.Y < rectangle2.Y)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (rectangle.Y > rectangle2.Y)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				return 0;
			}
		}

		private Cursor lastDragCursor;

		private Point offset = Point.Empty;

		private Point lastPoint = Point.Empty;

		private Point startPoint = Point.Empty;

		private bool isDragging;

		private bool messageFilterAdded;

		private DragDropIndicatorManager dragDropIndicatorManager;

		private Bitmap dragPreviewBitmap;

		public override UIElementBorderStyle BorderStyle
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				if (SplitterControl != null)
				{
					return SplitterControl.BorderStyle;
				}
				return ((UIElement)this).BorderStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				((UIElement)this).BorderStyle = value;
			}
		}

		public override Border3DSide BorderSides
		{
			get
			{
				if (SplitterControl.SuppressLeadingEdge)
				{
					if (IsVertical)
					{
						return Border3DSide.Right;
					}
					return Border3DSide.Bottom;
				}
				if (IsVertical)
				{
					return Border3DSide.Left | Border3DSide.Right;
				}
				return Border3DSide.Top | Border3DSide.Bottom;
			}
		}

		public override bool Adjustable => ((UIElement)this).Enabled;

		public SplitterBarControl SplitterControl => ((UIElement)this).Control as SplitterBarControl;

		internal bool IsDragging => isDragging;

		private bool IsVertical => SplitterControl.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical;

		public override UIRole UIRole => StyleUtils.GetRole(((ControlUIElementBase)this).UltraControl, IsVertical ? StyleUtils.Role.SplitterBarVert : StyleUtils.Role.SplitterBarHorz);

		public SplitterControlUIElement(SplitterBarControl splitterBarControl)
			: this(splitterBarControl, (IUltraControl)(object)splitterBarControl)
		{
		}

		public SplitterControlUIElement(SplitterBarControl splitterBarControl, IUltraControl ultraControl)
			: base((Control)(object)splitterBarControl, ultraControl)
		{
		}

		protected override void DrawBorders(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0013: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0016: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003c: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			if (SplitterControl.HasIntersections)
			{
				UIElementBorderStyle borderStyle = ((UIElement)this).BorderStyle;
				switch (borderStyle - 2)
				{
				case 3:
				case 4:
				case 5:
				case 6:
				case 7:
					DrawComplexSplitterBorders(ref drawParams);
					return;
				case 0:
				case 1:
				case 2:
					DrawSimpleIntersectedSplitterBorders(ref drawParams);
					return;
				}
			}
			DrawSimpleSplitterBorders(ref drawParams);
		}

		public override Cursor GetAdjustableCursor(Point point)
		{
			if (isDragging && lastDragCursor != null)
			{
				return lastDragCursor;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public override bool PointInAdjustableArea(Point point)
		{
			return ((UIElement)this).Rect.Contains(point);
		}

		protected override bool OnMouseDown(MouseEventArgs e, bool adjustableArea, ref UIElement captureMouseForElement)
		{
			if (e.Button == MouseButtons.Left && InitiateDrag(new Point(e.X, e.Y), initializeCursorPosition: false))
			{
				captureMouseForElement = (UIElement)(object)this;
				return true;
			}
			return ((UIElement)this).OnMouseDown(e, adjustableArea, ref captureMouseForElement);
		}

		protected override void OnCaptureAborted()
		{
			((ControlUIElementBase)this).OnCaptureAborted();
			ProcessEndDrag(cancelled: true);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (((UIElement)this).HasCapture)
			{
				ProcessDragPoint(new Point(e.X, e.Y));
			}
			((UIElement)this).OnMouseMove(e);
		}

		protected override bool OnMouseUp(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (isDragging)
			{
				ProcessEndDrag(cancelled: false);
			}
			return ((UIElement)this).OnMouseUp(e);
		}

		protected override void InitAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			SplitterControl.ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			if (Utilities.ShouldSetAppearanceProperty(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (AppearancePropFlags)137438953472L))
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).Cursor = SplitterControl.CurrentSplitterCursor;
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFDFFFFFFFFFuL);
			}
			((ControlUIElementBase)this).InitAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
		}

		private bool InitiateDrag(Point clientPt, bool initializeCursorPosition)
		{
			//IL_00d8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e2: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (isDragging)
			{
				return false;
			}
			CancelEventArgs e = new CancelEventArgs();
			SplitterControl.InvokeEvent(SplitterBarEventIds.SplitterDragging, e);
			if (e.Cancel)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (initializeCursorPosition)
			{
				Rectangle rect = ((UIElement)this).Rect;
				offset = new Point(rect.Width / 2, rect.Height / 2);
				Cursor.Position = ((UIElement)this).Control.PointToScreen(clientPt);
			}
			else
			{
				Rectangle rect2 = ((UIElement)this).Rect;
				offset = new Point(clientPt.X - rect2.X, clientPt.Y - rect2.Y);
			}
			startPoint = ((UIElement)this).Control.PointToScreen(clientPt);
			((UIElement)this).Control.Update();
			dragDropIndicatorManager = new DragDropIndicatorManager(Point.Empty, (UIElement[])(object)new UIElement[1] { (UIElement)this }, SplitterControl.Orientation, ((UIElement)this).Control, 1.0);
			Bitmap bitmap = CreateDragPreviewImage();
			dragDropIndicatorManager.InitializeDragIndicator((Image)bitmap);
			ProcessDragPoint(clientPt);
			isDragging = true;
			HookMessageFilter();
			return true;
		}

		private void HookMessageFilter()
		{
			if (messageFilterAdded)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
				if (Utilities.CanAssert)
				{
					securityPermission.Assert();
				}
				Utilities.AddMessageFilter((IMessageFilter)this);
				messageFilterAdded = true;
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
		}

		private void UnhookMessageFilter()
		{
			if (!messageFilterAdded)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
				if (Utilities.CanAssert)
				{
					securityPermission.Assert();
				}
				Utilities.RemoveMessageFilter((IMessageFilter)this);
				messageFilterAdded = false;
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
		}

		private void ProcessDragPoint(Point clientPt)
		{
			Point point = ((UIElement)this).Control.PointToScreen(clientPt);
			if (!(point == lastPoint))
			{
				Rectangle splitterRect = ((UIElement)this).Control.RectangleToScreen(((UIElement)this).Rect);
				if (IsVertical)
				{
					splitterRect.X += clientPt.X - offset.X;
				}
				else
				{
					splitterRect.Y += clientPt.Y - offset.Y;
				}
				SplitterMoveEventArgs e = new SplitterMoveEventArgs(splitterRect, point, startPoint);
				SplitterControl.InvokeEvent(SplitterBarEventIds.SplitterMove, e);
				lastDragCursor = e.DragCursor;
				dragDropIndicatorManager.ShowDragIndicator(e.SplitRectangle.Location);
				lastPoint = point;
			}
		}

		private void ProcessEndDrag(bool cancelled)
		{
			if (isDragging)
			{
				isDragging = false;
				UnhookMessageFilter();
				lastDragCursor = null;
				DisposeDragResources();
				((UIElement)this).DirtyChildElements();
				SplitterDraggedEventArgs e = new SplitterDraggedEventArgs(lastPoint, startPoint, cancelled);
				SplitterControl.InvokeEvent(SplitterBarEventIds.SplitterDragged, e);
			}
		}

		private void DrawSimpleSplitterBorders(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			//IL_0003: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0091: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			Region region = null;
			UIElementBorderStyle borderStyle = ((UIElement)this).BorderStyle;
			if (SplitterControl.HasIntersections)
			{
				region = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).Graphics.Clip;
				foreach (Rectangle intersection in SplitterControl.IntersectionList)
				{
					((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).Graphics.ExcludeClip(intersection);
				}
			}
			try
			{
				Rectangle rect2 = ((UIElement)this).Rect;
				if (IsVertical)
				{
					rect2.Inflate(0, 2);
				}
				else
				{
					rect2.Inflate(2, 0);
				}
				((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).DrawBorders(borderStyle, ((UIElement)this).BorderSides, rect2);
			}
			finally
			{
				if (region != null)
				{
					((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).Graphics.Clip = region;
					region.Dispose();
				}
			}
		}

		private void DrawSimpleIntersectedSplitterBorders(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			//IL_007c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0081: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0083: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0086: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0098: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			Rectangle[] array = ((!SplitterControl.HasIntersections) ? null : ((Rectangle[])SplitterControl.IntersectionList.ToArray(typeof(Rectangle))));
			Array.Sort(array, new RectangleComparer());
			Color color = ((AppearanceData)(ref drawParams.AppearanceData)).BorderColor;
			if (color.IsEmpty)
			{
				color = ((AppearanceData)(ref drawParams.AppearanceData)).ForeColor;
			}
			Graphics graphics = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).Graphics;
			Rectangle rect = ((UIElement)this).Rect;
			bool flag = SplitterControl.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical;
			UIElementBorderStyle borderStyle = ((UIElement)this).BorderStyle;
			Pen pen;
			switch (borderStyle - 2)
			{
			default:
				return;
			case 0:
				pen = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)2, color);
				break;
			case 1:
				pen = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)1, color);
				break;
			case 2:
				pen = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)0, color);
				break;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
			{
				Rectangle rectangle = rect;
				int num = ((!flag) ? ((i == 0) ? rectangle.Top : rectangle.Bottom) : ((i == 0) ? rectangle.Left : rectangle.Right));
				Rectangle[] array2 = array;
				for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
				{
					Rectangle rectangle2 = array2[j];
					int num2 = ((!flag) ? ((i == 0) ? rectangle2.Top : rectangle2.Bottom) : ((i == 0) ? rectangle2.Left : rectangle2.Right));
					if (num2 == num)
					{
						Rectangle rect2 = rectangle;
						if (flag)
						{
							rect2.Height = rectangle2.Top - rectangle.Top;
							rectangle.Height -= rect2.Height + rectangle2.Height;
							rectangle.Y = rectangle2.Bottom;
						}
						else
						{
							rect2.Width = rectangle2.Left - rectangle.Left;
							rectangle.Width -= rect2.Width + rectangle2.Width;
							rectangle.X = rectangle2.Right;
						}
						DrawBorderLineHelper(graphics, rect2, pen, null, i == 0, flag);
					}
				}
				DrawBorderLineHelper(graphics, rectangle, pen, null, i == 0, flag);
			}
		}

		private void DrawComplexSplitterBorders(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			//IL_00e8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ed: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ef: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00f2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_010c: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			Rectangle[] array = ((!SplitterControl.HasIntersections) ? null : ((Rectangle[])SplitterControl.IntersectionList.ToArray(typeof(Rectangle))));
			Array.Sort(array, new RectangleComparer());
			Color color = ((AppearanceData)(ref drawParams.AppearanceData)).BorderColor3DBase;
			if (color.IsEmpty)
			{
				color = ((AppearanceData)(ref drawParams.AppearanceData)).BackColor;
			}
			Graphics graphics = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).Graphics;
			Pen pen2;
			Pen pen3;
			Pen pen = (pen2 = (pen3 = null));
			Pen pen4;
			Pen pen5;
			Pen pen6;
			Pen pen7;
			if (color == SystemColors.Control)
			{
				pen4 = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)10, color);
				pen5 = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)9, color);
				pen6 = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)11, color);
				pen7 = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)8, color);
			}
			else
			{
				pen4 = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)5, color);
				pen5 = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)4, color);
				pen6 = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)5, color);
				pen7 = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)4, color);
			}
			Rectangle rect = ((UIElement)this).Rect;
			bool flag = SplitterControl.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical;
			UIElementBorderStyle borderStyle = ((UIElement)this).BorderStyle;
			Pen pen8;
			switch (borderStyle - 5)
			{
			default:
				return;
			case 4:
				pen8 = pen4;
				pen = pen5;
				pen2 = pen5;
				pen3 = pen4;
				break;
			case 0:
				pen8 = pen4;
				pen = pen5;
				pen2 = pen6;
				pen3 = pen7;
				break;
			case 2:
				pen8 = pen4;
				pen = pen5;
				break;
			case 1:
				pen8 = pen7;
				pen = pen6;
				pen2 = pen5;
				pen3 = pen4;
				break;
			case 3:
				pen8 = pen5;
				pen = pen4;
				break;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
			{
				Rectangle rectangle = rect;
				int num = ((!flag) ? ((i == 0) ? rectangle.Top : rectangle.Bottom) : ((i == 0) ? rectangle.Left : rectangle.Right));
				Rectangle[] array2 = array;
				for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
				{
					Rectangle rectangle2 = array2[j];
					int num2 = ((!flag) ? ((i == 0) ? rectangle2.Top : rectangle2.Bottom) : ((i == 0) ? rectangle2.Left : rectangle2.Right));
					if (num2 == num)
					{
						Rectangle rect2 = rectangle;
						if (flag)
						{
							rect2.Height = rectangle2.Top - rectangle.Top;
							rectangle.Height -= rect2.Height + rectangle2.Height;
							rectangle.Y = rectangle2.Bottom;
						}
						else
						{
							rect2.Width = rectangle2.Left - rectangle.Left;
							rectangle.Width -= rect2.Width + rectangle2.Width;
							rectangle.X = rectangle2.Right;
						}
						DrawBorderLineHelper(graphics, rect2, (i == 0) ? pen8 : pen, (i == 0) ? pen2 : pen3, i == 0, flag);
					}
				}
				DrawBorderLineHelper(graphics, rectangle, (i == 0) ? pen8 : pen, (i == 0) ? pen2 : pen3, i == 0, flag);
			}
			Rectangle[] array3 = array;
			for (int k = 0; k < array3.Length; k++)
			{
				Rectangle intersection = array3[k];
				if ((flag && intersection.Left == rect.Left) || (!flag && intersection.Top == rect.Top))
				{
					DrawCornerHelper(graphics, (Corners)1, intersection, pen8, pen2, pen, pen3);
				}
				if ((flag && intersection.Right == rect.Right) || (!flag && intersection.Bottom == rect.Bottom))
				{
					DrawCornerHelper(graphics, (Corners)8, intersection, pen8, pen2, pen, pen3);
				}
				if ((flag && intersection.Left == rect.Left) || (!flag && intersection.Bottom == rect.Bottom))
				{
					DrawCornerHelper(graphics, (Corners)4, intersection, pen8, pen2, pen, pen3);
				}
				if ((flag && intersection.Right == rect.Right) || (!flag && intersection.Top == rect.Top))
				{
					DrawCornerHelper(graphics, (Corners)2, intersection, pen8, pen2, pen, pen3);
				}
			}
		}

		private void DrawCornerHelper(Graphics g, Corners corner, Rectangle intersection, Pen outerLeftTopPen, Pen innerLeftTopPen, Pen outerRightBottomPen, Pen innerRightBottomPen)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0004: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0084: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0086: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0088: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0111: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0113: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0115: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_019a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_019c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_019e: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if ((corner & 1) == 1)
			{
				g.DrawLine(outerLeftTopPen, intersection.X - 1, intersection.Top, intersection.X, intersection.Top);
				if (innerLeftTopPen != null)
				{
					g.DrawLine(innerLeftTopPen, intersection.X + 1, intersection.Top - 1, intersection.X + 1, intersection.Top);
					g.DrawLine(innerLeftTopPen, intersection.X, intersection.Top + 1, intersection.X + 1, intersection.Top + 1);
				}
			}
			if ((corner & 8) == 8)
			{
				g.DrawLine(outerRightBottomPen, intersection.Right - 1, intersection.Bottom - 1, intersection.Right, intersection.Bottom - 1);
				if (innerRightBottomPen != null)
				{
					g.DrawLine(innerRightBottomPen, intersection.Right - 2, intersection.Bottom - 1, intersection.Right - 2, intersection.Bottom);
					g.DrawLine(innerRightBottomPen, intersection.Right - 2, intersection.Bottom - 2, intersection.Right - 1, intersection.Bottom - 2);
				}
			}
			if ((corner & 4) == 4)
			{
				g.DrawLine(outerLeftTopPen, intersection.X, intersection.Bottom - 1, intersection.X, intersection.Bottom);
				if (innerRightBottomPen != null)
				{
					g.DrawLine(innerRightBottomPen, intersection.X - 1, intersection.Bottom - 2, intersection.X, intersection.Bottom - 2);
					g.DrawLine(innerLeftTopPen, intersection.X + 1, intersection.Bottom - 2, intersection.X + 1, intersection.Bottom - 1);
				}
			}
			if ((corner & 2) == 2)
			{
				g.DrawLine(outerLeftTopPen, intersection.Right - 1, intersection.Top, intersection.Right, intersection.Top);
				if (innerRightBottomPen != null)
				{
					g.DrawLine(innerLeftTopPen, intersection.Right - 2, intersection.Top + 1, intersection.Right, intersection.Top + 1);
					g.DrawLine(innerRightBottomPen, intersection.Right - 2, intersection.Top - 1, intersection.Right - 2, intersection.Top);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void DrawBorderLineHelper(Graphics g, Rectangle rect, Pen outerPen, Pen innerPen, bool near, bool isVertical)
		{
			Point location = rect.Location;
			Point pt = new Point(rect.Right - 1, rect.Bottom - 1);
			if (isVertical && near)
			{
				pt.X = location.X;
			}
			else if (isVertical && !near)
			{
				location.X = pt.X;
			}
			else if (!isVertical && near)
			{
				pt.Y = location.Y;
			}
			else
			{
				location.Y = pt.Y;
			}
			g.DrawLine(outerPen, location, pt);
			if (innerPen != null)
			{
				if (isVertical)
				{
					int x = (pt.X = location.X + (near ? 1 : (-1)));
					location.X = x;
				}
				else
				{
					int x = (pt.Y = location.Y + (near ? 1 : (-1)));
					location.Y = x;
				}
				g.DrawLine(innerPen, location, pt);
			}
		}

		private Bitmap CreateDragPreviewImage()
		{
			DisposeDragPreviewImage();
			Rectangle rect = ((UIElement)this).Rect;
			dragPreviewBitmap = new Bitmap(rect.Size.Width, rect.Size.Height);
			using (Graphics graphics = Graphics.FromImage(dragPreviewBitmap))
			{
				using HatchBrush brush = new HatchBrush(HatchStyle.Percent50, SystemColors.Window);
				graphics.FillRectangle(brush, rect);
			}
			return dragPreviewBitmap;
		}

		private void DisposeDragPreviewImage()
		{
			if (dragPreviewBitmap != null)
			{
				dragPreviewBitmap.Dispose();
			}
		}

		private void DisposeDragDropIndicatorManager()
		{
			if (dragDropIndicatorManager != null)
			{
				dragDropIndicatorManager.HideDragIndicator();
				((DisposableObject)(object)dragDropIndicatorManager).Dispose();
				dragDropIndicatorManager = null;
			}
		}

		private void DisposeDragResources()
		{
			DisposeDragDropIndicatorManager();
			DisposeDragPreviewImage();
		}

		internal void StartDrag(Point pt)
		{
			Point clientPt = ((UIElement)this).Control.PointToClient(pt);
			if (InitiateDrag(clientPt, initializeCursorPosition: true))
			{
				((ControlUIElementBase)this).CaptureMouseForElement((UIElement)(object)this);
			}
		}

		internal void EndDrag(bool cancel)
		{
			ProcessEndDrag(cancel);
			if (((UIElement)this).HasCapture)
			{
				((UIElement)this).ControlElement.TerminateCapture();
			}
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode)]
		bool IMessageFilter.PreFilterMessage(ref Message m)
		{
			if (!isDragging)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (m.Msg >= 256 && m.Msg <= 264)
			{
				if (m.Msg == 256 && m.WParam.ToInt64() == 27)
				{
					EndDrag(cancel: true);
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	internal class TabGroupInfoManager
	{
		private Point mdiClientLocation = Point.Empty;

		private bool mdiClientLocationDirty = true;

		private bool tabGroupCollectionDirty = true;

		private HybridDictionary tabGroupInfos;

		private UltraTabbedMdiManager manager;

		internal const int DefaultTabGroupPadding = 2;

		internal const UIElementBorderStyle TabGroupControlBorderStyle = (UIElementBorderStyle)8;

		private HybridDictionary TabGroupInfos
		{
			get
			{
				if (tabGroupInfos == null)
				{
					tabGroupInfos = new HybridDictionary(3);
				}
				return tabGroupInfos;
			}
		}

		private Point MdiClientLocation
		{
			get
			{
				if (mdiClientLocationDirty)
				{
					mdiClientLocation = manager.MdiClientLocation;
				}
				return mdiClientLocation;
			}
		}

		internal TabGroupInfoManager(UltraTabbedMdiManager manager)
		{
			this.manager = manager;
		}

		private void VerifyMetrics()
		{
			if (tabGroupCollectionDirty)
			{
				tabGroupCollectionDirty = false;
				ResyncTabGroupCollection();
				RecalculateTabGroupInfos();
				return;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in manager.TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
			{
				TabGroupInfo tabGroupInfo = TabGroupInfos[nestedTabGroup] as TabGroupInfo;
				if (tabGroupInfo.Dirty)
				{
					RecalculateTabGroupInfo(nestedTabGroup);
				}
			}
		}

		private void VerifyTabGroupMetrics(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (tabGroupInfos == null || !TabGroupInfos.Contains(tabGroup))
			{
				ResyncTabGroupCollection();
			}
			TabGroupInfo tabGroupInfo = TabGroupInfos[tabGroup] as TabGroupInfo;
			if (tabGroupInfo.Dirty)
			{
				RecalculateTabGroupInfo(tabGroup);
			}
		}

		private bool ResyncTabGroupCollection()
		{
			if (!IsResyncNeeded())
			{
				return false;
			}
			TabGroupInfo[] array = null;
			if (tabGroupInfos != null && tabGroupInfos.Count > 0)
			{
				array = new TabGroupInfo[TabGroupInfos.Count];
				TabGroupInfos.Values.CopyTo(array, 0);
				TabGroupInfos.Clear();
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in manager.TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
			{
				TabGroupInfo tabGroupInfo = null;
				if (array != null && num < array.Length)
				{
					tabGroupInfo = array[num];
					tabGroupInfo.Dirty = true;
					num++;
				}
				else
				{
					tabGroupInfo = new TabGroupInfo();
				}
				TabGroupInfos.Add(nestedTabGroup, tabGroupInfo);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool IsResyncNeeded()
		{
			int num = ((tabGroupInfos != null) ? tabGroupInfos.Count : 0);
			int num2 = (manager.HasTabGroups ? manager.TabGroups.Count : 0);
			if (num2 != num)
			{
				return true;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in manager.TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
			{
				if (!TabGroupInfos.Contains(nestedTabGroup))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void RecalculateTabGroupInfos()
		{
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in manager.TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
			{
				RecalculateTabGroupInfo(nestedTabGroup);
			}
		}

		private void RecalculateTabGroupInfo(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			//IL_0032: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0038: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00dc: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0165: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_017a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0113: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_011d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			TabGroupInfo tabGroupInfo = TabGroupInfos[tabGroup] as TabGroupInfo;
			bool flag = IsInVerticalGroup(tabGroup);
			int num = manager.CalculateCommonExtent(tabGroup.Parent);
			TabOrientation tabOrientation = tabGroup.SettingsResolved.TabOrientation;
			bool flag2 = IsVerticalTabOrientation(tabOrientation);
			int tabRowHeight = ((!tabGroup.HasTabGroups) ? ((!flag2) ? tabGroup.TabManager.TabAreaSize.Height : tabGroup.TabManager.TabAreaSize.Width) : 0);
			int extent = tabGroup.Extent;
			Point relativeTabGroupOrigin = GetRelativeTabGroupOrigin(tabGroup);
			Size size = ((!flag) ? new Size(num, extent) : new Size(extent, num));
			Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle(relativeTabGroupOrigin, size);
			bool flag3 = NeedsSplitter(tabGroup);
			int splitterWidth = (flag3 ? manager.SplitterWidthResolved : 0);
			Rectangle splitterRectangle = Rectangle.Empty;
			Rectangle tabGroupArea = rectangle;
			bool flag4 = AllocateSplitterRectBeforeTabGroup(tabOrientation, flag, manager);
			Rectangle tabGroupControlRectangle;
			if (flag)
			{
				if (flag3 && flag4)
				{
					splitterRectangle = CalcSplitterRect(splitterWidth, ref tabGroupArea, flag);
				}
				Size tabGroupControlSize = CalcTabGroupControlSize(tabRowHeight, tabGroupArea.Width, tabGroupArea.Height, tabOrientation, flag);
				tabGroupControlRectangle = CalcTabGroupControlRectangle(tabOrientation, tabGroupControlSize, ref tabGroupArea);
				if (flag3 && !flag4)
				{
					splitterRectangle = CalcSplitterRect(splitterWidth, ref tabGroupArea, flag);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (flag3 && flag4)
				{
					splitterRectangle = CalcSplitterRect(splitterWidth, ref tabGroupArea, flag);
				}
				Size tabGroupSize = CalcTabGroupControlSize(tabRowHeight, tabGroupArea.Height, tabGroupArea.Width, tabOrientation, flag);
				tabGroupSize = RemoveTabGroupBorder(tabGroup, tabGroupSize);
				tabGroupControlRectangle = CalcTabGroupControlRectangle(tabOrientation, tabGroupSize, ref tabGroupArea);
				if (flag3 && !flag4)
				{
					splitterRectangle = CalcSplitterRect(splitterWidth, ref tabGroupArea, flag);
				}
			}
			Rectangle formRectangle = tabGroupArea;
			tabGroupInfo.FormRectangle = formRectangle;
			tabGroupInfo.SplitterRectangle = splitterRectangle;
			tabGroupInfo.TabGroupControlRectangle = tabGroupControlRectangle;
			tabGroupInfo.TabGroupArea = rectangle;
			tabGroupInfo.Dirty = false;
		}

		private static bool AllocateSplitterRectBeforeTabGroup(TabOrientation tabOrientation, bool isVerticalGroup, UltraTabbedMdiManager manager)
		{
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003a: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			if (manager.AllowNestedTabGroupsResolved || manager.HasNestedTabGroups)
			{
				return true;
			}
			switch (tabOrientation - 1)
			{
			default:
				return !isVerticalGroup;
			case 4:
			case 5:
			case 6:
			case 7:
				return isVerticalGroup;
			}
		}

		private Point GetRelativeTabGroupOrigin(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			Point empty = Point.Empty;
			MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = tabGroup;
			do
			{
				bool flag = IsInVerticalGroup(mdiTabGroup);
				foreach (MdiTabGroup item in mdiTabGroup.ParentCollection)
				{
					if (item == mdiTabGroup)
					{
						break;
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						empty.X += item.Extent;
					}
					else
					{
						empty.Y += item.Extent;
					}
				}
				mdiTabGroup = mdiTabGroup.Parent;
			}
			while (mdiTabGroup != null);
			return empty;
		}

		private Point GetActualTabGroupOrigin(Point relativeOrigin)
		{
			Point result = MdiClientLocation;
			result.Offset(relativeOrigin.X, relativeOrigin.Y);
			return result;
		}

		private Size CalcTabGroupControlSize(int tabRowHeight, int tabGroupExtent, int commonExtent, TabOrientation tabOrientation, bool isVerticalTabGroup)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0003: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0029: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			switch (tabOrientation - 1)
			{
			default:
				if (isVerticalTabGroup)
				{
					return new Size(tabGroupExtent, tabRowHeight);
				}
				return new Size(commonExtent, tabRowHeight);
			case 4:
			case 5:
			case 6:
			case 7:
				if (isVerticalTabGroup)
				{
					return new Size(tabRowHeight, commonExtent);
				}
				return new Size(tabRowHeight, tabGroupExtent);
			}
		}

		private Rectangle CalcTabGroupControlRectangle(TabOrientation tabOrientation, Size tabGroupControlSize, ref Rectangle tabGroupArea)
		{
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002f: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			Rectangle rectangle = tabGroupArea;
			switch (tabOrientation - 1)
			{
			default:
				tabGroupArea.Height -= tabGroupControlSize.Height;
				return new Rectangle(rectangle.Left, rectangle.Bottom - tabGroupControlSize.Height, rectangle.Width, tabGroupControlSize.Height);
			case 0:
			case 1:
				tabGroupArea.Y += tabGroupControlSize.Height;
				tabGroupArea.Height -= tabGroupControlSize.Height;
				return new Rectangle(rectangle.Left, rectangle.Top, rectangle.Width, tabGroupControlSize.Height);
			case 4:
			case 5:
				tabGroupArea.X += tabGroupControlSize.Width;
				tabGroupArea.Width -= tabGroupControlSize.Width;
				return new Rectangle(rectangle.Left, rectangle.Top, tabGroupControlSize.Width, rectangle.Height);
			case 6:
			case 7:
				tabGroupArea.Width -= tabGroupControlSize.Width;
				return new Rectangle(rectangle.Right - tabGroupControlSize.Width, rectangle.Top, tabGroupControlSize.Width, rectangle.Height);
			}
		}

		private Rectangle CalcSplitterRect(int splitterWidth, ref Rectangle tabGroupArea, bool isVerticalTabGroup)
		{
			if (splitterWidth == 0)
			{
				return Rectangle.Empty;
			}
			Rectangle rectangle = tabGroupArea;
			if (isVerticalTabGroup)
			{
				if (tabGroupArea.Width == 0)
				{
					return Rectangle.Empty;
				}
				tabGroupArea.Width -= splitterWidth;
				return new Rectangle(rectangle.Right - splitterWidth, rectangle.Top, splitterWidth, rectangle.Height);
			}
			if (tabGroupArea.Height == 0)
			{
				return Rectangle.Empty;
			}
			tabGroupArea.Height -= splitterWidth;
			return new Rectangle(rectangle.Left, rectangle.Bottom - splitterWidth, rectangle.Width, splitterWidth);
		}

		private bool IsInVerticalGroup(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			Orientation orientation = tabGroup?.ParentOrientation ?? manager.Orientation;
			return orientation == Orientation.Vertical;
		}

		internal void DirtyMdiClientLocation()
		{
			mdiClientLocationDirty = true;
		}

		internal void DirtyTabGroupExtent()
		{
			DirtyAllTabGroups();
		}

		internal void DirtyAllTabGroups()
		{
			tabGroupCollectionDirty = true;
			foreach (TabGroupInfo value in TabGroupInfos.Values)
			{
				value.Dirty = true;
			}
		}

		internal void DirtyTabGroup(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (tabGroupInfos == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!tabGroupInfos.Contains(tabGroup))
			{
				DirtyAllTabGroups();
				return;
			}
			TabGroupInfo tabGroupInfo = TabGroupInfos[tabGroup] as TabGroupInfo;
			tabGroupInfo.Dirty = true;
			if (!tabGroup.HasTabGroups)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup2 in tabGroup.TabGroups)
			{
				DirtyTabGroup(tabGroup2);
			}
		}

		internal Size CalcMaxAvailableTabGroupSize(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			return CalcMaxAvailableTabGroupSize(tabGroup, manager.CalculateCommonExtent(tabGroup.Parent));
		}

		internal Size CalcMaxAvailableTabGroupSize(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, int commonExtent)
		{
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			int extent = tabGroup.Extent;
			TabOrientation tabOrientation = tabGroup.SettingsResolved.TabOrientation;
			bool flag = IsInVerticalGroup(tabGroup);
			Size tabGroupSize = ((!flag) ? new Size(commonExtent, extent) : new Size(extent, commonExtent));
			if (NeedsSplitter(tabGroup) && AllocateSplitterRectBeforeTabGroup(tabOrientation, flag, manager))
			{
				int splitterWidthResolved = manager.SplitterWidthResolved;
				if (flag)
				{
					tabGroupSize.Width = Math.Max(0, tabGroupSize.Width - splitterWidthResolved);
				}
				else
				{
					tabGroupSize.Height = Math.Max(0, tabGroupSize.Height - splitterWidthResolved);
				}
			}
			return RemoveTabGroupBorder(tabGroup, tabGroupSize);
		}

		private Size RemoveTabGroupBorder(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, Size tabGroupSize)
		{
			//IL_000a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			Border3DSide tabGroupBorderSide = GetTabGroupBorderSide(tabGroup);
			UIElementBorderWidths val = DrawUtility.CalculateBorderWidths((UIElementBorderStyle)8, tabGroupBorderSide);
			tabGroupSize.Width -= ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref val)).Left + ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref val)).Right;
			tabGroupSize.Height -= ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref val)).Top + ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref val)).Bottom;
			return tabGroupSize;
		}

		internal bool NeedsSplitter(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (manager.SplitterWidthResolved == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!manager.HasTabGroups)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (manager.MaximizedTabGroup != null && manager.MaximizedTabGroupDisplayStyle == MaximizedMdiTabGroupDisplayStyle.ShowMaximizedGroupOnly)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return tabGroup.ParentCollection[tabGroup.ParentCollection.Count - 1] != tabGroup;
		}

		internal Rectangle GetTabGroupControlRectangle(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			VerifyTabGroupMetrics(tabGroup);
			TabGroupInfo tabGroupInfo = TabGroupInfos[tabGroup] as TabGroupInfo;
			Rectangle tabGroupControlRectangle = tabGroupInfo.TabGroupControlRectangle;
			tabGroupControlRectangle.Offset(MdiClientLocation);
			return tabGroupControlRectangle;
		}

		internal Rectangle GetSplitterControlRectangle(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			VerifyTabGroupMetrics(tabGroup);
			TabGroupInfo tabGroupInfo = TabGroupInfos[tabGroup] as TabGroupInfo;
			Rectangle splitterRectangle = tabGroupInfo.SplitterRectangle;
			splitterRectangle.Offset(MdiClientLocation);
			return splitterRectangle;
		}

		internal Rectangle GetFormPosition(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			VerifyTabGroupMetrics(tabGroup);
			TabGroupInfo tabGroupInfo = TabGroupInfos[tabGroup] as TabGroupInfo;
			return tabGroupInfo.FormRectangle;
		}

		internal static bool IsVerticalTabOrientation(TabOrientation tabOrientation)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0018: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			switch (tabOrientation - 5)
			{
			case 0:
			case 1:
			case 2:
			case 3:
				return true;
			default:
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal Rectangle GetTabGroupArea(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			VerifyTabGroupMetrics(tabGroup);
			TabGroupInfo tabGroupInfo = TabGroupInfos[tabGroup] as TabGroupInfo;
			Rectangle tabGroupArea = tabGroupInfo.TabGroupArea;
			tabGroupArea.Offset(MdiClientLocation);
			return tabGroupArea;
		}

		internal Border3DSide GetTabGroupBorderSide(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0074: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (tabGroup == null)
			{
				return (Border3DSide)0;
			}
			TabOrientation tabOrientation = tabGroup.SettingsResolved.TabOrientation;
			bool flag = IsInVerticalGroup(tabGroup);
			if (NeedsSplitter(tabGroup) && AllocateSplitterRectBeforeTabGroup(tabOrientation, flag, manager))
			{
				return (Border3DSide)0;
			}
			if (manager.IsActiveTabGroupMaximized)
			{
				return (Border3DSide)0;
			}
			if (tabGroup.ParentCollection == null || tabGroup.ParentCollection.Count == 0 || tabGroup.ParentCollection[tabGroup.ParentCollection.Count - 1] == tabGroup)
			{
				return (Border3DSide)0;
			}
			bool flag2 = Utilities.IsVerticalTabOrientation(tabOrientation);
			if (flag && !flag2)
			{
				return Border3DSide.Right;
			}
			if (!flag && flag2)
			{
				return Border3DSide.Bottom;
			}
			return (Border3DSide)0;
		}

		internal Rectangle GetTabGroupDropRect(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			Rectangle formPosition = GetFormPosition(tabGroup);
			formPosition.Offset(MdiClientLocation);
			if (!NeedsSplitter(tabGroup))
			{
				return formPosition;
			}
			if (formPosition.IsEmpty)
			{
				return GetSplitterControlRectangle(tabGroup);
			}
			if (AllocateSplitterRectBeforeTabGroup(tabGroup.SettingsResolved.TabOrientation, IsInVerticalGroup(tabGroup), manager))
			{
				return formPosition;
			}
			return Rectangle.Union(formPosition, GetSplitterControlRectangle(tabGroup));
		}

		internal Rectangle GetTabGroupFormRect(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			Rectangle formPosition = GetFormPosition(tabGroup);
			formPosition.Offset(MdiClientLocation);
			return formPosition;
		}

		internal void RemoveUnusedTabGroups()
		{
			ResyncTabGroupCollection();
		}
	}

		}

		{
		}
	}
