		private class TabGroupExtentInfo
		{
			private MdiTabGroup tabGroup;

			private int newExtent;

			internal MdiTabGroup TabGroup => tabGroup;

			internal int NewExtent
			{
				get
				{
					return newExtent;
				}
				set
				{
					newExtent = value;
				}
			}

			internal bool IsExtentDifferent => tabGroup.Extent != newExtent;

			internal TabGroupExtentInfo(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, int newExtent)
			{
				this.tabGroup = tabGroup;
				this.newExtent = newExtent;
			}
		}

		private class TabGroupExtentInfoCollection : ICollection, IEnumerable
		{
			private ArrayList list;

			public int Count => list.Count;

			bool ICollection.IsSynchronized => list.IsSynchronized;

			object ICollection.SyncRoot => list.SyncRoot;

			internal TabGroupExtentInfo this[int index] => list[index] as TabGroupExtentInfo;

			internal TabGroupExtentInfo this[MdiTabGroup tabGroup]
			{
				get
				{
					foreach (TabGroupExtentInfo item in list)
					{
						if (item.TabGroup == tabGroup)
						{
							return item;
						}
					}
					return null;
				}
			}

			internal TabGroupExtentInfoCollection()
				: this(5)
			{
			}

			internal TabGroupExtentInfoCollection(int capacity)
			{
				list = new ArrayList(capacity);
			}

			internal TabGroupExtentInfo Add(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, int newExtent)
			{
				TabGroupExtentInfo tabGroupExtentInfo = new TabGroupExtentInfo(tabGroup, newExtent);
				list.Add(tabGroupExtentInfo);
				return tabGroupExtentInfo;
			}

			void ICollection.CopyTo(Array array, int index)
			{
				list.CopyTo(array, index);
			}

			IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
			{
				return list.GetEnumerator();
			}

			internal MdiTabGroupResizingEventArgs CreateTabGroupResizingArgs()
			{
				MdiTabGroup[] array = new MdiTabGroup[list.Count];
				int[] array2 = new int[list.Count];
				for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
				{
					TabGroupExtentInfo tabGroupExtentInfo = this[i];
					array[i] = tabGroupExtentInfo.TabGroup;
					array2[i] = tabGroupExtentInfo.NewExtent;
				}
				return new MdiTabGroupResizingEventArgs(new MdiTabGroupsCollection(array), array2);
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public MdiTab ActiveTab
		{
			get
			{
				if (MdiParent == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				Form activeMdiChild = MdiParent.ActiveMdiChild;
				if (activeMdiChild == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				bool createIfNull = !IsInitializing && !IsReleasingTabs && !IsRestoringTabs && !IsDisposing;
				return GetTabFromForm(activeMdiChild, verifyFormIsInitialized: true, verifyTabGroup: false, createIfNull);
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Behavior")]
		public bool ShowToolTips
		{
			get
			{
				return showToolTips;

			set

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public Form MdiParent
		{
			get
			{
				return mdiParent;
			}
			set
			{
				if (mdiParent != value)
				{
					EnsureOnlyOneTabbedMdiManager(value);
					MdiClient mdiClient = this.mdiClient;
					UnhookMdiParent();
					mdiParent = value;
					HookMdiParent();
					if (mdiClient != null && ((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled && !mdiClient.Disposing && !mdiClient.IsDisposed)
					{
						mdiClient.LayoutMdi(MdiLayout.Cascade);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public HiddenMdiTabsCollection HiddenTabs
		{

				if (hiddenTabs == null)

					hiddenTabs = new HiddenMdiTabsCollection();
					hiddenTabs.SubObjectPropChanged += SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				}
				return hiddenTabs;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public bool HasHiddenTabs
		{

					return hiddenTabs.Count > 0;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public bool CanCreateNewGroup
		{
			get
			{
				if (MaxTabGroups != 0)
				{
					return TabGroupsWithTabsCount < MaxTabGroups;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public bool IsDragging
		{
			get
			{
				if (!HasTabGroups)
				{
					return false;
				}
				foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
				{
					if (nestedTabGroup.HasTabManager && nestedTabGroup.TabManager.IsDragging)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal MdiTabGroup ActiveTabGroup
		{
			get
			{
				if (MdiParent == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return ActiveTab?.TabGroup;
			}
		}

		internal MdiTabGroup PreviousActiveTabGroup
		{
			get
			{
				if (lastActiveTabGroup != null && (lastActiveTabGroup.Manager == null || lastActiveTabGroup.Index < 0))
				{
					lastActiveTabGroup = null;
				}
				return lastActiveTabGroup;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsInitializing => isInitializing;

		internal bool IsDisposing => isDisposing;

		internal bool SubclasserActivatingMdiChild
		{
			get
			{
				return isSubclasserActivatingMdiChild;
			}
			set
			{
				isSubclasserActivatingMdiChild = value;
			}
		}

		internal Point MdiClientLocation
		{
			get
			{
				if (mdiClient == null)
				{
					return Point.Empty;
				}
				Point location = mdiClient.Location;
				if (mdiClient.IsHandleCreated && MdiParent != null && MdiParent.IsHandleCreated)
				{
					Point location2 = mdiClient.Location;
					location2 = MdiParent.PointToScreen(location2);
					Point point = mdiClient.PointToClient(location2);
					if (!point.IsEmpty)
					{
						if (MdiParent.Created)
						{
							location.Offset(-point.X, -point.Y);
						}
						else
						{
							location.Offset((mdiClient.Width - mdiClient.ClientSize.Width) / 2, (mdiClient.Height - mdiClient.ClientSize.Height) / 2);
						}
					}
				}
				return location;
			}
		}

		internal int CommonMdiClientExtent
		{
			get
			{
				if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
				{
					return MdiClientSize.Height;
				}
				return MdiClientSize.Width;
			}
		}

		internal TabGroupInfoManager TabGroupManager
		{
			get
			{
				if (tabGroupManager == null)
				{
					tabGroupManager = new TabGroupInfoManager(this);
				}
				return tabGroupManager;
			}
		}

		private Size MdiClientSize
		{
			get
			{
				if (mdiClient == null)
				{
					return Size.Empty;
				}
				if (MdiParent != null && MdiParent.WindowState == FormWindowState.Minimized)
				{
					return lastMdiClientSize;
				}
				return mdiClient.ClientSize;
			}
		}

		internal Rectangle MdiClientScreenRect
		{
			get
			{
				if (mdiClient == null || !mdiClient.IsHandleCreated)
				{
					return Rectangle.Empty;
				}
				Rectangle clientRectangle = mdiClient.ClientRectangle;
				return mdiClient.RectangleToScreen(clientRectangle);
			}
		}

		internal bool AllowSelectedTabActivation => !ensuringTabIsSelected;

		internal bool IsDesignMode => ((Component)this).DesignMode;

		internal MicrosoftColorTable MSColors => (MicrosoftColorTable)(ViewStyleResolved switch
		{
			ViewStyle.Office2003 => Office2003ColorTable.Colors, 
			ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005 => VisualStudio2005ColorTable.Colors, 
			_ => null, 
		});

		internal bool ThemesActive
		{
			get
			{
				if (((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).UseOsThemesResolved && XPThemes.ThemesSupported && XPThemes.IsThemeActive)
				{
					return XPThemes.AppControlsThemeThemed;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool HasNestedTabGroups
		{
			get
			{
				if (HasTabGroups)
				{
					foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup in TabGroups)
					{
						if (tabGroup.HasTabGroups)
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool CanCreateHorizontalGroup
		{
			get
			{
				if (!CanCreateNewGroup)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!AllowHorizontalTabGroups)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!AllowNestedTabGroupsResolved && Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					return TabGroups.Count == 1;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal bool CanCreateRootHorizontalGroup
		{
			get
			{
				if (CanCreateHorizontalGroup)
				{
					if (!AllowHorizontalTabGroups)
					{
						return Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal;
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool CanCreateVerticalGroup
		{
			get
			{
				if (!CanCreateNewGroup)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!AllowVerticalTabGroups)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!AllowNestedTabGroupsResolved && Orientation != Orientation.Vertical)
				{
					return TabGroups.Count == 1;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal bool CanCreateRootVerticalGroup
		{
			get
			{
				if (CanCreateVerticalGroup)
				{
					if (!AllowVerticalTabGroups)
					{
						return Orientation == Orientation.Vertical;
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal MdiTabGroup MaximizedTabGroup => maximizedTabGroup;

		internal bool IsDraggingInternal => isDraggingTab;

		internal IToolbarsManager ToolbarsManager => toolbarsManager;

		private bool IsMdiClientSubclassed
		{
			get
			{
				if (mdiClientSubclasser == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return mdiClientSubclasser.IsActive;
			}
		}

		private bool IsMdiParentSubclassed
		{
			get
			{
				if (mdiParentSubclasser == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return mdiParentSubclasser.IsActive;
			}
		}

		private bool IsActivatingMdiChild => isActivatingMdiChild;

		private bool IsRestoringTabs => isRestoringTabs;

		private bool IsRestoringTabGroup => restoringTabGroup;

		private bool IsReleasingTabs => isReleasingTabs;

		private bool HasMdiTabs
		{
			get
			{
				if (HasTabGroups)
				{
					foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup in tabGroups)
					{
						if (tabGroup.HasTabsResolved)
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
				return HasHiddenTabs;
			}
		}

		private int AvailableMdiClientExtent
		{
			get
			{
				if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
				{
					return MdiClientSize.Width;
				}
				return MdiClientSize.Height;
			}
		}

		private HybridDictionary TabGroupControls
		{
			get
			{
				if (tabGroupControls == null)
				{
					tabGroupControls = new HybridDictionary(3);
				}
				return tabGroupControls;
			}
		}

		private HybridDictionary SplitterControls
		{
			get
			{
				if (splitterControls == null)
				{
					splitterControls = new HybridDictionary(3);
				}
				return splitterControls;
			}
		}

		private bool IsLayoutSuspended => suspendLayoutRequests > 0;

		private bool IsLayoutSuspendedResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsLayoutSuspended)
				{
					return ((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).IsUpdating;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		private ResolveAppearanceCallback SplitterAppearanceCallbackVert
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001a: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (splitterAppearanceCallbackVert == null)
				{
					splitterAppearanceCallbackVert = new ResolveAppearanceCallback(ResolveSplitterAppearanceVert);
				}
				return splitterAppearanceCallbackVert;
			}

				//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001a: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (splitterAppearanceCallbackHorz == null)
				{
					splitterAppearanceCallbackHorz = new ResolveAppearanceCallback(ResolveSplitterAppearanceHorz);
				}
				return splitterAppearanceCallbackHorz;

		internal ContextMenuManager TabContextMenuManager
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0013: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (tabContextMenuManager == null)
				{
					tabContextMenuManager = new ContextMenuManager();
					tabContextMenuManager.ImageList = ImageList;
					((ContextMenuItemBase)tabContextMenuManager).Popup += OnContextMenuPopup;
				}
				switch (ViewStyleResolved)
				{
				case ViewStyle.Standard:
					tabContextMenuManager.Style = (MenuStyle)2;
					break;
				case ViewStyle.Office2003:
					tabContextMenuManager.Style = (MenuStyle)3;
					break;
				case ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005:
					tabContextMenuManager.Style = (MenuStyle)4;
					break;
				case ViewStyle.Office2007:
					tabContextMenuManager.Style = (MenuStyle)6;
					break;
				}
				return tabContextMenuManager;
			}
		}

		private Bitmap NewHorizontalGroupImage
		{
			get
			{
				if (newTabGroupHorzImage == null)
				{
					Type typeFromHandle = typeof(UltraTabbedMdiManager);
					newTabGroupHorzImgStream = typeFromHandle.Assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(typeFromHandle, "TabGroupHorizontal.bmp");
					newTabGroupHorzImage = Image.FromStream(newTabGroupHorzImgStream) as Bitmap;
				}
				return newTabGroupHorzImage;
			}
		}

		{

					newTabGroupVertImgStream = typeFromHandle.Assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(typeFromHandle, "TabGroupVertical.bmp");
					newTabGroupVertImage = Image.FromStream(newTabGroupVertImgStream) as Bitmap;
				}
				return newTabGroupVertImage;
			}
		}

		private bool PositioningControls => positioningControls;

		private DragDropHelper DragDropHelper
		{
			get
			{
				if (dragHelper == null)
				{
					dragHelper = new DragDropHelper(this);
				}
				return dragHelper;
			}
		}

		private bool WaitingForDelayedRedraw
		{
			get
			{
				if (hookedIdle)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (delayedRedraw != null && !delayedRedraw.IsCompleted)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool SuppressSplitterLeadingEdge
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				if (AllowNestedTabGroupsResolved || HasNestedTabGroups)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (ViewStyleResolved == ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005 && (int)SplitterBorderStyle == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private UltraTabbedMdiManagerApplicationSettings Settings
		{
			get
			{
				if (settings == null)
				{
					settings = new UltraTabbedMdiManagerApplicationSettings((IComponent)this, settingsKey);
				}
				return settings;
			}
		}

		private int TabGroupsWithTabsCount
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (HasTabGroups)
				{
					foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
					{
						if (nestedTabGroup.HasTabs)
						{
							num++;
						}
					}
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		private bool CanSetAllowDrop
		{
			get
			{
				if (!((Component)this).DesignMode)
				{
					return Thread.CurrentThread.GetApartmentState() == ApartmentState.STA;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool AreTouchMetricsEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				Form form = MdiParent;
				if (form == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return TouchProviderManager.AreTouchMetricsEnabled((Control)form);
			}
		}

		internal int SplitterWidthResolved
		{
			get
			{
				int num = SplitterWidth;
				if (AreTouchMetricsEnabled && num > 0)
				{
					return Math.Max(num, TouchUtilities.GetTouchElementSize((Control)mdiClient, (TouchElementSizes)1).Width);
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		private IFormatter CreateSoapFormatter()
		{
			SoapFormatter soapFormatter = new SoapFormatter();
			soapFormatter.AssemblyFormat = FormatterAssemblyStyle.Simple;
			return soapFormatter;
		}

		public void SaveAsXml(Stream stream)
		{
			SaveAsXmlHelper(stream);
		}

		public void SaveAsXml(string filename)
		{
			FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(filename, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write);
			try
			{
				SaveAsXmlHelper(fileStream);
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream.Close();
			}
		}

		private void SaveAsXmlHelper(Stream stream)
		{
			IFormatter formatter = CreateSoapFormatter();
			SaveHelper(stream, formatter);
		}

		public void LoadFromXml(Stream stream)
		{
			LoadFromXmlHelper(stream);
		}

		public void LoadFromXml(string filename)
		{
			FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(filename, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);
			try
			{
				LoadFromXmlHelper(fileStream);
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream.Close();
			}
		}

		private void LoadFromXmlHelper(Stream stream)
		{
			IFormatter formatter = CreateSoapFormatter();
			LoadHelper(stream, formatter);
		}

		private bool LoadComponentSettingsFromXml()
		{
			bool result = false;
			string xmlData = Settings.XmlData;
			if (xmlData != null && xmlData.Length != 0)
			{
				using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
				StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(memoryStream);
				streamWriter.Write(xmlData);
				streamWriter.Flush();
				memoryStream.Position = 0L;
				try
				{
					LoadFromXml(memoryStream);
					result = true;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public UltraTabbedMdiManager()
		{
			try
			{
				license = LicenseManager.Validate(typeof(UltraTabbedMdiManager), this) as UltraLicense;
			}
			catch (FileNotFoundException)
			{
			}
			Office2007ColorTable.ColorSchemeChanged += OnOffice2007ColorSchemeChanged;
		}

					return true;

			{
				return;

				tabGroup.InitializeExtent(0);
			}
			InitializeTabGroupExtents(invokeResizeEvents: true);
		}

		public MdiTabGroup MoveToNewGroup(MdiTab tab)

		public MdiTabGroup MoveToNewGroup(MdiTab tab, MdiTabGroupPosition relativeGroup)
		{
			Orientation tabGroupOrientation = Orientation;
			if (tab.TabGroup != null && tab.TabGroup.Parent != null)
			{
				tabGroupOrientation = tab.TabGroup.TabGroupOrientation;
			}
			return MoveToNewGroup(tab, relativeGroup, tabGroupOrientation);
		}

		public MdiTabGroup MoveToNewGroup(MdiTab tab, MdiTabGroupPosition relativeGroup, Orientation orientation)
		{
			//IL_0052: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0056: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0061: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0070: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (tab == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_28"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_29"));
			}
			MdiTabGroup relativeGroup2 = ((tab.TabGroup != null) ? TabGroupFromPosition(tab.TabGroup, relativeGroup) : null);
			RelativePosition relativePosition;
			switch (relativeGroup)
			{
			case MdiTabGroupPosition.First:
				relativePosition = (RelativePosition)0;
				break;
			default:
				relativePosition = (RelativePosition)1;
				break;
			case MdiTabGroupPosition.Previous:
				relativeGroup2 = tab.TabGroup;
				relativePosition = (RelativePosition)0;
				break;
			case MdiTabGroupPosition.Next:
				relativeGroup2 = tab.TabGroup;
				relativePosition = (RelativePosition)1;
				break;
			}
			return MoveToNewGroup(tab, relativeGroup2, relativePosition, orientation);
		}

		public virtual MdiTabGroup MoveToNewGroup(MdiTab tab, MdiTabGroup relativeGroup, RelativePosition relativePosition)
		{
			//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			Orientation tabGroupOrientation = Orientation;
			if (relativeGroup != null && relativeGroup.Parent != null)
			{
				tabGroupOrientation = relativeGroup.TabGroupOrientation;
			}
			return MoveToNewGroup(tab, relativeGroup, relativePosition, tabGroupOrientation);
		}

		public virtual MdiTabGroup MoveToNewGroup(MdiTab tab, MdiTabGroup relativeGroup, RelativePosition relativePosition, Orientation orientation)
		{
			//IL_00c0: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (tab == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_28"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_29"));
			}
			if (!tab.IsFormVisible)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_35"));
			}
			if (EventManager.InProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.InitializeTab))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_36"));
			}
			if (EventManager.InProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.InitializeTabGroup))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_37"));
			}
			if (EventManager.InProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.RestoreTab))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_64"));
			}
			if (TabGroups.Count == 0)
			{
				if (Orientation != orientation)
				{
					Orientation = orientation;
				}
				AddTabToTabGroup(tab);
			}
			else
			{
				SplitTabGroup(tab, relativeGroup, relativePosition, orientation);
			}
			return tab.TabGroup;
		}

		public bool MoveToGroup(MdiTab tab, MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (tab == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_28"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_29"));
			}
			if (tabGroup == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_13"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_24"));
			}
			if (tabGroup == tab.TabGroup)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return MoveToGroup(tab, tabGroup, tabGroup.Tabs.Count);
		}

		public virtual bool MoveToGroup(MdiTab tab, MdiTabGroup tabGroup, int tabIndex)
		{
			if (tab == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_28"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_29"));
			}
			if (tabGroup == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_13"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_24"));
			}
			if (!tab.IsFormVisible)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_35"));
			}
			if (EventManager.InProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.InitializeTab))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_36"));
			}
			if (EventManager.InProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.InitializeTabGroup))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_37"));
			}
			if (EventManager.InProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.RestoreTab))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_64"));
			}
			if (tabGroup.HasTabGroups)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_66"));
			}
			return MoveTab(tab, tabGroup, tabIndex, invokeDisplayEvents: false, invokeTabMoveEvents: true);
		}

		public bool MoveToGroup(MdiTab tab, MdiTabGroupPosition position)
		{
			if (tab == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_28"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_29"));
			}
			MdiTabGroup tabGroup = TabGroupFromPosition(tab.TabGroup, position);
			return MoveToGroup(tab, tabGroup);
		}

		public bool MoveToGroup(MdiTab tab, MdiTabGroupPosition position, int tabIndex)
		{

			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_28"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_29"));

			MdiTabGroup tabGroup = TabGroupFromPosition(tab.TabGroup, position);
			return MoveToGroup(tab, tabGroup, tabIndex);
		}

		public void LoadFromBinary(Stream stream)
		{
			LoadFromBinaryHelper(stream);
		}

		public void LoadFromBinary(string filename)
		{
			FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(filename, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);
			try
			{
				LoadFromBinaryHelper(fileStream);
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream.Close();
			}
		}

		{
			SaveAsBinaryHelper(stream);
		}

		{
			FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(filename, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write);
			try
			{
				SaveAsBinaryHelper(fileStream);
			}
			finally
			{
				fileStream.Close();
			}
		}

		public MdiTabGroup TabGroupFromPoint(Point point)
		{
			if (mdiClient == null || mdiClient.IsDisposed || mdiClient.Disposing)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Point pt = mdiClient.PointToClient(point);
			if (!mdiClient.ClientRectangle.Contains(pt))
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (MdiParent == null || MdiParent.IsDisposed || MdiParent.Disposing)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Point formPoint = MdiParent.PointToClient(point);
			return TabGroupFromPointHelper(TabGroups, formPoint);
		}

		private MdiTabGroup TabGroupFromPointHelper(MdiTabGroupsCollection tabGroups, Point formPoint)
		{
			foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup in tabGroups)
			{
				if (TabGroupManager.GetTabGroupArea(tabGroup).Contains(formPoint))
				{
					if (tabGroup.HasTabGroups)
					{
						MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = TabGroupFromPointHelper(tabGroup.TabGroups, formPoint);
						return (mdiTabGroup != null) ? mdiTabGroup : tabGroup;
					}
					return tabGroup;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public MdiTab TabFromPoint(Point point)
		{
			MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = TabGroupFromPoint(point);
			if (mdiTabGroup == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (!(TabGroupControls[mdiTabGroup] is MdiTabGroupControl { IsDisposed: false, Disposing: false } mdiTabGroupControl))
			{
				return null;
			}
			Point point2 = ((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).PointToClient(point);
			UIElement mainUIElement = (UIElement)(object)((IUltraControlElement)mdiTabGroupControl).MainUIElement;
			if (mainUIElement == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			UIElement val = mainUIElement.ElementFromPoint(point2);
			if (val != null)
			{
				return val.GetContext(typeof(MdiTab), true) as MdiTab;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public MdiTab TabFromForm(Form form)
		{
			return GetTabFromForm(form, verifyFormIsInitialized: true, verifyTabGroup: true, createIfNull: true);
		}

		public MdiTab TabFromKey(string key)
		{
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
			{
				foreach (MdiTab tab in nestedTabGroup.Tabs)
				{
					if (tab.Key == key)
					{
						return tab;
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (MdiTab hiddenTab in HiddenTabs)
			{
				if (hiddenTab.Key == key)
				{
					return hiddenTab;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public MdiTab[] TabsFromKey(string key)
		{
			ArrayList arrayList = null;
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
			{
				foreach (MdiTab tab in nestedTabGroup.Tabs)
				{
					if (tab.Key == key)
					{
						if (arrayList == null)
						{
							arrayList = new ArrayList();
						}
						arrayList.Add(tab);
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (MdiTab hiddenTab in HiddenTabs)
			{
				if (hiddenTab.Key == key)
				{
					if (arrayList == null)
					{
						arrayList = new ArrayList();
					}
					arrayList.Add(hiddenTab);
				}
			}
			if (arrayList == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			arrayList.TrimToSize();
			return (MdiTab[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(MdiTab));
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			ResetComponent(deserializing: false);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			if (HasAppearances && propChangeInfo.Source == appearances)
			{
				((UltraComponentBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Appearances, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (HasTabSettings && propChangeInfo.Source == tabSettings)
			{
				DirtyTabGroups(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabSettings, propChangeInfo);
				((UltraComponentBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabSettings, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (HasTabGroupSettings && propChangeInfo.Source == tabGroupSettings)
			{
				StyleUtils.DirtyCachedPropertyVals(this);
				DirtyTabGroups(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabGroupSettings, propChangeInfo);
				((UltraComponentBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabGroupSettings, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (TabGroups == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				((UltraComponentBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)TabbedMdiPropertyIds.TabGroups, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (HasAppearance && propChangeInfo.Source == appearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				DirtyTabGroups(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Appearance, propChangeInfo);
				((UltraComponentBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Appearance, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (HasSplitterAppearance && propChangeInfo.Source == splitterAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				((UltraComponentBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)TabbedMdiPropertyIds.SplitterAppearance, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (propChangeInfo.Source == hiddenTabs)
			{
				((UltraComponentBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)TabbedMdiPropertyIds.HiddenTabs, propChangeInfo);
			}
		}

		protected virtual MdiTab CreateMdiTab(Form form)
		{
			if (form == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new MdiTab(form);
		}

		}

		protected virtual SplitterBarControl CreateSplitterBarControl()
		{
			SplitterBarControl splitterBarControl = new SplitterBarControl((IUltraControl)(object)this);
			splitterBarControl.SuppressLeadingEdge = SuppressSplitterLeadingEdge;
			return splitterBarControl;
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				isDisposing = true;
				if (saveSettings)
				{
					SaveComponentSettings();
				}
				UnsubclassMdiClient();
				UnhookMdiParent();
				if (appearanceHolder != null)
				{
					appearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					appearanceHolder.Reset();
					appearanceHolder = null;
				}
				if (appearances != null && !((DisposableObject)(object)appearances).Disposed)
				{
					((SubObjectBase)(object)appearances).SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					((DisposableObject)(object)appearances).Dispose();
					appearances = null;
				}
				if (tabGroups != null && !tabGroups.Disposed)
				{
					tabGroups.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					tabGroups.Dispose();
					tabGroups = null;
				}
				if (tabSettings != null && !tabSettings.Disposed)
				{
					tabSettings.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					tabSettings.Dispose();
					tabSettings = null;
				}
				if (tabGroupSettings != null && !tabGroupSettings.Disposed)
				{
					tabGroupSettings.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					tabGroupSettings.Dispose();
					tabGroupSettings = null;
				}
				if (menuItems != null)
				{
					ContextMenuItem[] array = menuItems;
					foreach (ContextMenuItem val in array)
					{
						if (val != null)
						{
							val.Click -= MenuItemClickHandler;
						}
					}
				}
				if (tabContextMenuManager != null)
				{
					foreach (MdiTabGroupControl value in TabGroupControls.Values)
					{
						if (!((Control)(object)value).Disposing && !((Control)(object)value).IsDisposed)
						{
							ContextMenuManager.ClearWindowsFormsContextMenu((Control)(object)value);
						}
					}
					((ContextMenuItemBase)tabContextMenuManager).Popup -= OnContextMenuPopup;
					((DisposableObject)(object)tabContextMenuManager).Dispose();
					tabContextMenuManager = null;
				}
				if (menuItems != null)
				{
					ContextMenuItem[] array2 = menuItems;
					for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
					{
						((DisposableObject)(object)array2[j])?.Dispose();
					}
					menuItems = null;
				}
				if (newTabGroupHorzImage != null)
				{
					newTabGroupHorzImage.Dispose();
					newTabGroupHorzImage = null;
				}
				if (newTabGroupHorzImgStream != null)
				{
					newTabGroupHorzImgStream.Close();
					newTabGroupHorzImgStream = null;
				}
				if (newTabGroupVertImage != null)
				{
					newTabGroupVertImage.Dispose();
					newTabGroupVertImage = null;
				}
				if (newTabGroupVertImgStream != null)
				{
					newTabGroupVertImgStream.Close();
					newTabGroupVertImgStream = null;
				}
				if (newTabGroupVertImageVS2005 != null)
				{
					newTabGroupVertImageVS2005.Dispose();
					newTabGroupVertImageVS2005 = null;
				}
				if (newTabGroupVertImgStreamVS2005 != null)
				{
					newTabGroupVertImgStreamVS2005.Close();
					newTabGroupVertImgStreamVS2005 = null;
				}
				Office2007ColorTable.ColorSchemeChanged -= OnOffice2007ColorSchemeChanged;
			}
			((UltraStylableComponent)this).Dispose(disposing);
		}

		protected virtual void OnBeginInit()
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnEndInit()
		{
		}

		private void ResolveSplitterAppearanceVert(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			ResolveSplitterAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, Orientation.Vertical);
		}

		private void ResolveSplitterAppearanceHorz(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			ResolveSplitterAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, Orientation.Horizontal);
		}

		protected internal virtual void ResolveSplitterAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps, Orientation orientation)
		{
			ResolutionOrderInfo resOrder;
			UIRole role = StyleUtils.GetRole((IUltraControl)(object)this, (orientation == Orientation.Vertical) ? StyleUtils.Role.SplitterBarVert : StyleUtils.Role.SplitterBarHorz, out resOrder);
			if (role != null && ((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resOrder)).UseStyleBefore)
			{
				role.ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (RoleState)1);
			}
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resOrder)).UseControlInfo && HasSplitterAppearance)
			{
				SplitterAppearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
			if (role != null && ((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resOrder)).UseStyleAfter)
			{
				role.ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (RoleState)1);
			}
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resOrder)).UseControlInfo && HasAppearance)
			{
				Appearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
			ViewStyle viewStyleResolved = ViewStyleResolved;
			if (Utilities.ShouldSetAppearanceProperty(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (AppearancePropFlags)1))
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColor = viewStyleResolved switch
				{
					ViewStyle.Office2003 => MSColors.OutlookNavPaneLabelItemGradientLight, 
					ViewStyle.Office2007 => Office2007ColorTable.Colors.TabBorderColor, 
					_ => SystemColors.Control, 
				};
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEuL);
			}
			if (Utilities.ShouldSetAppearanceProperty(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (AppearancePropFlags)1073741824))
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BorderColor3DBase = ((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColor;
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFBFFFFFFFuL);
			}
			if (Utilities.ShouldSetAppearanceProperty(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (AppearancePropFlags)4))
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BorderColor = viewStyleResolved switch
				{
					ViewStyle.Office2003 => MSColors.ToolBorder, 
					ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005 => SystemColors.AppWorkspace, 
					_ => DrawUtility.Dark(((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColor), 
				};
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFBuL);
			}
		}

		internal void ActivateMdiChild(MdiTab tab, bool invokeTabSelecting, bool invokeTabSelected, bool allowCancel, bool activate)
		{
			if (isActivatingMdiChild || tab == null || tab.Form == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			isActivatingMdiChild = true;
			try
			{
				if (invokeTabSelecting)
				{
					CancelableMdiTabEventArgs e = new CancelableMdiTabEventArgs(tab);
					InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabSelecting, e);
					if (allowCancel && e.Cancel)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				if (activate)
				{
					if (tab.IsFormActive)
					{
						if (MdiParent != null && MdiParent.ActiveControl != tab.Form)
						{
							Control activeControl = tab.Form.ActiveControl;
							while (activeControl is IContainerControl)
							{
								activeControl = ((IContainerControl)activeControl).ActiveControl;
							}
							if (activeControl != null && !activeControl.ContainsFocus && activeControl.CanFocus)
							{
								Utilities.FocusControl(activeControl);
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						Utilities.ActivateForm(tab.Form);
					}
				}
				if (tab.TabGroup != null)
				{
					tab.TabGroup.InitializeSelectedTab(tab);
				}
				tab.EnsureTabInView();
				if (invokeTabSelected)
				{
					InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabSelected, new MdiTabEventArgs(tab));
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				isActivatingMdiChild = false;
				InvokeTabActivatedEvent();
			}
		}

		internal bool ShouldSubclasserAllowFormActivation(Form form, bool fireTabSelecting)
		{
			if (IsInitializing)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (IsActivatingMdiChild)
			{
				return true;
			}
			MdiTab tabFromForm = GetTabFromForm(form, verifyFormIsInitialized: false, verifyTabGroup: false, createIfNull: false);
			if (tabFromForm == null || !fireTabSelecting)
			{
				return true;
			}
			CancelableMdiTabEventArgs e = new CancelableMdiTabEventArgs(tabFromForm);
			InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabSelecting, e);
			return !e.Cancel;
		}

		internal bool ShouldSubclasserNotifyFormActivation(Form form, Form previousForm, ref bool fireSelected)
		{
			MdiTab tabFromForm = GetTabFromForm(form, verifyFormIsInitialized: true, verifyTabGroup: true, createIfNull: true);
			if (tabFromForm != null)
			{
				fireSelected = !tabFromForm.IsSelected;
				if (previousForm != null)
				{
					MdiTab tabFromForm2 = GetTabFromForm(previousForm, verifyFormIsInitialized: false, verifyTabGroup: false, createIfNull: false);
					if (tabFromForm2 != null && tabFromForm2.TabGroup != tabFromForm.TabGroup)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return fireSelected;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal void AfterSubclasserFormActivation(Form form, Form previousActiveForm, bool fireSelected)
		{
			if (IsInitializing || IsActivatingMdiChild)
			{
				return;
			}
			MdiTab mdiTab = TabFromForm(form);
			if (mdiTab == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			MdiTabGroup tabGroup = mdiTab.TabGroup;
			tabGroup?.InitializeSelectedTab(mdiTab);
			mdiTab.EnsureTabInView();
			if (previousActiveForm != null)
			{
				MdiTab mdiTab2 = TabFromForm(previousActiveForm);
				if (mdiTab2 != null)
				{
					MdiTabGroup tabGroup2 = mdiTab2.TabGroup;
					if (tabGroup2 != null && tabGroup2 != tabGroup)
					{
						EnsureTabIsSelected(tabGroup2, allowEventNotifications: true);
					}
				}
			}
			if (fireSelected)
			{
				MdiTabEventArgs e = new MdiTabEventArgs(mdiTab);
				InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabSelected, e);
			}
			InvokeTabActivatedEvent();
		}

		internal bool CanAllowSelectionCancel(MdiTab tab)
		{
			if (tab == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (tab == ActiveTab)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (GetTopMostTab(tab.TabGroup) == tab)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void InvokeTabActivatedEvent()
		{
			Form form = ((MdiParent == null) ? null : MdiParent.ActiveMdiChild);
			MdiTab mdiTab = ((form == null) ? null : GetTabFromForm(form, verifyFormIsInitialized: true, verifyTabGroup: false, createIfNull: false));
			if (!IsActivatingMdiChild && !SubclasserActivatingMdiChild && mdiTab != lastActivatedTab && mdiTab != null)
			{
				lastActivatedTab = mdiTab;
				VerifyMaximizedTabGroup();
				InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabActivated, new MdiTabEventArgs(mdiTab));
			}
			else
			{
				lastActivatedTab = mdiTab;
				VerifyMaximizedTabGroup();
			}
		}

		internal void DirtyGroupPosition(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (!IsInitializing)
			{
				TabGroupManager.DirtyTabGroup(tabGroup);
				PositionAndSizeTabGroups();
			}
		}

		internal void InitializeTabFormSettings(MdiTab tab)
		{
			if (tab == null || tab.IsFormInitialized || !((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled || tab.MdiChild == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (tab.IsCustomTab)
			{
				tab.CustomMdiTab.IsMdiTab = true;
			}
			try
			{
				SetRedraw(redrawOn: false);
				tab.InitializeTabFormSettings();
				DelayedRedraw();
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				tab.InitializeTabFormSettings();
			}
		}

		private void DelayedMdiClientRedraw()
		{
			SetRedraw(redrawOn: true);
		}

		private void OnApplicationIdle(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			Application.Idle -= OnApplicationIdle;
			hookedIdle = false;
			SetRedraw(redrawOn: true);
		}

		internal void MdiChildVisibilityChanged(MdiTab tab)
		{
			if (tab.IsFormVisible)
			{
				if (tab.TabGroup != null)
				{
					tab.TabGroup.DirtyTabManager(MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.Collection | MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate);
				}
				else
				{
					AddTabToTabGroup(tab);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				RemoveTabFromTabGroup(tab);
			}
		}

		internal void MoveAllTabs(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, MdiTabGroupPosition position)
		{
			if (EventManager.InProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.RestoreTab))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_64"));
			}
			MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = TabGroupFromPosition(tabGroup, position);
			if (mdiTabGroup == tabGroup)
			{
				return;
			}
			SuspendLayout();
			try
			{
				for (int num = tabGroup.Tabs.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
				{
					MdiTab tab = tabGroup.Tabs[num];
					MoveTab(tab, mdiTabGroup, 0, invokeDisplayEvents: false, invokeTabMoveEvents: true);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				ResumeLayout(positionControls: true);
			}
		}

		internal void RepositionTab(MdiTab tabToReposition, MdiTab relativeTab, MdiTabPosition relativePosition)
		{
			if (EventManager.InProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.RestoreTab))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_64"));
			}
			if (!tabToReposition.IsFormVisible)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_35"));
			}
			if (!relativeTab.IsFormVisible)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_38"));
			}
			bool flag = tabToReposition.TabGroup == null;
			bool invokeTabMoveEvents = !flag;
			switch (relativePosition)
			{
			case MdiTabPosition.First:
				MoveTab(tabToReposition, relativeTab.TabGroup, 0, flag, invokeTabMoveEvents);
				break;
			case MdiTabPosition.Last:
			{
				int num = relativeTab.TabGroup.Tabs.Count;
				if (relativeTab.TabGroup.Tabs.Contains(tabToReposition))
				{
					num--;
				}
				MoveTab(tabToReposition, relativeTab.TabGroup, num, flag, invokeTabMoveEvents);
				break;
			}
			case MdiTabPosition.Previous:
				if (tabToReposition != relativeTab)
				{
					if (relativeTab.TabGroup != tabToReposition.TabGroup)
					{
						MoveTab(tabToReposition, relativeTab.TabGroup, ((ITabItem)relativeTab).Index, flag, invokeTabMoveEvents);
						break;
					}
					int index3 = ((ITabItem)relativeTab).Index;
					int index4 = ((ITabItem)tabToReposition).Index;
					int tabIndex2 = ((index4 >= index3) ? index3 : (index3 - 1));
					MoveTab(tabToReposition, relativeTab.TabGroup, tabIndex2, flag, invokeTabMoveEvents);
				}
				break;
			case MdiTabPosition.Next:
				if (tabToReposition != relativeTab)
				{
					if (relativeTab.TabGroup != tabToReposition.TabGroup)
					{
						MoveTab(tabToReposition, relativeTab.TabGroup, ((ITabItem)relativeTab).Index + 1, flag, invokeTabMoveEvents);
						break;
					}
					int index = ((ITabItem)relativeTab).Index;
					int index2 = ((ITabItem)tabToReposition).Index;
					int tabIndex = ((index2 <= index) ? index : (index + 1));
					MoveTab(tabToReposition, relativeTab.TabGroup, tabIndex, flag, invokeTabMoveEvents);
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		internal MdiTabGroupControl GetTabGroupControl(MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup)
		{
			return TabGroupControls[mdiTabGroup] as MdiTabGroupControl;
		}

		internal int GetTabGroupIndex(MdiTab tab, MdiTabGroup parentTabGroupToFind)
		{
			MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = tab?.TabGroup;
			MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup2;
			for (mdiTabGroup2 = mdiTabGroup?.Parent; mdiTabGroup2 != parentTabGroupToFind; mdiTabGroup2 = mdiTabGroup2.Parent)
			{
				mdiTabGroup = mdiTabGroup2;
			}
			if (mdiTabGroup2 != parentTabGroupToFind)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			return mdiTabGroup.Index;
		}

		internal int CalculateCommonExtent(MdiTabGroup parentTabGroup)
		{
			if (parentTabGroup == null)
			{
				return CommonMdiClientExtent;
			}
			return Math.Max(0, parentTabGroup.ClientExtent);
		}

		internal int CalculateSizableExtent(MdiTabGroup parentTabGroup)
		{
			if (parentTabGroup == null)
			{
				return AvailableMdiClientExtent;
			}
			if (parentTabGroup.Parent == null)
			{
				return CommonMdiClientExtent;
			}
			return parentTabGroup.Parent.ClientExtent;
		}

		internal static MdiTabGroup GetFirstLeafTabGroup(MdiTabGroupsCollection tabGroups)
		{
			if (tabGroups.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = tabGroups[0];
			if (mdiTabGroup.HasTabGroups)
			{
				return GetFirstLeafTabGroup(mdiTabGroup.TabGroups);
			}
			return mdiTabGroup;
		}

		internal static MdiTabGroup GetLastLeafTabGroup(MdiTabGroupsCollection tabGroups)
		{
			if (tabGroups.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = tabGroups[tabGroups.Count - 1];
			if (mdiTabGroup.HasTabGroups)
			{
				return GetLastLeafTabGroup(mdiTabGroup.TabGroups);
			}
			return mdiTabGroup;
		}

		internal int CalculateMaxTabGroupRows()
		{
			return CalculateTabGroupRowsColsHelper(null, Orientation.Horizontal);
		}

		internal int CalculateMaxTabGroupCols()
		{
			return CalculateTabGroupRowsColsHelper(null, Orientation.Vertical);
		}

		private void VerifyMaximizedTabGroup()
		{
			if (IsActiveTabGroupMaximized)
			{
				MaximizeTabGroup((lastActivatedTab == null) ? null : lastActivatedTab.TabGroup, cachePreMaximizedExtent: true);
			}
			else
			{
				MaximizeTabGroup(null, cachePreMaximizedExtent: true);
			}
		}

		private void MaximizeTabGroup(MdiTabGroup tabGroupToMaximize, bool cachePreMaximizedExtent)
		{
			if (tabGroupToMaximize == maximizedTabGroup || IsInitializing || IsRestoringTabs || IsRestoringTabGroup)
			{
				return;
			}
			MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = maximizedTabGroup;
			SuspendLayout();
			try
			{
				if (tabGroupToMaximize == null)
				{
					foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
					{
						nestedTabGroup.InitializeExtent(nestedTabGroup.PreMaximizedExtent);
					}
				}
				else if (mdiTabGroup == null && cachePreMaximizedExtent)
				{
					VerifyTabGroupExtents();
					foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup2 in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
					{
						nestedTabGroup2.CachePreMaximizedExtent();
					}
				}
				maximizedTabGroup = tabGroupToMaximize;
				if (tabGroupToMaximize != null)
				{
					foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup3 in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
					{
						nestedTabGroup3.InitializeExtent(-1);
					}
				}
				DirtyTabGroups(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Extent);
				SynchronizeSplitterProperties();
			}
			finally
			{
				ResumeLayout(positionControls: true);
			}
		}

		internal void OnDragTabStart()
		{
			isDraggingTab = true;
		}

		internal void OnDragTabEnd()
		{
			isDraggingTab = false;
		}

		internal Font CreateTabMetricsFont(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			//IL_0039: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0049: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0059: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0069: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0079: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0089: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_008e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0096: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			Font baseFont = GetBaseFont(tabGroup);
			MdiTabSettings groupSettings = (tabGroup.HasTabSettings ? tabGroup.TabSettings : null);
			MdiTabSettings managerSettings = (HasTabSettings ? TabSettings : null);
			return Utilities.CreateLargestFont((AppearanceData[])(object)new AppearanceData[6]
			{
				MdiTab.ResolveTabFontAppearance(tabGroup, (TabState)7, groupSettings, managerSettings),
				MdiTab.ResolveTabFontAppearance(tabGroup, (TabState)5, groupSettings, managerSettings),
				MdiTab.ResolveTabFontAppearance(tabGroup, (TabState)3, groupSettings, managerSettings),
				MdiTab.ResolveTabFontAppearance(tabGroup, (TabState)1, groupSettings, managerSettings),
				MdiTab.ResolveTabFontAppearance(tabGroup, (TabState)2, groupSettings, managerSettings),
				MdiTab.ResolveTabFontAppearance(tabGroup, (TabState)1, groupSettings, managerSettings)
			}, baseFont, DrawUtility.GetGdiDrawStringFlags((UltraComponentBase)(object)this));
		}

		internal UIElement GetUIElement(MdiTab tab)
		{
			if (tab == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			MdiTabGroup tabGroup = tab.TabGroup;
			if (tabGroup == null || !tabGroup.HasTabManager)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return tabGroup.TabManager.GetUIElement((ITabItem)(object)tab);
		}

		internal bool ProcessMnemonic(char character, MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (!HasTabGroups || tabGroup != GetFirstLeafTabGroup(TabGroups))
			{
				return false;
			}
			MdiTab activeTab = ActiveTab;
			if (activeTab == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			MdiTabGroup tabGroup2 = activeTab.TabGroup;
			int num = ((ITabItem)activeTab).Index + 1;
			int num2 = num;
			for (MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = tabGroup2; mdiTabGroup != null; mdiTabGroup = mdiTabGroup.NextLeafTabGroup)
			{
				for (int i = num2; i < mdiTabGroup.Tabs.Count; i++)
				{
					if (ProcessMnemonic(character, mdiTabGroup.Tabs[i]))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				num2 = 0;
			}
			for (MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = GetFirstLeafTabGroup(TabGroups); mdiTabGroup != tabGroup2; mdiTabGroup = mdiTabGroup.NextLeafTabGroup)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < mdiTabGroup.Tabs.Count; j++)
				{
					if (ProcessMnemonic(character, mdiTabGroup.Tabs[j]))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
			for (int k = 0; k < num; k++)
			{
				if (ProcessMnemonic(character, tabGroup2.Tabs[k]))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool ProcessMnemonic(char character, MdiTab tab)
		{
			if (!tab.IsFormEnabled || !tab.IsFormVisible)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!Control.IsMnemonic(character, tab.TextResolved))
			{
				return false;
			}
			tab.Activate();
			return true;
		}

		internal Point PointToClient(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, Point screenPt)
		{
			if (!(TabGroupControls[tabGroup] is MdiTabGroupControl mdiTabGroupControl))
			{
				return Point.Empty;
			}
			return ((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).PointToClient(screenPt);
		}

		{
			if (!(TabGroupControls[tabGroup] is MdiTabGroupControl mdiTabGroupControl))
			{
				return Point.Empty;
			}
			return ((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).PointToScreen(clientPt);
		}

		}

		internal void ShowDragDropContextMenu(MdiTab tab, Point screenPt)
		{
			InitializeTabContextMenu(tab, MdiTabContextMenu.Reposition);
			tabForContextMenu = tab;
			MdiTabGroup tabGroup = tab.TabGroup;
			MdiTabContextMenuEventArgs e = new MdiTabContextMenuEventArgs(this, tab, TabContextMenuManager.ContextMenu, MdiTabContextMenu.Reposition);
			InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.InitializeContextMenu, e);
			if (e.Cancel)
			{
				((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Clear();
				return;
			}
			if (!(TabGroupControls[tabGroup] is Control control))
			{
				((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Clear();
				return;
			}
			Point point = control.PointToClient(screenPt);
			ignoreContextMenuPopup = true;
			try
			{
				TabContextMenuManager.Show(control, point);
			}
			finally
			{
				ignoreContextMenuPopup = false;
			}
		}

		internal void ResetFormPosition(MdiTab tab)
		{
			Form form = tab.Form;
			if (tab.IsFormInitialized && form != null && form.WindowState != FormWindowState.Normal)
			{
				form.WindowState = FormWindowState.Normal;
			}
			if (!PositioningControls && !IsLayoutSuspended && !((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).IsUpdating && ((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled && form != null && !form.IsDisposed && !form.Disposing)
			{
				MdiTabGroup tabGroup = tab.TabGroup;
				if (tabGroup != null)
				{
					Rectangle formPosition = TabGroupManager.GetFormPosition(tabGroup);
					form.Bounds = formPosition;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void MdiClientCreateNotification()
		{
			if (!WaitingForDelayedRedraw)
			{
				DisableMdiClientRedraw();
				DelayedRedraw();
			}
		}

		internal void OnAppStyleChanged()
		{
			StyleUtils.DirtyCachedPropertyVals(this);
			DirtyTabGroups(MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.AllMetrics | MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate | MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.Font);
			SynchronizeSplitterProperties();
			InvalidateControls(dirtyChildElements: true, invalidate: true, splitterControls: true, tabGroupControls: true);
			TabGroupManager.DirtyAllTabGroups();
			RefreshSplitterIntersections();
			PositionAndSizeTabGroups();
		}

		private void DirtyTabGroups(TabbedMdiPropertyIds property)
		{
			if (HasTabGroups)
			{
				DirtyTabGroups(MdiTabGroup.GetTabChangeType(property));
			}
		}

		private void DirtyTabGroups(TabbedMdiPropertyIds property, PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			if (HasTabGroups)
			{
				DirtyTabGroups(MdiTabGroup.GetTabChangeType(property, propChangeInfo));
			}
		}

		private void DirtyTabGroups(MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType changeType)
		{
			if (changeType == MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.None || IsInitializing)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = !IsLayoutSuspended;
			SuspendLayout();
			try
			{
				foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
				{
					nestedTabGroup.DirtyTabManager(changeType);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				bool positionControls = (changeType & MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.AllMetrics) != MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.None || (changeType & MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.Collection) != 0;
				ResumeLayout(positionControls);
			}
		}

		private void SaveAsBinaryHelper(Stream stream)
		{
			IFormatter formatter = CreateBinaryFormatter();
			SaveHelper(stream, formatter);
		}

		private void SaveHelper(Stream stream, IFormatter formatter)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_39"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_40"));
			}
			if (HasMdiTabs)
			{
				StoreTabEventArgs e = new StoreTabEventArgs(null);
				foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
				{
					foreach (MdiTab tab in nestedTabGroup.Tabs)
					{
						e.Initialize(tab);
						InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.StoreTab, e);
					}
				}
				foreach (MdiTab hiddenTab in HiddenTabs)
				{
					e.Initialize(hiddenTab);
					InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.StoreTab, e);
				}
			}
			ObjectStreamer graph = CreateObjectStreamer();
			formatter.Serialize(stream, graph);
		}

		private IFormatter CreateBinaryFormatter()
		{
			BinaryFormatter binaryFormatter = new BinaryFormatter();
			binaryFormatter.AssemblyFormat = FormatterAssemblyStyle.Simple;
			return binaryFormatter;
		}

		private void LoadFromBinaryHelper(Stream stream)
		{
			IFormatter formatter = CreateBinaryFormatter();
			LoadHelper(stream, formatter);
		}

		private void LoadHelper(Stream stream, IFormatter formatter)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_39"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_40"));
			}
			formatter.Binder = (SerializationBinder)(object)new Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabbedMdi.Serialization.Binder();
			ObjectStreamer streamer = formatter.Deserialize(stream) as ObjectStreamer;
			try
			{
				if (MdiParent != null)
				{
					MdiParent.SuspendLayout();
				}
				if (mdiClient != null)
				{
					mdiClient.SuspendLayout();
				}
				SuspendLayout();
				LoadHelper(streamer);
			}
			finally
			{
				if (MdiParent != null)
				{
					MdiParent.ResumeLayout(performLayout: false);
				}
				if (mdiClient != null)
				{
					mdiClient.ResumeLayout(performLayout: false);
				}
				ResumeLayout(positionControls: true);
			}
		}

		private void LoadHelper(ObjectStreamer streamer)
		{
			try
			{
				ReleaseMdiTabsAndForms();
				isInitializing = true;
				InitializeFrom(streamer);
			}
			finally
			{
				isInitializing = false;
			}
			StyleUtils.DirtyCachedPropertyVals(this);
			TabGroupManager.DirtyAllTabGroups();
			lastActiveTabGroup = null;
			cachedActiveTab = null;
			lastActivatedTab = null;
			maximizedTabGroup = null;
			if (MdiParent == null)
			{
				ReleaseMdiTabsAndForms();
			}
			else
			{
				InitializeDeserializedTabs();
			}
			if (IsMdiClientSubclassed)
			{
				UpdateBorderStyle();
				if (mdiClientSubclasser != null)
				{
					mdiClientSubclasser.BorderColorChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		private void InitializeMdiTabMdiChild(Form[] mdiChildren)
		{
			if (tabGroups == null || tabGroups.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup in tabGroups)
			{
				if (!tabGroup.HasTabsResolved)
				{
					continue;
				}
				MdiTabsCollection tabs = tabGroup.Tabs;
				foreach (MdiTab item in tabs)
				{
					if (item.MdiChild == null)
					{
						int mdiChildIndex = item.MdiChildIndex;
						if (mdiChildIndex >= 0 && mdiChildIndex < mdiChildren.Length && mdiChildren[mdiChildIndex] != null)
						{
							item.MdiChild = mdiChildren[mdiChildIndex];
							mdiChildren[mdiChildIndex] = null;
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void InitializeDeserializedTabs()
		{
			try
			{
				if (HasMdiTabs)
				{
					Form[] mdiChildren = ((MdiParent != null) ? MdiParent.MdiChildren : null);
					InitializeMdiTabMdiChild(mdiChildren);
					if (HasHiddenTabs)
					{
						RestoreTabs(HiddenTabs);
					}
					RestoreTabGroups();
					if (HasTabGroups)
					{
						foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
						{
							for (int num = nestedTabGroup.Tabs.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
							{
								if (nestedTabGroup.Tabs[num].Form == null)
								{
									nestedTabGroup.Tabs[num].InitializeOwner(null);
									nestedTabGroup.Tabs.RemoveAt(num);
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if (HasHiddenTabs)
					{
						for (int num2 = HiddenTabs.Count - 1; num2 >= 0; num2--)
						{
							if (HiddenTabs[num2].Form == null)
							{
								HiddenTabs[num2].InitializeOwner(null);
								HiddenTabs.RemoveAt(num2);
							}
						}
					}
					if (HasTabGroups)
					{
						foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup2 in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
						{
							for (int num3 = nestedTabGroup2.Tabs.Count - 1; num3 >= 0; num3--)
							{
								MdiTab mdiTab = nestedTabGroup2.Tabs[num3];
								if (!mdiTab.IsFormVisible)
								{
									nestedTabGroup2.Tabs.RemoveAt(num3);
									HiddenTabs.Add(mdiTab);
									mdiTab.InitializeOwner(this);
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if (HasHiddenTabs)
					{
						for (int num4 = HiddenTabs.Count - 1; num4 >= 0; num4--)
						{
							MdiTab mdiTab2 = HiddenTabs[num4];
							if (mdiTab2.IsFormVisible)
							{
								AddTabToTabGroup(mdiTab2);
							}
						}
					}
					RemoveEmptyTabGroups();
				}
				if (MdiParent.MdiChildren.Length == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				Form[] mdiChildren2 = MdiParent.MdiChildren;
				foreach (Form form in mdiChildren2)
				{
					if (GetTabFromForm(form, verifyFormIsInitialized: false, verifyTabGroup: false, createIfNull: false) == null)
					{
						arrayList.Add(form);
					}
				}
				if (arrayList.Count > 0)
				{
					CreateTabsForForms((Form[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(Form)));
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				CacheLastActiveTabGroup();
				cachedActiveTab = ActiveTab;
				lastActivatedTab = cachedActiveTab;
				if (IsActiveTabGroupMaximized)
				{
					MaximizeTabGroup((lastActivatedTab == null) ? null : lastActivatedTab.TabGroup, cachePreMaximizedExtent: false);
				}
				DirtyTabGroups(MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.AllMetrics);
				InitializeTabGroupExtents(invokeResizeEvents: true);
				foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup3 in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
				{
					EnsureTabIsSelected(nestedTabGroup3, allowEventNotifications: false);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RestoreTabGroups()
		{
			if (TabGroups.Count != 0)
			{
				RestoreTabGroups(TabGroups, serializedActiveTabGroupIndex);
			}
		}

		private void RestoreTabGroups(MdiTabGroupsCollection tabGroups, int serializedActiveTabGroupIndex)
		{
			int num = serializedActiveTabGroupIndex;
			int count = tabGroups.Count;
			if (num < 0 || num >= count)
			{
				num = -1;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (i != num)
				{
					RestoreTabGroup(tabGroups[i]);
				}
			}
			if (num >= 0 && num < tabGroups.Count)
			{
				RestoreTabGroup(tabGroups[num]);
			}
		}

		private void RestoreTabGroup(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (tabGroup.HasTabGroups)
			{
				RestoreTabGroups(tabGroup.TabGroups, tabGroup.SerializedActiveTabGroupIndex);
				return;
			}
			restoringTabGroup = true;
			try
			{
				MdiTab serializedSelectedTab = tabGroup.SerializedSelectedTab;
				MdiTab serializedFirstDisplayedTab = tabGroup.SerializedFirstDisplayedTab;
				RestoreTabs(tabGroup.Tabs);
				if (serializedFirstDisplayedTab != null && serializedFirstDisplayedTab.Form != null && ((ITabItem)serializedFirstDisplayedTab).Visible && tabGroup.Tabs.Contains(serializedFirstDisplayedTab))
				{
					tabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab = serializedFirstDisplayedTab;
				}
				if (serializedSelectedTab != null && serializedSelectedTab.Form != null && ((ITabItem)serializedSelectedTab).Visible && tabGroup.Tabs.Contains(serializedSelectedTab))
				{
					serializedSelectedTab.Activate();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				restoringTabGroup = false;
			}
		}

		private ObjectStreamer CreateObjectStreamer()
		{
			//IL_014f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01d7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01dd: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_01e6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0214: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0232: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0222: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0240: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0308: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ObjectStreamer objectStreamer = new ObjectStreamer("Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabbedMdi", 25);
			IDictionary dictionary = objectStreamer.Dictionary;
			if (ShouldSerializeAllowHorizontalTabGroups())
			{
				dictionary.Add("AllowHorizontalTabGroups", AllowHorizontalTabGroups);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeAllowVerticalTabGroups())
			{
				dictionary.Add("AllowVerticalTabGroups", AllowVerticalTabGroups);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeAppearance())
			{
				dictionary.Add("Appearance", appearanceHolder);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeSplitterAppearance())
			{
				dictionary.Add("SplitterAppearance", splitterAppearanceHolder);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeAppearances())
			{
				dictionary.Add("Appearances", Appearances);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTabSettings())
			{
				dictionary.Add("TabSettings", TabSettings);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTabGroupSettings())
			{
				dictionary.Add("TabGroupSettings", TabGroupSettings);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeTabGroups())
			{
				dictionary.Add("TabGroups", TabGroups);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeShowToolTips())
			{
				dictionary.Add("ShowToolTips", ShowToolTips);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeUseMnemonics())
			{
				dictionary.Add("UseMnemonics", UseMnemonics);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeOrientation())
			{
				dictionary.Add("Orientation", Orientation);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeSplitterBorderStyle())
			{
				dictionary.Add("SplitterBorderStyle", SplitterBorderStyle);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeMaxTabGroups())
			{
				dictionary.Add("MaxTabGroups", MaxTabGroups);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeSplitterWidth())
			{
				dictionary.Add("SplitterWidth", SplitterWidth);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeImageSize())
			{
				dictionary.Add("ImageSize", ImageSize);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeImageTransparentColor())
			{
				dictionary.Add("ImageTransparentColor", ImageTransparentColor);
			}
			if ((int)((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).AlphaBlendMode != 2)
			{
				dictionary.Add("AlphaBlendMode", ((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).AlphaBlendMode);
			}
			if (!((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled)
			{
				dictionary.Add("Enabled", ((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled);
			}
			if ((int)((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).UseFlatMode != 0)
			{
				dictionary.Add("UseFlatMode", ((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).UseFlatMode);
			}
			if ((int)((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).UseOsThemes != 0)
			{
				dictionary.Add("UseOsThemes", ((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).UseOsThemes);
			}
			if (HasHiddenTabs)
			{
				dictionary.Add("HiddenTabs", HiddenTabs);
			}
			MdiTab activeTab = ActiveTab;
			if (activeTab != null && activeTab.TabGroup != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("ActiveTabGroupIndex", GetTabGroupIndex(ActiveTab, null));
			}
			if (BorderStyle != MdiClientBorderStyle.Solid)
			{
				dictionary.Add("BorderStyle", BorderStyle);
			}
			if (!BorderColor.IsEmpty)
			{
				dictionary.Add("BorderColor", BorderColor);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeViewStyle())
			{
				dictionary.Add("ViewStyle", ViewStyle);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeAllowNestedTabGroups())
			{
				dictionary.Add("AllowNestedTabGroups", AllowNestedTabGroups);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeMaximizedTabGroupDisplayStyle())
			{
				dictionary.Add("MaximizedTabGroupDisplayStyle", MaximizedTabGroupDisplayStyle);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeIsActiveTabGroupMaximized())
			{
				dictionary.Add("IsActiveTabGroupMaximized", IsActiveTabGroupMaximized);
			}
			if (ShouldSerializeAllowMaximize())
			{
				dictionary.Add("AllowMaximize", AllowMaximize);
			}
			if (TabNavigationMode != MdiTabNavigationMode.ActivationOrder)
			{
				dictionary.Add("TabNavigationMode", TabNavigationMode);
			}
			return objectStreamer;
		}

		private void RestoreTabs(MdiTabsBaseCollection tabs)
		{
			isRestoringTabs = true;
			try
			{
				bool wasVisible = tabs is MdiTabsCollection;
				foreach (MdiTab tab in tabs)
				{
					RestoreTabEventArgs e = new RestoreTabEventArgs(tab, wasVisible);
					if (tab.PersistedInfo != null)
					{
						e.Form = FindMatchingForm(tab);
					}
					InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.RestoreTab, e);
					if (e.Form != null)
					{
						MdiTab tabFromForm = GetTabFromForm(e.Form, verifyFormIsInitialized: false, verifyTabGroup: false, createIfNull: false);
						if (tabFromForm != null)
						{
							tabFromForm.MdiChild = null;
						}
						tab.MdiChild = e.Form;
						InitializeTabFormSettings(tab);
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				isRestoringTabs = false;
			}
		}

		private Form FindMatchingForm(MdiTab tab)
		{
			//IL_0032: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (tab == null || tab.PersistedInfo == null || mdiParent == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Form[] mdiChildren = MdiParent.MdiChildren;
			foreach (Form form in mdiChildren)
			{
				if (form is ICustomMdiTab && object.Equals(((ICustomMdiTab)form).UniqueId, tab.PersistedInfo) && GetTabFromForm(form, verifyFormIsInitialized: false, verifyTabGroup: false, createIfNull: false) == null)
				{
					return form;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void EnsureOnlyOneTabbedMdiManager(Form form)
		{
			if (!((Component)this).DesignMode && form != null)
			{
				ITabbedMdiManager tabbedMdiManager = MdiParentManager.GetTabbedMdiManager(form);
				if (tabbedMdiManager != null && (object)tabbedMdiManager != this)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_42"));
				}
			}
		}

		private void UnhookMdiParent()
		{
			if (!((Component)this).DesignMode && mdiParent != null && hookedMdiParent)
			{
				MdiParentManager.UnregisterTabbedMdiManager((ITabbedMdiManager)(object)this, mdiParent);
				mdiParent.ControlAdded -= OnMdiParentFormControlAdded;
				mdiParent.ControlRemoved -= OnMdiParentFormControlRemoved;
				mdiParent.MdiChildActivate -= OnMdiParentMdiChildActivated;
				mdiParent.HandleCreated -= OnMdiParentHandleCreated;
				UnsubclassMdiParent();
				hookedMdiParent = false;
				UnhookMdiClient(mdiClient);
				ReleaseMdiTabsAndForms();
			}
		}

		private void HookMdiParent()
		{
			if (((Component)this).DesignMode || mdiParent == null || IsInitializing)
			{
				return;
			}
			EnsureOnlyOneTabbedMdiManager(mdiParent);
			if (!hookedMdiParent)
			{
				MdiParentManager.RegisterTabbedMdiManager((ITabbedMdiManager)(object)this, mdiParent);
				mdiParent.ControlAdded += OnMdiParentFormControlAdded;
				mdiParent.ControlRemoved += OnMdiParentFormControlRemoved;
				mdiParent.MdiChildActivate += OnMdiParentMdiChildActivated;
				hookedMdiParent = true;
				if (!mdiParent.IsHandleCreated)
				{
					mdiParent.HandleCreated += OnMdiParentHandleCreated;
				}
				else
				{
					SubclassMdiParent(mdiParent);
				}
				HookMdiClient(null);
				if (mdiClient != null)
				{
					CreateTabsForForms(MdiParent.MdiChildren);
				}
			}
		}

		private void HookMdiClient(MdiClient mdiClient)
		{
			if (((Component)this).DesignMode || IsInitializing || hookedMdiClient || mdiParent == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (mdiClient == null)
			{
				foreach (Control control in mdiParent.Controls)
				{
					if (control is MdiClient)
					{
						mdiClient = control as MdiClient;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (mdiClient != null)
			{
				this.mdiClient = mdiClient;
				mdiClient.ControlAdded += OnMdiClientControlAdded;
				mdiClient.ControlRemoved += OnMdiClientControlRemoved;
				mdiClient.Resize += OnMdiClientResize;
				mdiClient.LocationChanged += OnMdiClientLocationChanged;
				mdiClient.Layout += OnMdiClientLayout;
				if (!mdiClient.IsHandleCreated)
				{
					mdiClient.HandleCreated += OnMdiClientHandleCreated;
				}
				hookedMdiClient = true;
				if (mdiClient.IsHandleCreated)
				{
					SubclassMdiClient(mdiClient);
				}
			}
		}

		private void UnhookMdiClient(MdiClient mdiClient)
		{
			if (mdiClient == null)
			{
				foreach (Control control in mdiParent.Controls)
				{
					if (control is MdiClient)
					{
						mdiClient = control as MdiClient;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (mdiClient != null)
			{
				mdiClient.HandleCreated -= OnMdiClientHandleCreated;
				mdiClient.ControlAdded -= OnMdiClientControlAdded;
				mdiClient.ControlRemoved -= OnMdiClientControlRemoved;
				mdiClient.Resize -= OnMdiClientResize;
				mdiClient.LocationChanged -= OnMdiClientLocationChanged;
				mdiClient.Layout -= OnMdiClientLayout;
				hookedMdiClient = false;
				UnsubclassMdiClient();
				this.mdiClient = null;
			}
		}

		private void SubclassMdiClient(MdiClient mdiClient)
		{
			if (IsMdiClientSubclassed || !mdiClient.IsHandleCreated)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				if (mdiClientSubclasser == null || !mdiClientSubclasser.Activate(this, mdiClient))
				{
					SubclassMdiClientUnsafe(mdiClient);
				}
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				hasUnmanagedCodeRights = false;
			}
		}

		private void SubclassMdiClientUnsafe(MdiClient mdiClient)
		{
			MdiClientSubclasser mdiClientSubclasser = new MdiClientSubclasser(this);
			mdiClientSubclasser.AssignHandle(mdiClient.Handle);
			this.mdiClientSubclasser = mdiClientSubclasser;
		}

		private void UnsubclassMdiClient()
		{
			if (mdiClientSubclasser != null)
			{
				mdiClientSubclasser.Unsubclass();
			}
		}

		private void SubclassMdiParent(Form mdiParent)
		{
			if (IsMdiParentSubclassed || !mdiParent.IsHandleCreated)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				if (mdiParentSubclasser == null || !mdiParentSubclasser.Activate(this, mdiParent))
				{
					SubclassMdiParentUnsafe(mdiParent);
				}
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				hasUnmanagedCodeRights = false;
			}
		}

		private void SubclassMdiParentUnsafe(Form mdiParent)
		{
			MdiParentSubclasser mdiParentSubclasser = new MdiParentSubclasser(this);
			mdiParentSubclasser.AssignHandle(mdiParent.Handle);
			this.mdiParentSubclasser = mdiParentSubclasser;
		}

		private void UnsubclassMdiParent()
		{
			if (mdiParentSubclasser != null)
			{
				mdiParentSubclasser.Unsubclass();
			}
		}

		private void OnMdiParentFormControlAdded(object sender, ControlEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Control is MdiClient)
			{
				HookMdiClient(e.Control as MdiClient);
			}
		}

		private void OnMdiParentFormControlRemoved(object sender, ControlEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Control is MdiClient)
			{
				UnhookMdiClient(e.Control as MdiClient);
			}
		}

		private void OnMdiParentMdiChildActivated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsRestoringTabs || IsReleasingTabs || IsInitializing)
			{
				return;
			}
			CacheLastActiveTabGroup();
			if (cachedActiveTab != null && cachedActiveTab.TabGroup != null)
			{
				cachedActiveTab.TabGroup.DirtyTabItem(cachedActiveTab, invalidate: true, textChanged: false, imageChanged: false);
			}
			cachedActiveTab = ActiveTab;
			if (cachedActiveTab != null && cachedActiveTab.TabGroup != null)
			{
				cachedActiveTab.TabGroup.DirtyTabItem(cachedActiveTab, invalidate: true, textChanged: false, imageChanged: false);
			}
			if (!IsActivatingMdiChild && !SubclasserActivatingMdiChild)
			{
				EnsureActiveTabIsSelected(addToGroupIfNecessary: true);
				if (ActiveTab != null)
				{
					ActiveTab.EnsureTabInView();
				}
				InvokeTabActivatedEvent();
			}
		}

		private void OnMdiParentHandleCreated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsMdiParentSubclassed)
			{
				SubclassMdiParent(sender as Form);
			}
			if (IsMdiParentSubclassed && sender is Control control)
			{
				control.HandleCreated -= OnMdiParentHandleCreated;
			}
		}

		private void OnMdiClientLocationChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (mdiClient.Size != lastMdiClientSize)
			{
				sizeChanged = true;
				{
					foreach (MdiTabGroupControl value in TabGroupControls.Values)
					{
						((UltraControlBase)value).BeginUpdate();
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			PositionAndSizeTabGroups();
		}

		private void OnMdiClientHandleCreated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsMdiClientSubclassed)
			{
				SubclassMdiClient(sender as MdiClient);
			}
			if (IsMdiClientSubclassed && sender is Control control)
			{
				control.HandleCreated -= OnMdiClientHandleCreated;
			}
		}

		private void OnMdiClientLayout(object sender, LayoutEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void OnMdiClientControlRemoved(object sender, ControlEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsInitializing && e.Control is Form)
			{
				MdiTab tabFromForm = GetTabFromForm(e.Control as Form, verifyFormIsInitialized: false, verifyTabGroup: false, createIfNull: false);
				RemoveTabFromTabGroup(tabFromForm);
			}
		}

		private void OnMdiClientControlAdded(object sender, ControlEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsInitializing && !IsRestoringTabs)
			{
				MdiTab tabFromForm = GetTabFromForm(e.Control as Form, verifyFormIsInitialized: false, verifyTabGroup: false, createIfNull: false);
				if (tabFromForm == null)
				{
					CreateTabsForForms(new Form[1] { e.Control as Form });
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnMdiClientResize(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsDisposing)
			{
				return;
			}
			CachedMdiClientSize();
			if (!HasTabGroups)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				SetRedraw(redrawOn: false);
				SuspendLayout();
				InitializeTabGroupExtents(invokeResizeEvents: true);
				DirtyTabGroups(MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.AllMetrics | MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.Invalidate);
				TabGroupManager.DirtyAllTabGroups();
				PositionAndSizeTabGroups();
			}
			finally
			{
				if (sizeChanged)
				{
					foreach (MdiTabGroupControl value in TabGroupControls.Values)
					{
						((UltraControlBase)value).EndUpdate();
					}
					sizeChanged = false;
				}
				ResumeLayout(positionControls: true);
				SetRedraw(redrawOn: true);
			}
		}

		private void RemoveTabGroupControls()
		{
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
			{
				RemoveTabGroupControl(nestedTabGroup);
			}
		}

		private void RemoveTabGroupControl(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (TabGroupControls[tabGroup] is MdiTabGroupControl mdiTabGroupControl)
			{
				((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).FontChanged -= OnTabGroupControlFontChanged;
				TabGroupControls.Remove(tabGroup);
				((Component)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).Dispose();
			}
		}

		private void CreateTabGroupControls()
		{
			if (!((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
			{
				MdiTabGroupControl mdiTabGroupControl = TabGroupControls[nestedTabGroup] as MdiTabGroupControl;
				if (mdiTabGroupControl == null)
				{
					CreateTabGroupControl(nestedTabGroup);
				}
			}
		}

		private void CreateTabGroupControl(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled)
			{
				MdiTabGroupControl mdiTabGroupControl = CreateMdiTabGroupControl(tabGroup);
				if (mdiTabGroupControl == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_43"));
				}
				((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).Visible = false;
				((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).FontChanged += OnTabGroupControlFontChanged;
				((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).DragDrop += OnTabGroupControlDragDrop;
				((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).DragEnter += OnTabGroupControlDragEnter;
				((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).DragLeave += OnTabGroupControlDragLeave;
				((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).DragOver += OnTabGroupControlDragOver;
				if (CanSetAllowDrop && allowExternalDrop)
				{
					((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).AllowDrop = true;
				}
				TabGroupControls.Add(tabGroup, mdiTabGroupControl);
				MdiParent.Controls.Add((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl);
				MdiParent.Controls.SetChildIndex((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl, 0);
			}
		}

		private void CreateSplitterControls()
		{
			if (!((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
			{
				SplitterBarControl splitterBarControl = SplitterControls[nestedTabGroup] as SplitterBarControl;
				bool flag = TabGroupManager.NeedsSplitter(nestedTabGroup);
				if ((splitterBarControl != null || flag) && !(splitterBarControl != null && flag))
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						CreateSplitterControl(nestedTabGroup);
					}
					else
					{
						RemoveSplitterControl(nestedTabGroup);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void CreateSplitterControl(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			//IL_002d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled)
			{
				SplitterBarControl splitterBarControl = CreateSplitterBarControl();
