				if (splitterBarControl == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_44"));
				}
				((Control)(object)splitterBarControl).Visible = false;
				splitterBarControl.BorderStyle = SplitterBorderStyleResolved;
				splitterBarControl.Orientation = tabGroup.ParentOrientation;
				splitterBarControl.ResolveAppearanceCallback = ((splitterBarControl.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical) ? SplitterAppearanceCallbackVert : SplitterAppearanceCallbackHorz);
				splitterBarControl.SplitterDragging += OnSplitterBarDragging;
				splitterBarControl.SplitterDragged += OnSplitterBarDragged;
				splitterBarControl.SplitterMove += OnSplitterBarMove;
				SplitterControls.Add(tabGroup, splitterBarControl);
				MdiParent.Controls.Add((Control)(object)splitterBarControl);
				MdiParent.Controls.SetChildIndex((Control)(object)splitterBarControl, 0);
			}
		}

		private void RemoveSplitterControls()
		{
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
			{
				RemoveSplitterControl(nestedTabGroup);
			}
		}

		private void RemoveSplitterControl(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (SplitterControls[tabGroup] is SplitterBarControl splitterBarControl)
			{
				splitterBarControl.SplitterDragging -= OnSplitterBarDragging;
				splitterBarControl.SplitterDragged -= OnSplitterBarDragged;
				splitterBarControl.SplitterMove -= OnSplitterBarMove;
				SplitterControls.Remove(tabGroup);
				((Component)(object)splitterBarControl).Dispose();
			}
		}

		private void OnTabGroupControlFontChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is MdiTabGroupControl { TabGroup: not null } mdiTabGroupControl)
			{
				mdiTabGroupControl.TabGroup.DirtyTabManager(MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.Font);
			}
		}

		private void OnSplitterBarDragging(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
		{
			MdiTabGroup tabGroupFromSplitter = GetTabGroupFromSplitter(sender as SplitterBarControl);
			if (tabGroupFromSplitter == null)
			{
				e.Cancel = true;
				return;
			}
			MdiSplitterDraggingEventArgs e2 = new MdiSplitterDraggingEventArgs(tabGroupFromSplitter);
			InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.SplitterDragging, e2);
			if (e2.Cancel)
			{
				e.Cancel = true;
			}
			minDelta = -Math.Max(0, tabGroupFromSplitter.Extent - GetMinimumTabGroupExtent(tabGroupFromSplitter));
			int index = tabGroupFromSplitter.Index + 1;
			maxDelta = Math.Max(0, tabGroupFromSplitter.ParentCollection[index].Extent - GetMinimumTabGroupExtent(tabGroupFromSplitter.ParentCollection[index]));
		}

		private void OnSplitterBarDragged(object sender, SplitterDraggedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!e.Cancelled)
			{
				MdiTabGroup tabGroupFromSplitter = GetTabGroupFromSplitter(sender as SplitterBarControl);
				if (tabGroupFromSplitter != null)
				{
					int num = ((tabGroupFromSplitter.ParentOrientation != Orientation.Horizontal) ? (e.MousePosition.X - e.OriginalMousePosition.X) : (e.MousePosition.Y - e.OriginalMousePosition.Y));
					UpdateTabGroupExtent(tabGroupFromSplitter, tabGroupFromSplitter.Extent + num);
					MdiTabGroupEventArgs e2 = new MdiTabGroupEventArgs(tabGroupFromSplitter);
					InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.SplitterDragged, e2);
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnSplitterBarMove(object sender, SplitterMoveEventArgs e)
		{
			bool flag = ((SplitterBarControl)sender).Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal;
			int num = ((!flag) ? (e.MousePosition.X - e.OriginalMousePosition.X) : (e.MousePosition.Y - e.OriginalMousePosition.Y));
			if (minDelta > num)
			{
				Rectangle splitRectangle = e.SplitRectangle;
				if (flag)
				{
					splitRectangle.Offset(0, minDelta - num);
				}
				else
				{
					splitRectangle.Offset(minDelta - num, 0);
				}
				e.SplitRectangle = splitRectangle;
			}
			else if (maxDelta < num)
			{
				Rectangle splitRectangle2 = e.SplitRectangle;
				if (flag)
				{
					splitRectangle2.Offset(0, maxDelta - num);
				}
				else
				{
					splitRectangle2.Offset(maxDelta - num, 0);
				}
				e.SplitRectangle = splitRectangle2;
			}
			if (HasSplitterAppearance && SplitterAppearance.HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)137438953472L))
			{
				e.DragCursor = SplitterAppearance.Cursor;
			}
		}

		private void OnTabGroupControlDragEnter(object sender, DragEventArgs e)
		{
			DragDropHelper.DragEnter(sender as MdiTabGroupControl, e);
		}

		private void OnTabGroupControlDragLeave(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			DragDropHelper.DragLeave(sender as MdiTabGroupControl);
		}

		private void OnTabGroupControlDragOver(object sender, DragEventArgs e)
		{
			DragDropHelper.DragOver(sender as MdiTabGroupControl, e);
		}

		private void OnTabGroupControlDragDrop(object sender, DragEventArgs e)
		{
			DragDropHelper.DragDrop(sender as MdiTabGroupControl, e);
		}

		private void PositionAndSizeTabGroups()
		{
			if (IsLayoutSuspendedResolved || ((Component)this).DesignMode)
			{
				return;
			}
			MdiClient mdiClient = this.mdiClient;
			Form form = MdiParent;
			if (mdiClient == null || this.mdiClient.Disposing || this.mdiClient.IsDisposed)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (invokeTabGroupResized)
			{
				invokeTabGroupResized = false;
				if (!AreTabGroupExtentsAllocated())
				{
					InitializeTabGroupExtents(invokeResizeEvents: true);
					return;
				}
				InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabGroupResized, new MdiTabGroupResizedEventArgs(TabGroups));
			}
			else
			{
				VerifyTabGroupExtents();
			}
			if (!((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled || !HasTabGroups)
			{
				return;
			}
			form.SuspendLayout();
			mdiClient.SuspendLayout();
			positioningControls = true;
			try
			{
				CreateTabGroupControls();
				CreateSplitterControls();
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList(TabGroupsWithTabsCount * 2);
				foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
				{
					if (SplitterControls[nestedTabGroup] is SplitterBarControl splitterBarControl)
					{
						splitterBarControl.SuppressLeadingEdge = SuppressSplitterLeadingEdge;
						Rectangle splitterControlRectangle = TabGroupManager.GetSplitterControlRectangle(nestedTabGroup);
						((Control)(object)splitterBarControl).Bounds = splitterControlRectangle;
						splitterBarControl.Orientation = nestedTabGroup.ParentOrientation;
						splitterBarControl.ResolveAppearanceCallback = ((splitterBarControl.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical) ? SplitterAppearanceCallbackVert : SplitterAppearanceCallbackHorz);
						arrayList.Add(splitterBarControl);
					}
					if (!nestedTabGroup.HasTabs)
					{
						continue;
					}
					MdiTabGroupControl mdiTabGroupControl = TabGroupControls[nestedTabGroup] as MdiTabGroupControl;
					Rectangle tabGroupControlRectangle = TabGroupManager.GetTabGroupControlRectangle(nestedTabGroup);
					((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).Bounds = tabGroupControlRectangle;
					arrayList.Add(mdiTabGroupControl);
					Rectangle formPosition = TabGroupManager.GetFormPosition(nestedTabGroup);
					foreach (MdiTab tab in nestedTabGroup.Tabs)
					{
						tab.Form.Bounds = formPosition;
					}
				}
				RefreshSplitterIntersections();
				foreach (Control item in arrayList)
				{
					item.Visible = true;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				positioningControls = false;
				mdiClient.ResumeLayout();
				form.ResumeLayout();
			}
		}

		private void ReleaseMdiTabsAndForms()
		{
			isReleasingTabs = true;
			try
			{
				Form form = MdiParent;
				Control control = mdiClient;
				form?.SuspendLayout();
				control?.SuspendLayout();
				RemoveSplitterControls();
				RemoveTabGroupControls();
				InitializeMdiTabForms(restore: true);
				foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
				{
					if (nestedTabGroup.HasTabs)
					{
						foreach (MdiTab tab in nestedTabGroup.Tabs)
						{
							tab.InitializeOwner(null);
						}
					}
					nestedTabGroup.InitializeTabbedMdiManager(null);
					nestedTabGroup.Tabs.Clear();
				}
				TabGroups.Clear();
				foreach (MdiTab hiddenTab in HiddenTabs)
				{
					hiddenTab.InitializeOwner(null);
				}
				HiddenTabs.Clear();
				TabGroupManager.RemoveUnusedTabGroups();
				form?.ResumeLayout();
				control?.ResumeLayout();
			}
			finally
			{
				isReleasingTabs = false;
			}
		}

		private void EnsureActiveTabIsSelected(bool addToGroupIfNecessary)
		{
			MdiTab activeTab = ActiveTab;
			if (activeTab == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (activeTab.TabGroup == null)
			{
				if (addToGroupIfNecessary && activeTab.Form != null && activeTab.Form.Visible)
				{
					AddTabToTabGroup(activeTab);
				}
			}
			else if (activeTab.TabGroup.SelectedTab != activeTab)
			{
				ActivateMdiChild(activeTab, invokeTabSelecting: true, invokeTabSelected: true, allowCancel: false, activate: false);
			}
		}

		private void EnsureTabIsSelected(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, bool allowEventNotifications)
		{
			if (tabGroup == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			MdiTab selectedTab = tabGroup.SelectedTab;
			MdiTab activeTab = ActiveTab;
			MdiTab mdiTab = ((activeTab == null || activeTab.TabGroup != tabGroup) ? GetTopMostTab(tabGroup) : activeTab);
			if (selectedTab == mdiTab)
			{
				return;
			}
			ensuringTabIsSelected = true;
			try
			{
				if (allowEventNotifications)
				{
					tabGroup.SelectedTab = mdiTab;
				}
				else
				{
					tabGroup.InitializeSelectedTab(mdiTab);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				ensuringTabIsSelected = false;
			}
			TabGroupManager.DirtyTabGroup(tabGroup);
		}

		private MdiTab GetTopMostTab(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (tabGroup == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (mdiClient == null)
			{
				return tabGroup.TabManager.FirstVisibleTabItem as MdiTab;
			}
			MdiTab result = null;
			int num = int.MaxValue;
			foreach (MdiTab tab in tabGroup.Tabs)
			{
				int childIndex = mdiClient.Controls.GetChildIndex(tab.Form, throwException: false);
				if (childIndex >= 0 && childIndex < num)
				{
					result = tab;
					num = childIndex;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void AddTabToTabGroup(MdiTab tab)
		{
			MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = ActiveTabGroup;
			if (mdiTabGroup == null)
			{
				mdiTabGroup = PreviousActiveTabGroup;
			}
			if (mdiTabGroup == null)
			{
				mdiTabGroup = ((TabGroups.Count <= 0) ? CreateNewTabGroup(0, AvailableMdiClientExtent, null) : GetFirstLeafTabGroup(TabGroups));
			}
			MoveTab(tab, mdiTabGroup, mdiTabGroup.Tabs.Count, tab.TabGroup == null, tab.TabGroup != null);
			mdiTabGroup.Manager.GetTabGroupControl(mdiTabGroup)?.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder, -1);
		}

		private MdiTabGroup CreateNewTabGroup(int index, int initialExtent, MdiTabGroup owningTabGroup)
		{
			MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = CreateMdiTabGroup();
			MdiTabGroupsCollection mdiTabGroupsCollection = ((owningTabGroup == null) ? TabGroups : owningTabGroup.TabGroups);
			mdiTabGroupsCollection.Insert(index, mdiTabGroup);
			mdiTabGroup.InitializeExtent(initialExtent);
			mdiTabGroup.DirtyTabManager(MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.AllMetrics);
			mdiTabGroup.InitializeParentTabGroup(owningTabGroup);
			TabGroupManager.DirtyAllTabGroups();
			InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.InitializeTabGroup, new MdiTabGroupEventArgs(mdiTabGroup));
			return mdiTabGroup;
		}

		private void RemoveTabGroupTabItem(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, MdiTab tab)
		{
			if (tabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab != tab)
			{
				tabGroup.Tabs.Remove(tab);
			}
			else
			{
				int index = ((ITabItem)tab).Index;
				tabGroup.Tabs.Remove(tab);
				if (tabGroup.Tabs.Count == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				int index2 = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(tabGroup.Tabs.Count - 1, index));
				tabGroup.FirstDisplayedTab = tabGroup.Tabs[index2];
			}
			tabGroup.Manager.GetTabGroupControl(tabGroup)?.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder, -1);
		}

		private bool MoveTab(MdiTab tab, MdiTabGroup newTabGroup, int tabIndex, bool invokeDisplayEvents, bool invokeTabMoveEvents)
		{
			if (invokeTabMoveEvents)
			{
				MdiTabMovingEventArgs e = new MdiTabMovingEventArgs(tab, newTabGroup, tabIndex);
				InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabMoving, e);
				if (e.Cancel)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			MdiTabGroup tabGroup = tab.TabGroup;
			SuspendLayout();
			MdiTabEventArgs e2 = null;
			try
			{
				if (tabGroup != null && tabGroup == newTabGroup)
				{
					RepositionTabInTabGroup(tab, tabIndex);
				}
				else
				{
					if (tabGroup == null)
					{
						HiddenTabs.Remove(tab);
					}
					else
					{
						RemoveTabGroupTabItem(tabGroup, tab);
					}
					newTabGroup.Tabs.Insert(tabIndex, tab);
					tab.InitializeOwner(newTabGroup);
					EnsureTabIsSelected(newTabGroup, allowEventNotifications: true);
					EnsureTabIsSelected(tabGroup, allowEventNotifications: true);
					if (invokeDisplayEvents)
					{
						e2 = new MdiTabEventArgs(tab);
						InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabDisplaying, e2);
					}
					RemoveEmptyTabGroups();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				CacheLastActiveTabGroup();
				VerifyMaximizedTabGroup();
				ResumeLayout(positionControls: true);
			}
			if (e2 != null)
			{
				InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabDisplayed, e2);
			}
			if (invokeTabMoveEvents)
			{
				MdiTabEventArgs e3 = new MdiTabEventArgs(tab);
				InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabMoved, e3);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void RemoveTabFromTabGroup(MdiTab tab)
		{
			if (tab == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			MdiTabGroup tabGroup = tab.TabGroup;
			if (tabGroup != null)
			{
				RemoveTabGroupTabItem(tabGroup, tab);
			}
			if (tab.Disposed || tab.MdiChild == null || !tab.MdiChild.IsMdiChild || tab.MdiChild.IsDisposed || tab.MdiChild.Disposing)
			{
				tab.MdiChild = null;
				if (HiddenTabs.Contains(tab))
				{
					HiddenTabs.Remove(tab);
				}
				tab.InitializeOwner(null);
				if (cachedActiveTab == tab)
				{
					cachedActiveTab = null;
				}
				if (lastActivatedTab == tab)
				{
					lastActivatedTab = null;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				tab.InitializeOwner(this);
				if (!HiddenTabs.Contains(tab))
				{
					HiddenTabs.Add(tab);
				}
			}
			if (tabGroup != null)
			{
				if (tabGroup.HasTabs)
				{
					if (tabGroup.SelectedTab == null || tabGroup.SelectedTab == tab)
					{
						EnsureTabIsSelected(tabGroup, allowEventNotifications: false);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					RemoveEmptyTabGroups();
				}
			}
			CacheLastActiveTabGroup();
		}

		private void CreateTabsForForms(Form[] forms)
		{
			MdiTab[] array = CreateMdiTabs(forms);
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MdiTab mdiTab in array)
			{
				if (mdiTab.IsCustomTab)
				{
					mdiTab.PersistedInfo = mdiTab.CustomMdiTab.UniqueId;
				}
				mdiTab.InitializeOwner(this);
				HiddenTabs.Add(mdiTab);
				Form mdiChild = mdiTab.MdiChild;
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
			{
				MdiTabEventArgs e = new MdiTabEventArgs(array[j]);
				InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.InitializeTab, e);
			}
			if (((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled)
			{
				MdiTab[] array2 = array;
				foreach (MdiTab tab in array2)
				{
					InitializeTabFormSettings(tab);
				}
			}
			MdiTab[] array3 = array;
			foreach (MdiTab mdiTab2 in array3)
			{
				if (mdiTab2.IsFormVisible && mdiTab2.TabGroup == null)
				{
					AddTabToTabGroup(mdiTab2);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RemoveEmptyTabGroups()
		{
			if (!HasTabGroups)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			try
			{
				bool flag2 = false;
				do
				{
					MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = GetLastLeafTabGroup(TabGroups);
					while (mdiTabGroup != null)
					{
						if (mdiTabGroup.HasTabs)
						{
							mdiTabGroup = mdiTabGroup.PreviousLeafTabGroup;
							continue;
						}
						MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup2 = mdiTabGroup;
						MdiTabGroup parent = mdiTabGroup2.Parent;
						while (parent != null && !parent.HasTabsResolved)
						{
							mdiTabGroup2 = parent;
							parent = parent.Parent;
						}
						if (!flag)
						{
							flag = true;
							SuspendLayout();
						}
						flag2 = true;
						mdiTabGroup = mdiTabGroup2.PreviousLeafTabGroup;
						RemoveEmptyTabGroup(mdiTabGroup2);
					}
				}
				while (RemoveUnnecessaryGroupLevels());
				if (flag)
				{
					EnsureActiveTabIsSelected(addToGroupIfNecessary: true);
					InitializeTabGroupExtents(invokeResizeEvents: true);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					ResumeLayout(positionControls: true);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RemoveEmptyTabGroup(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			RemoveEmptyTabGroup(tabGroup, isParentBeingRemoved: false);
		}

		private void RemoveEmptyTabGroup(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, bool isParentBeingRemoved)
		{
			if (tabGroup == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (tabGroup.HasTabGroups)
			{
				foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup2 in tabGroup.TabGroups)
				{
					RemoveEmptyTabGroup(tabGroup2, isParentBeingRemoved: true);
				}
			}
			if (!isParentBeingRemoved && tabGroup.ParentCollection.Count > 1)
			{
				MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = TabGroupFromPosition(tabGroup, MdiTabGroupPosition.Previous);
				if (mdiTabGroup == null || mdiTabGroup == tabGroup)
				{
					mdiTabGroup = TabGroupFromPosition(tabGroup, MdiTabGroupPosition.Next);
				}
				if (mdiTabGroup != null && mdiTabGroup != tabGroup)
				{
					mdiTabGroup.InitializeExtent(tabGroup.ExtentForAdjustments + mdiTabGroup.ExtentForAdjustments);
				}
			}
			tabGroup.ParentCollection.Remove(tabGroup);
			tabGroup.InitializeTabbedMdiManager(null);
			tabGroup.InitializeParentTabGroup(null);
			RemoveTabGroupControl(tabGroup);
			RemoveSplitterControl(tabGroup);
			if (!isParentBeingRemoved)
			{
				TabGroupManager.RemoveUnusedTabGroups();
			}
		}

		private bool RemoveUnnecessaryGroupLevels()
		{
			SuspendLayout();
			try
			{
				bool flag = false;
				if (TabGroups.Count == 1 && TabGroups[0].HasTabGroups)
				{
					MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = TabGroups[0];
					int count = mdiTabGroup.TabGroups.Count;
					for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
					{
						ReparentTabGroup(mdiTabGroup.TabGroups[num], null, 1);
					}
					mdiTabGroup.InitializeExtent(0);
					Orientation = ((Orientation != Orientation.Vertical) ? Orientation.Vertical : Orientation.Horizontal);
					flag = true;
				}
				return RemoveUnnecessaryGroupLevelsHelper(null) || flag;
			}
			finally
			{
				ResumeLayout(positionControls: true);
			}
		}

		private bool RemoveUnnecessaryGroupLevelsHelper(MdiTabGroup parent)
		{
			MdiTabGroupsCollection mdiTabGroupsCollection = ((parent == null) ? TabGroups : parent.TabGroups);
			int num = 0;
			bool result = false;
			while (num < mdiTabGroupsCollection.Count)
			{
				MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = mdiTabGroupsCollection[num++];
				if (mdiTabGroup.HasTabs)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (mdiTabGroup.HasTabGroups && mdiTabGroup.TabGroups.Count == 1)
				{
					if (!mdiTabGroup.TabGroups[0].HasTabGroups)
					{
						MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup2 = mdiTabGroup.TabGroups[0];
						ReparentTabGroup(mdiTabGroup2, parent, num);
						mdiTabGroup2.InitializeExtent(mdiTabGroup.Extent);
						if (IsActiveTabGroupMaximized && MaximizedTabGroup != null)
						{
							mdiTabGroup2.CachePreMaximizedExtent(mdiTabGroup.ExtentForAdjustments);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						int count = mdiTabGroup.TabGroups[0].TabGroups.Count;
						for (int num2 = count - 1; num2 >= 0; num2--)
						{
							MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup3 = mdiTabGroup.TabGroups[0].TabGroups[num2];
							bool flag = TabGroupManager.NeedsSplitter(mdiTabGroup) && !TabGroupManager.NeedsSplitter(mdiTabGroup3) && num2 == count - 1;
							ReparentTabGroup(mdiTabGroup3, parent, num);
							if (flag)
							{
								mdiTabGroup3.InitializeExtent(mdiTabGroup3.Extent + SplitterWidthResolved);
								if (IsActiveTabGroupMaximized && MaximizedTabGroup != null)
								{
									mdiTabGroup3.CachePreMaximizedExtent(mdiTabGroup3.ExtentForAdjustments + SplitterWidthResolved);
								}
							}
						}
					}
					result = true;
					mdiTabGroup.InitializeExtent(0);
				}
				else if (RemoveUnnecessaryGroupLevelsHelper(mdiTabGroup))
				{
					result = true;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void ReparentTabGroup(MdiTabGroup groupToMove, MdiTabGroup newParent, int newIndex)
		{
			if (groupToMove.ParentCollection != null)
			{
				groupToMove.ParentCollection.Remove(groupToMove);
			}
			MdiTabGroupsCollection mdiTabGroupsCollection = ((newParent == null) ? TabGroups : newParent.TabGroups);
			mdiTabGroupsCollection.Insert(newIndex, groupToMove);
			groupToMove.InitializeParentTabGroup(newParent);
		}

		private void RepositionTabInTabGroup(MdiTab tab, int newIndex)
		{
			if (tab != null && tab.TabGroup != null && ((ITabItem)tab).Index != newIndex)
			{
				tab.TabGroup.Tabs.Reposition(tab, newIndex);
				tab.TabGroup.Manager.GetTabGroupControl(tab.TabGroup)?.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder, -1);
			}
		}

		private void SplitTabGroup(MdiTab tabToAdd, MdiTabGroup groupToSplit, RelativePosition relation, Orientation orientation)
		{
			//IL_0078: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007a: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00cd: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_012c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00f2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!tabToAdd.IsFormVisible)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_35"));
			}
			if (TabGroups.Count == 0)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_46"));
			}
			if (!CanCreateNewGroup && (tabToAdd.TabGroup == null || tabToAdd.TabGroup.Tabs.Count != 1))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_47"));
			}
			int num = groupToSplit?.Index ?? 0;
			if ((int)relation == 1)
			{
				num++;
			}
			Orientation orientation2 = groupToSplit?.ParentOrientation ?? Orientation;
			bool flag = orientation2 != orientation;
			if (flag && TabGroups.Count == 1 && groupToSplit == TabGroups[0])
			{
				Orientation = orientation;
				flag = false;
			}
			if (groupToSplit == null && !flag)
			{
				flag = false;
				num = (((int)relation != 0) ? TabGroups.Count : 0);
			}
			else if (flag)
			{
				if (groupToSplit != null && groupToSplit.HasTabGroups)
				{
					flag = false;
					if ((int)relation == 0)
					{
						groupToSplit = groupToSplit.TabGroups[0];
						num = 0;
					}
					else
					{
						groupToSplit = groupToSplit.TabGroups[groupToSplit.TabGroups.Count - 1];
						num = groupToSplit.Index + 1;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					num = (((int)relation != 0) ? 1 : 0);
				}
			}
			SuspendLayout();
			try
			{
				MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = null;
				MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup2;
				if (!flag)
				{
					mdiTabGroup2 = ((groupToSplit != null) ? CreateNewTabGroup(num, 0, groupToSplit.Parent) : CreateNewTabGroup(num, 0, null));
				}
				else
				{
					int index = groupToSplit?.Index ?? 0;
					int num2 = groupToSplit?.ExtentForAdjustments ?? CommonMdiClientExtent;
					mdiTabGroup = CreateNewTabGroup(index, num2, groupToSplit?.Parent);
					if (IsActiveTabGroupMaximized && MaximizedTabGroup != null)
					{
						mdiTabGroup.CachePreMaximizedExtent(num2);
					}
					if (groupToSplit == null)
					{
						Orientation = orientation;
					}
					if (groupToSplit == null)
					{
						for (int num3 = TabGroups.Count - 1; num3 >= 1; num3--)
						{
							MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup3 = TabGroups[num3];
							if (mdiTabGroup3 != mdiTabGroup)
							{
								ReparentTabGroup(mdiTabGroup3, mdiTabGroup, mdiTabGroup.Index);
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						ReparentTabGroup(groupToSplit, mdiTabGroup, mdiTabGroup.TabGroups.Count);
					}
					mdiTabGroup2 = CreateNewTabGroup(num, 0, (groupToSplit == null) ? null : mdiTabGroup);
				}
				int num4 = groupToSplit?.ExtentForAdjustments ?? 0;
				int tabIndex = 0;
				MdiTabMovingEventArgs e = new MdiTabMovingEventArgs(tabToAdd, mdiTabGroup2, tabIndex);
				InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabMoving, e);
				if (e.Cancel)
				{
					RemoveEmptyTabGroups();
					return;
				}
				if (groupToSplit == null)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						int num5 = AvailableMdiClientExtent / CalculateTabGroupRowsColsHelper(null, Orientation);
						MdiTabGroupsCollection mdiTabGroupsCollection = new MdiTabGroupsCollection(new MdiTabGroup[2] { mdiTabGroup, mdiTabGroup2 });
						int[] newExtents = new int[2]
						{
							AvailableMdiClientExtent - num5,
							num5
						};
						UpdateTabGroupExtentsHelper(mdiTabGroupsCollection, newExtents, invokeEvents: true);
					}
					else
					{
						int extent = AvailableMdiClientExtent / (CalculateTabGroupRowsColsHelper(null, Orientation) - 1);
						mdiTabGroup2.InitializeExtent(extent);
						InitializeTabGroupExtents(invokeResizeEvents: true);
					}
				}
				else if (groupToSplit.Manager == null)
				{
					MdiTabGroupsCollection mdiTabGroupsCollection2 = new MdiTabGroupsCollection(new MdiTabGroup[1] { mdiTabGroup2 });
					int[] newExtents2 = new int[1] { num4 };
					UpdateTabGroupExtentsHelper(mdiTabGroupsCollection2, newExtents2, invokeEvents: true);
				}
				else
				{
					int num6 = num4 / 2;
					MdiTabGroupsCollection mdiTabGroupsCollection3 = new MdiTabGroupsCollection(new MdiTabGroup[2] { groupToSplit, mdiTabGroup2 });
					int[] newExtents3 = new int[2]
					{
						num4 - num6,
						num6
					};
					UpdateTabGroupExtentsHelper(mdiTabGroupsCollection3, newExtents3, invokeEvents: true);
				}
				MoveTab(tabToAdd, mdiTabGroup2, tabIndex, invokeDisplayEvents: false, invokeTabMoveEvents: false);
				MdiTabEventArgs e2 = new MdiTabEventArgs(tabToAdd);
				InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabMoved, e2);
			}
			finally
			{
				ResumeLayout(positionControls: true);
			}
		}

		private int GetMinimumTabGroupExtent(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			int num = 1;
			if (tabGroup != null && tabGroup.HasTabGroups)
			{
				num = CalculateTabGroupRowsColsHelper(tabGroup, tabGroup.ParentOrientation);
			}
			return SplitterWidthResolved * num;
		}

		internal void UpdateTabGroupExtent(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, int newExtent)
		{
			if (((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).IsUpdating)
			{
				invokeTabGroupResized = true;
				tabGroup.InitializeExtent(newExtent);
				return;
			}
			currentTabGroupExtentVersion++;
			int index = tabGroup.Index;
			int count = tabGroup.ParentCollection.Count;
			if (count == 1)
			{
				int[] newExtents = new int[1] { AvailableMdiClientExtent };
				MdiTabGroupsCollection mdiTabGroupsCollection = new MdiTabGroupsCollection(new MdiTabGroup[1] { tabGroup });
				UpdateTabGroupExtentsHelper(mdiTabGroupsCollection, newExtents, invokeEvents: true);
				return;
			}
			int minimumTabGroupExtent = GetMinimumTabGroupExtent(tabGroup);
			if (newExtent <= minimumTabGroupExtent)
			{
				newExtent = minimumTabGroupExtent;
			}
			int num = newExtent - tabGroup.ExtentForAdjustments;
			int index2 = tabGroup.Index;
			int index3 = ((index2 == count - 1) ? (index2 - 1) : (index2 + 1));
			MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup = tabGroup.ParentCollection[index3];
			int minimumTabGroupExtent2 = GetMinimumTabGroupExtent(mdiTabGroup);
			int extentForAdjustments = mdiTabGroup.ExtentForAdjustments;
			extentForAdjustments -= num;
			if (extentForAdjustments <= minimumTabGroupExtent2)
			{
				newExtent += extentForAdjustments;
				extentForAdjustments = minimumTabGroupExtent2;
			}
			int[] newExtents2 = new int[2] { newExtent, extentForAdjustments };
			MdiTabGroupsCollection mdiTabGroupsCollection2 = new MdiTabGroupsCollection(new MdiTabGroup[2] { tabGroup, mdiTabGroup });
			UpdateTabGroupExtentsHelper(mdiTabGroupsCollection2, newExtents2, invokeEvents: true);
		}

		private bool UpdateTabGroupExtentsHelper(MdiTabGroupsCollection tabGroups, int[] newExtents, bool invokeEvents)
		{
			TabGroupExtentInfoCollection tabGroupExtentInfoCollection = new TabGroupExtentInfoCollection(10);
			for (int i = 0; i < tabGroups.Count; i++)
			{
				tabGroupExtentInfoCollection.Add(tabGroups[i], newExtents[i]);
			}
			if (IsActiveTabGroupMaximized && MaximizedTabGroup != null)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < tabGroups.Count; j++)
				{
					tabGroups[j].CachePreMaximizedExtent(newExtents[j]);
				}
			}
			for (int k = 0; k < tabGroups.Count; k++)
			{
				TabGroupExtentInfo tabGroupExtentInfo = tabGroupExtentInfoCollection[k];
				if (tabGroupExtentInfo.TabGroup.HasTabGroups)
				{
					int num = tabGroupExtentInfo.NewExtent;
					if (TabGroupManager.NeedsSplitter(tabGroupExtentInfo.TabGroup))
					{
						num -= SplitterWidthResolved;
					}
					InitializeTabGroupExtentsHelper(tabGroupExtentInfoCollection, tabGroupExtentInfo.TabGroup.TabGroups, CalculateCommonExtent(tabGroupExtentInfo.TabGroup.Parent), num);
				}
			}
			return UpdateTabGroupExtentsHelper(tabGroupExtentInfoCollection, invokeEvents);
		}

		private bool UpdateTabGroupExtentsHelper(TabGroupExtentInfoCollection extentInfos, bool invokeEvents)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (TabGroupExtentInfo item in (IEnumerable)extentInfos)
			{
				if (item.IsExtentDifferent)
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int num = currentTabGroupExtentVersion;
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (invokeEvents)
			{
				MdiTabGroupResizingEventArgs e = extentInfos.CreateTabGroupResizingArgs();
				InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabGroupResizing, e);
				flag2 = e.Cancel;
			}
			if (flag2 || currentTabGroupExtentVersion != num)
			{
				return false;
			}
			SuspendLayout();
			try
			{
				foreach (TabGroupExtentInfo item2 in (IEnumerable)extentInfos)
				{
					item2.TabGroup.InitializeExtent(item2.NewExtent);
				}
				VerifyTabGroupExtents();
			}
			finally
			{
				ResumeLayout(positionControls: true);
			}
			if (invokeEvents)
			{
				MdiTabGroupResizedEventArgs e2 = new MdiTabGroupResizedEventArgs(tabGroups);
				InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.TabGroupResized, e2);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void InitializeTabGroupExtents(bool invokeResizeEvents)
		{
			InitializeTabGroupExtents(invokeResizeEvents, force: false);
		}

		private void InitializeTabGroupExtents(bool invokeResizeEvents, bool force)
		{
			if (!suspendExtentInitialization && HasTabGroups && !IsInitializing && (force || !AreTabGroupExtentsAllocated()))
			{
				TabGroupExtentInfoCollection tabGroupExtentInfoCollection = new TabGroupExtentInfoCollection();
				InitializeTabGroupExtentsHelper(tabGroupExtentInfoCollection, TabGroups, AvailableMdiClientExtent, CommonMdiClientExtent);
				UpdateTabGroupExtentsHelper(tabGroupExtentInfoCollection, invokeResizeEvents);
			}
		}

		private void InitializeTabGroupExtentsHelper(TabGroupExtentInfoCollection tabGroupExtentList, MdiTabGroupsCollection tabGroups, int sizableExtent, int commonExtent)
		{
			int count = tabGroups.Count;
			int[] array = new int[count];
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			int num4 = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (tabGroups[i].IsMinimized)
				{
					num2++;
					if (TabGroupManager.NeedsSplitter(tabGroups[i]))
					{
						num3++;
					}
				}
				array[i] = tabGroups[i].Extent + 1;
				num += array[i];
				num4 += tabGroups[i].Extent;
			}
			if (num4 != sizableExtent)
			{
				int num5 = sizableExtent;
				int[] array2 = new int[count];
				if (num2 > 0 && num2 < tabGroups.Count)
				{
					int num6 = num5 - num3 * SplitterWidthResolved;
					int num7 = tabGroups.Count - num2;
					for (int j = 0; j < count; j++)
					{
						if (tabGroups[j].IsMinimized)
						{
							array2[j] = ((num3 != 0 && TabGroupManager.NeedsSplitter(tabGroups[j])) ? SplitterWidthResolved : 0);
						}
						else
						{
							array2[j] = num6 / num7;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (num2 > 0 && num2 == tabGroups.Count)
				{
					array2.Initialize();
				}
				else
				{
					for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
					{
						int num8;
						if (num == 0)
						{
							num8 = 0;
						}
						else
						{
							num8 = (int)Math.Round((decimal)array[k] / (decimal)num * (decimal)sizableExtent);
							if (k == count - 1 && num8 > num5)
							{
								num8 = num5;
							}
						}
						array2[k] = num8;
						num5 -= num8;
					}
					if (num5 > 0)
					{
						int num9 = num5 / count;
						for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
						{
							int num10 = ((l != count - 1) ? num9 : num5);
							array2[l] += num10;
							num5 -= num10;
						}
					}
				}
				for (int m = 0; m < array2.Length; m++)
				{
					if (array2[m] != tabGroups[m].Extent)
					{
						tabGroupExtentList.Add(tabGroups[m], array2[m]);
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup in tabGroups)
			{
				if (tabGroup.HasTabGroups)
				{
					int num11 = tabGroupExtentList[tabGroup]?.NewExtent ?? tabGroup.Extent;
					if (TabGroupManager.NeedsSplitter(tabGroup))
					{
						num11 -= SplitterWidthResolved;
					}
					InitializeTabGroupExtentsHelper(tabGroupExtentList, tabGroup.TabGroups, commonExtent, num11);
				}
			}
		}

		private void VerifyTabGroupExtents()
		{
			if (AreTabGroupExtentsAllocated())
			{
				return;
			}
			SuspendLayout();
			try
			{
				InitializeTabGroupExtents(invokeResizeEvents: false);
			}
			finally
			{
				ResumeLayout(positionControls: false);
			}
		}

		private bool AreTabGroupExtentsAllocated()
		{
			return AreTabGroupExtentsAllocatedHelper(TabGroups, AvailableMdiClientExtent);
		}

		private bool AreTabGroupExtentsAllocatedHelper(MdiTabGroupsCollection tabGroups, int availableExtent)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup in tabGroups)
			{
				num += tabGroup.Extent;
			}
			if (availableExtent != num)
			{
				return false;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup tabGroup2 in tabGroups)
			{
				if (tabGroup2.HasTabGroups && !AreTabGroupExtentsAllocatedHelper(tabGroup2.TabGroups, CalculateSizableExtent(tabGroup2)))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private int CalculateTabGroupRowsColsHelper(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, Orientation orientation)
		{
			if (tabGroup != null && !tabGroup.HasTabGroups)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			MdiTabGroupsCollection mdiTabGroupsCollection = ((tabGroup == null) ? TabGroups : tabGroup.TabGroups);
			int num = 0;
			foreach (MdiTabGroup item in mdiTabGroupsCollection)
			{
				num = ((item.ParentOrientation != orientation) ? Math.Max(num, CalculateTabGroupRowsColsHelper(item, orientation)) : (num + CalculateTabGroupRowsColsHelper(item, orientation)));
			}
			return num;
		}

		private void RefreshMaximizedTabGroupExtents()
		{
			if (MaximizedTabGroup == null || IsInitializing)
			{
				return;
			}
			SuspendLayout();
			try
			{
				foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
				{
					nestedTabGroup.InitializeExtent(-1);
				}
				DirtyTabGroups(TabbedMdiPropertyIds.Extent);
				InitializeTabGroupExtents(invokeResizeEvents: false, force: true);
			}
			finally
			{
				ResumeLayout(positionControls: true);
			}
		}

		private MdiTab[] CreateMdiTabs(ICollection forms)
		{
			if (forms == null || forms.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (object form in forms)
			{
				if (form is Form)
				{
					num++;
				}
			}
			if (num == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			MdiTab[] array = new MdiTab[num];
			num = 0;
			foreach (object form2 in forms)
			{
				if (form2 is Form)
				{
					array[num] = CreateMdiTab(form2 as Form);
					num++;
				}
			}
			return array;
		}

		private Font GetBaseFont(MdiTabGroup tabGroup)
		{
			if (TabGroupControls[tabGroup] is MdiTabGroupControl mdiTabGroupControl)
			{
				return ((Control)(object)mdiTabGroupControl).Font;
			}
			if (MdiParent != null)
			{
				return MdiParent.Font;
			}
			if (mdiClient != null)
			{
				return mdiClient.Font;
			}
			return Control.DefaultFont;
		}

		private void InitializeMdiTabForms(bool restore)
		{
			try
			{
				SuspendLayout();
				InitializeMdiTabFormsHelper(restore);
			}
			finally
			{
				ResumeLayout(positionControls: false);
			}
		}

		private void InitializeMdiTabFormsHelper(bool restore)
		{
			if (restore)
			{
				SetRedraw(redrawOn: false);
				foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
				{
					foreach (MdiTab tab in nestedTabGroup.Tabs)
					{
						if (tab.IsCustomTab)
						{
							tab.CustomMdiTab.IsMdiTab = false;
						}
						tab.RestoreTabFormSettings();
					}
				}
				foreach (MdiTab hiddenTab in HiddenTabs)
				{
					if (hiddenTab.IsCustomTab)
					{
						hiddenTab.CustomMdiTab.IsMdiTab = false;
					}
					hiddenTab.RestoreTabFormSettings();
				}
				SetRedraw(redrawOn: true);
				return;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup2 in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
			{
				foreach (MdiTab tab2 in nestedTabGroup2.Tabs)
				{
					InitializeTabFormSettings(tab2);
				}
			}
			foreach (MdiTab hiddenTab2 in HiddenTabs)
			{
				InitializeTabFormSettings(hiddenTab2);
			}
		}

		private void SynchronizeSplitterProperties()
		{
			//IL_0048: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (IsInitializing || !((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
			{
				if (SplitterControls[nestedTabGroup] is SplitterBarControl splitterBarControl)
				{
					splitterBarControl.BorderStyle = SplitterBorderStyleResolved;
					splitterBarControl.Orientation = nestedTabGroup.ParentOrientation;
					splitterBarControl.ResolveAppearanceCallback = ((splitterBarControl.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical) ? SplitterAppearanceCallbackVert : SplitterAppearanceCallbackHorz);
					((Control)(object)splitterBarControl).Enabled = MaximizedTabGroup == null;
				}
			}
		}

		private MdiTabGroup TabGroupFromPosition(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, MdiTabGroupPosition relativePosition)
		{
			MdiTabGroup result = null;
			if (HasTabGroups)
			{
				switch (relativePosition)
				{
				case MdiTabGroupPosition.First:
					result = tabGroup.ParentCollection[0];
					break;
				case MdiTabGroupPosition.Last:
					result = tabGroup.ParentCollection[tabGroup.ParentCollection.Count - 1];
					break;
				case MdiTabGroupPosition.Next:
				case MdiTabGroupPosition.Previous:
				{
					int num = 0;
					if (tabGroup != null)
					{
						num = tabGroup.Index;
					}
					int num2 = ((relativePosition != MdiTabGroupPosition.Next) ? (num - 1) : (num + 1));
					if (num2 < 0)
					{
						num2 = 0;
					}
					else if (num2 > tabGroup.ParentCollection.Count - 1)
					{
						num2 = tabGroup.ParentCollection.Count - 1;
					}
					result = tabGroup.ParentCollection[num2];
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private MdiTab GetTabFromForm(Form form, bool verifyFormIsInitialized, bool verifyTabGroup, bool createIfNull)
		{
			MdiTab tabFromForm = GetTabFromForm(form);
			if (tabFromForm != null)
			{
				if (verifyFormIsInitialized && !tabFromForm.IsFormInitialized)
				{
					InitializeTabFormSettings(tabFromForm);
				}
				bool flag = EventManager.InProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.InitializeTab);
				if (verifyTabGroup && !flag)
				{
					if (tabFromForm.IsFormVisible && tabFromForm.TabGroup == null)
					{
						AddTabToTabGroup(tabFromForm);
					}
					else if (!tabFromForm.IsFormVisible && tabFromForm.TabGroup != null)
					{
						RemoveTabFromTabGroup(tabFromForm);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (createIfNull && MdiParent != null && !IsRestoringTabs && !restoringTabGroup && form != null && form.IsMdiChild && Utilities.GetMdiParent(form) == MdiParent && !form.IsDisposed && !form.Disposing && !IsDisposing)
			{
				CreateTabsForForms(new Form[1] { form });
			}
			return tabFromForm;
		}

		private MdiTab GetTabFromForm(Form form)
		{
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
			{
				foreach (MdiTab tab in nestedTabGroup.Tabs)
				{
					if (tab.Form == form)
					{
						return tab;
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (MdiTab hiddenTab in HiddenTabs)
			{
				if (hiddenTab.Form == form)
				{
					return hiddenTab;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void CacheLastActiveTabGroup()
		{
			MdiTab activeTab = ActiveTab;
			if (activeTab != null && activeTab.TabGroup != null && activeTab.TabGroup != lastActiveTabGroup)
			{
				lastActiveTabGroup = activeTab.TabGroup;
			}
			else if (activeTab == null && lastActiveTabGroup != null && (lastActiveTabGroup.Manager == null || lastActiveTabGroup.Index < 0))
			{
				lastActiveTabGroup = null;
			}
		}

		private int SuspendLayout()
		{
			return ++suspendLayoutRequests;
		}

		private int ResumeLayout(bool positionControls)
		{
			if (suspendLayoutRequests == 0)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			suspendLayoutRequests--;
			if (suspendLayoutRequests == 0 && positionControls)
			{
				PositionAndSizeTabGroups();
			}
			return suspendLayoutRequests;
		}

		private MdiTabGroup GetTabGroupFromSplitter(SplitterBarControl splitter)
		{
			if (splitter != null)
			{
				foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
				{
					if (SplitterControls[nestedTabGroup] == splitter)
					{
						return nestedTabGroup;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void CachedMdiClientSize()
		{
			lastMdiClientSize = MdiClientSize;
		}

		private void ResetComponent(bool deserializing)
		{
			SuspendLayout();
			try
			{
				ResetAllowHorizontalTabGroups();
				ResetAllowVerticalTabGroups();
				ResetAppearance();
				ResetAppearances();
				((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).ResetCursor();
				if (!deserializing)
				{
					ResetImageList();
				}
				ResetImageSize();
				ResetImageTransparentColor();
				ResetMaxTabGroups();
				ResetOrientation();
				ResetShowToolTips();
				ResetSplitterAppearance();
				ResetSplitterBorderStyle();
				ResetSplitterWidth();
				ResetTabGroups();
				ResetTabGroupSettings();
				ResetTabSettings();
				ResetUseMnemonics();
				ResetBorderColor();
				BorderStyle = MdiClientBorderStyle.Solid;
				ResetAllowNestedTabGroups();
				ResetMaximizedTabGroupDisplayStyle();
				ResetIsActiveTabGroupMaximized();
				ResetAllowMaximize();
				((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).AlphaBlendMode = (AlphaBlendMode)2;
				((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).UseFlatMode = (DefaultableBoolean)0;
				((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).UseOsThemes = (DefaultableBoolean)0;
				ResetViewStyle();
				TabNavigationMode = MdiTabNavigationMode.ActivationOrder;
				((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled = true;
			}
			finally
			{
				ResumeLayout(positionControls: true);
			}
		}

		private void ReactivateFocusedControl(Control control)
		{
			if (control == null || !control.ContainsFocus)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (control.Focused)
			{
				using FocusHelper focusHelper = new FocusHelper();
				focusHelper.ToggleFocus(control);
			}
			foreach (Control control2 in control.Controls)
			{
				if (control2.Focused || control2.ContainsFocus)
				{
					ReactivateFocusedControl(control2);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetRedraw(bool redrawOn)
		{
			if (mdiClient == null || !hasUnmanagedCodeRights)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				NativeWindowMethods.SetRedraw(mdiClient, redrawOn, redrawOn);
				if (redrawOn && mdiClient != null)
				{
					ReactivateFocusedControl(mdiClient);
				}
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				hasUnmanagedCodeRights = false;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateBorderStyle()
		{
			if (IsMdiClientSubclassed)
			{
				mdiClientSubclasser.VerifyBorderStyle();
			}
		}

		private void DisableMdiClientRedraw()
		{
			if (mdiClient != null && mdiClient.IsHandleCreated && !mdiClient.IsDisposed && !mdiClient.Disposing)
			{
				NativeWindowMethods.SetRedraw(mdiClient, newValue: false, redraw: false);
			}
		}

		private void DelayedRedraw()
		{
			if (!WaitingForDelayedRedraw)
			{
				if (MdiParent == null || !MdiParent.IsHandleCreated)
				{
					hookedIdle = true;
					Application.Idle += OnApplicationIdle;
				}
				else
				{
					delayedRedraw = MdiParent.BeginInvoke(new MethodInvoker(DelayedMdiClientRedraw));
				}
			}
		}

		private void RefreshSplitterIntersections()
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0013: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0041: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			UIElementBorderStyle splitterBorderStyleResolved = SplitterBorderStyleResolved;
			UIElementBorderWidths val = DrawUtility.CalculateBorderWidths(splitterBorderStyleResolved, Border3DSide.All, (UIElement)null);
			int left = ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref val)).Left;
			bool flag = HasNestedTabGroups && left > 0;
			int num;
			switch (splitterBorderStyleResolved - 2)
			{
			case 0:
			case 1:
			case 2:
				num = -left;
				break;
			default:
				num = 0;
				break;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: false))
			{
				if (!(SplitterControls[nestedTabGroup] is SplitterBarControl splitterBarControl))
				{
					continue;
				}
				splitterBarControl.RemoveIntersections();
				if (!flag)
				{
					continue;
				}
				Rectangle splitterControlRectangle = TabGroupManager.GetSplitterControlRectangle(nestedTabGroup);
				if (splitterBarControl.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
				{
					splitterControlRectangle.Inflate(num, left);
				}
				else
				{
					splitterControlRectangle.Inflate(left, num);
				}
				MdiTabGroup parent = nestedTabGroup.Parent;
				MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup2 = null;
				MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup3 = null;
				while (parent != null && (mdiTabGroup2 == null || mdiTabGroup3 == null))
				{
					if (parent.ParentOrientation != splitterBarControl.Orientation)
					{
						if (mdiTabGroup2 == null)
						{
							mdiTabGroup2 = AddSplitterIntersection(parent, (RelativePosition)0, splitterControlRectangle);
						}
						if (mdiTabGroup3 == null)
						{
							mdiTabGroup3 = AddSplitterIntersection(parent, (RelativePosition)1, splitterControlRectangle);
						}
					}
					parent = parent.Parent;
				}
			}
		}

		private MdiTabGroup AddSplitterIntersection(MdiTabGroup tabGroup, RelativePosition relativePosition, Rectangle splitterRect)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			MdiTabGroup mdiTabGroup;
			if ((int)relativePosition == 0)
			{
				mdiTabGroup = TabGroupFromPosition(tabGroup, MdiTabGroupPosition.Previous);
				if (mdiTabGroup == tabGroup)
				{
					return null;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!TabGroupManager.NeedsSplitter(tabGroup))
				{
					return null;
				}
				mdiTabGroup = tabGroup;
			}
			Rectangle splitterControlRectangle = TabGroupManager.GetSplitterControlRectangle(mdiTabGroup);
			if (!splitterControlRectangle.IsEmpty && splitterRect.IntersectsWith(splitterControlRectangle))
			{
				SplitterBarControl splitterBarControl = SplitterControls[mdiTabGroup] as SplitterBarControl;
				splitterBarControl.AddIntersection(splitterRect);
			}
			return mdiTabGroup;
		}

		private void InvalidateControls(bool dirtyChildElements, bool invalidate, bool splitterControls, bool tabGroupControls)
		{
			if (tabGroupControls)
			{
				foreach (MdiTabGroupControl value in TabGroupControls.Values)
				{
					UIElement mainUIElement = (UIElement)(object)((IUltraControlElement)value).MainUIElement;
					if (mainUIElement != null)
					{
						if (dirtyChildElements)
						{
							mainUIElement.DirtyChildElements(invalidate && mainUIElement.Control.IsHandleCreated && !mainUIElement.IsDrawing);
						}
						else if (invalidate && mainUIElement.Control.IsHandleCreated)
						{
							mainUIElement.Invalidate(false);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (!splitterControls)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (SplitterBarControl value2 in SplitterControls.Values)
			{
				UIElement mainUIElement2 = (UIElement)(object)((IUltraControlElement)value2).MainUIElement;
				if (mainUIElement2 != null)
				{
					if (dirtyChildElements)
					{
						mainUIElement2.DirtyChildElements(invalidate && mainUIElement2.Control.IsHandleCreated && !mainUIElement2.IsDrawing);
					}
					else if (invalidate && mainUIElement2.Control.IsHandleCreated)
					{
						mainUIElement2.Invalidate(false);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void InitializeTabContextMenu(MdiTab tab, MdiTabContextMenu menuType)
		{
			((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Clear();
			if (tab == null || tab.TabGroup == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			MdiTabGroup tabGroup = tab.TabGroup;
			MdiTabGroup previousLeafTabGroup = tabGroup.PreviousLeafTabGroup;
			MdiTabGroup nextLeafTabGroup = tabGroup.NextLeafTabGroup;
			bool flag = CanCreateHorizontalGroup;
			bool flag2 = CanCreateVerticalGroup;
			bool flag3 = nextLeafTabGroup != null;
			bool flag4 = previousLeafTabGroup != null;
			bool allowClose = tab.SettingsResolved.AllowClose;
			bool flag5 = AllowMaximize;
			if (tabGroup.Tabs.Count == 1)
			{
				flag2 = false;
				flag = false;
			}
			if (tab.SettingsResolved.AllowDrag != MdiTabDragStyle.WithinAndAcrossGroups)
			{
				flag3 = false;
				flag4 = false;
				flag2 = false;
				flag = false;
			}
			if (flag3 && !nextLeafTabGroup.SettingsResolved.AllowDrop)
			{
				flag3 = false;
			}
			if (flag4 && !previousLeafTabGroup.SettingsResolved.AllowDrop)
			{
				flag4 = false;
			}
			switch (menuType)
			{
			case MdiTabContextMenu.Default:
				if (allowClose)
				{
					((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.Close));
					if (flag || flag2 || flag3 || flag4)
					{
						((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.CloseSeparator));
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.NewHorizontalGroup));
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.NewVerticalGroup));
				}
				if (flag3)
				{
					((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.MoveToNextGroup));
				}
				if (flag4)
				{
					((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.MoveToPreviousGroup));
				}
				if (flag5)
				{
					if (((DisposableObjectCollectionBase)(object)((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems).Count > 0)
					{
						((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.MaximizeSeparator));
					}
					((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.Maximize));
				}
				break;
			case MdiTabContextMenu.Reposition:
				if (flag)
				{
					((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.NewHorizontalGroup));
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.NewVerticalGroup));
				}
				if (flag3)
				{
					((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.MoveToNextGroup));
				}
				if (flag4)
				{
					((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.MoveToPreviousGroup));
				}
				if (flag || flag2 || flag3 || flag4)
				{
					((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.CancelSeparator));
				}
				((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Add(GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems.Cancel));
				break;
			}
		}

		internal MdiTabMenuItems GetMenuType(MenuItem menuItem)
		{
			if (menuItems != null && menuItem != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < menuItems.Length; i++)
				{
					if ((object)menuItems[i].View == menuItem)
					{
						return (MdiTabMenuItems)i;
					}
				}
			}
			return (MdiTabMenuItems)(-1);
		}

		internal MdiTabMenuItems GetMenuType(ContextMenuItem menuItem)
		{
			if (menuItems != null && menuItem != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < menuItems.Length; i++)
				{
					if (menuItems[i] == menuItem)
					{
						return (MdiTabMenuItems)i;
					}
				}
			}
			return (MdiTabMenuItems)(-1);
		}

		private ContextMenuItem GetMenuItem(MdiTabMenuItems menuItem)
		{
			//IL_0081: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0087: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00b6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00bc: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00ec: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00f2: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0122: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0128: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0145: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_014b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0174: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_017a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0194: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_019a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (menuItems == null)
			{
				menuItems = (ContextMenuItem[])(object)new ContextMenuItem[Enum.GetValues(typeof(MdiTabMenuItems)).Length];
			}
			if (menuItems[(int)menuItem] == null)
			{
				switch (menuItem)
				{
				case MdiTabMenuItems.Close:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem] = new ContextMenuItem(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemClose"), MenuItemClickHandler);
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.CloseSeparator:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem] = ContextMenuManager.CreateSeparator();
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.NewHorizontalGroup:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem] = new ContextMenuItem(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemNewHorizontalGroup"), MenuItemClickHandler);
					menuItems[(int)menuItem].Image = NewHorizontalGroupImage;
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.NewVerticalGroup:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem] = new ContextMenuItem(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemNewVerticalGroup"), MenuItemClickHandler);
					menuItems[(int)menuItem].Image = NewVerticalGroupImage;
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.MoveToNextGroup:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem] = new ContextMenuItem(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemMoveToNextGroup"), MenuItemClickHandler);
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.MoveToPreviousGroup:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem] = new ContextMenuItem(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemMoveToPreviousGroup"), MenuItemClickHandler);
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.CancelSeparator:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem] = ContextMenuManager.CreateSeparator();
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.Cancel:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem] = new ContextMenuItem(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemCancel"), MenuItemClickHandler);
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.Maximize:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem] = new ContextMenuItem(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemMaximize"), MenuItemClickHandler);
					menuItems[(int)menuItem].Checked = IsActiveTabGroupMaximized;
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.MaximizeSeparator:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem] = ContextMenuManager.CreateSeparator();
					break;
				}
				((SubObjectBase)(object)menuItems[(int)menuItem]).SubObjectDisposed += MenuItemDisposedHandler;
			}
			else
			{
				switch (menuItem)
				{
				case MdiTabMenuItems.Close:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem].Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemClose");
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.NewHorizontalGroup:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem].Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemNewHorizontalGroup");
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.NewVerticalGroup:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem].Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemNewVerticalGroup");
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.MoveToNextGroup:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem].Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemMoveToNextGroup");
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.MoveToPreviousGroup:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem].Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemMoveToPreviousGroup");
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.Cancel:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem].Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemCancel");
					break;
				case MdiTabMenuItems.Maximize:
					menuItems[(int)menuItem].Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "MenuItemMaximize");
					menuItems[(int)menuItem].Checked = IsActiveTabGroupMaximized;
					break;
				}
			}
			return menuItems[(int)menuItem];
		}

		private void OnContextMenuPopup(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (ignoreContextMenuPopup)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (sender == tabContextMenuManager)
			{
				MdiTab mdiTab = TabFromPoint(Control.MousePosition);
				if (mdiTab == null)
				{
					((ContextMenuItemBase)tabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Clear();
					return;
				}
				InitializeTabContextMenu(mdiTab, MdiTabContextMenu.Default);
				ICustomMdiTab customMdiTab = mdiTab.CustomMdiTab;
				if (customMdiTab != null)
				{
					customMdiTab.InitializeMenu(TabContextMenuManager);
				}
				tabForContextMenu = mdiTab;
				MdiTabContextMenuEventArgs e2 = new MdiTabContextMenuEventArgs(this, mdiTab, TabContextMenuManager.ContextMenu, MdiTabContextMenu.Default);
				InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.InitializeContextMenu, e2);
				if (e2.Cancel)
				{
					((ContextMenuItemBase)TabContextMenuManager).MenuItems.Clear();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				tabForContextMenu = null;
			}
		}

		private void OnMenuItemClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (tabForContextMenu == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			MdiTabMenuItems menuType = GetMenuType((ContextMenuItem)((sender is ContextMenuItem) ? sender : null));
			MdiTab mdiTab = tabForContextMenu;
			if (mdiTab == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			switch (menuType)
			{
			case MdiTabMenuItems.Cancel:
				break;
			case MdiTabMenuItems.Close:
				mdiTab.Close(MdiTabCloseReason.CloseMenuItem);
				break;
			case MdiTabMenuItems.MoveToNextGroup:
				MoveToGroup(mdiTab, mdiTab.TabGroup.NextLeafTabGroup);
				if (mdiTab.CanActivate)
				{
					mdiTab.Activate();
				}
				break;
			case MdiTabMenuItems.MoveToPreviousGroup:
				MoveToGroup(mdiTab, mdiTab.TabGroup.PreviousLeafTabGroup);
				if (mdiTab.CanActivate)
				{
					mdiTab.Activate();
				}
				break;
			case MdiTabMenuItems.NewHorizontalGroup:
				MoveToNewGroup(mdiTab, mdiTab.TabGroup, (RelativePosition)1, Orientation.Horizontal);
				if (mdiTab.CanActivate)
				{
					mdiTab.Activate();
				}
				break;
			case MdiTabMenuItems.NewVerticalGroup:
				MoveToNewGroup(mdiTab, mdiTab.TabGroup, (RelativePosition)1, Orientation.Vertical);
				if (mdiTab.CanActivate)
				{
					mdiTab.Activate();
				}
				break;
			case MdiTabMenuItems.Maximize:
				IsActiveTabGroupMaximized = !IsActiveTabGroupMaximized;
				break;
			case MdiTabMenuItems.CloseSeparator:
			case MdiTabMenuItems.CancelSeparator:
			case MdiTabMenuItems.MaximizeSeparator:
				break;
			}
		}

		private void OnMenuItemDisposed(SubObjectBase notifier)
		{
			ContextMenuItem val = (ContextMenuItem)(object)((notifier is ContextMenuItem) ? notifier : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			((SubObjectBase)(object)val).SubObjectDisposed -= MenuItemDisposedHandler;
			if (!IsDisposing)
			{
				try
				{
					val.Click -= MenuItemClickHandler;
				}
				catch (NullReferenceException)
				{
				}
			}
			MdiTabMenuItems menuType = GetMenuType(val);
			if (Enum.IsDefined(typeof(MdiTabMenuItems), menuType))
			{
				menuItems[(int)menuType] = null;
			}
		}

		private void InitializeAllowDrop(bool value)
		{
			if (IsInitializing || ((Component)this).DesignMode || !CanSetAllowDrop)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MdiTabGroupControl value2 in TabGroupControls.Values)
			{
				((Control)(object)value2).AllowDrop = value;
			}
		}

		private void ViewStyleChangeDirtyHelper()
		{
			StyleUtils.DirtyCachedPropertyVals(this);
			DirtyTabGroups(MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.AllMetrics | MdiTabGroup.TabManagerChangeType.Font);
			((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).InvalidateControls();
			((UltraComponentBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ViewStyle);
		}

		private void OnOffice2007ColorSchemeChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (ViewStyleResolved == ViewStyle.Office2007)
			{
				ViewStyleChangeDirtyHelper();
			}
		}

		void ISupportInitialize.BeginInit()
		{
			isInitializing = true;
			OnBeginInit();
		}

		void ISupportInitialize.EndInit()
		{
			isInitializing = false;
			HookMdiParent();
			OnEndInit();
		}

		Image ITabbedMdiManager.GetFormImage(Form mdiChildForm)
		{
			return GetTabFromForm(mdiChildForm)?.GetResolvedImage();
		}

		string ITabbedMdiManager.GetFormText(Form mdiChildForm)
		{
			MdiTab tabFromForm = GetTabFromForm(mdiChildForm);
			if (tabFromForm == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return tabFromForm.TextResolved;
		}

		string ITabbedMdiManager.GetFormToolTipText(Form mdiChildForm)
		{
			MdiTab tabFromForm = GetTabFromForm(mdiChildForm);
			if (tabFromForm == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return tabFromForm.ToolTipResolved;
		}

		public void LoadComponentSettings()
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			bool flag3 = false;
			bool flag4 = true;
			SaveSettingsFormat val = saveSettingsFormat;
			if ((int)val != 0)
			{
				if ((int)val == 1)
				{
					flag4 = false;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				flag4 = true;
			}
			if (flag4)
			{
				flag2 = true;
				if (!LoadComponentSettingsFromBinary() && !flag3)
				{
					LoadComponentSettingsFromXml();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				flag3 = true;
				if (!LoadComponentSettingsFromXml() && !flag2)
				{
					LoadComponentSettingsFromBinary();
				}
			}
		}

		private bool LoadComponentSettingsFromBinary()
		{
			bool result = false;
			byte[] binaryData = Settings.BinaryData;
			if (binaryData != null && binaryData.Length != 0)
			{
				using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
				memoryStream.Write(binaryData, 0, binaryData.Length);
				memoryStream.Position = 0L;
				try
				{
					LoadFromBinary(memoryStream);
					result = true;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void ResetComponentSettings()
		{
			((ApplicationSettingsBase)(object)Settings).Reset();
		}

		public void SaveComponentSettings()
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0014: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			bool flag = true;
			SaveSettingsFormat val = saveSettingsFormat;
			if ((int)val != 0)
			{
				if ((int)val == 1)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				SaveAsBinary(memoryStream);
				memoryStream.Position = 0L;
				byte[] array = new byte[memoryStream.Length];
				memoryStream.Read(array, 0, (int)memoryStream.Length);
				Settings.BinaryData = array;
				((SettingsBase)(object)Settings).Save();
			}
			else
			{
				SaveAsXml(memoryStream);
				memoryStream.Position = 0L;
				StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(memoryStream);
				Settings.XmlData = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
				((SettingsBase)(object)Settings).Save();
			}
		}

		protected override void InvalidateControls()
		{
			InvalidateControls(dirtyChildElements: false, invalidate: true, splitterControls: true, tabGroupControls: true);
		}

		protected override void DirtyChildElements()
		{
			InvalidateControls(dirtyChildElements: true, invalidate: true, splitterControls: true, tabGroupControls: true);
		}

		protected override void ChangeControlsVisibleState()
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_48"));
		}

		protected override void ChangeControlsEnabledState()
		{
			if (!IsInitializing)
			{
				((EventHandler)((UltraComponentBase)this).Events[EventEnabledChanging])?.Invoke(this, new EventArgs());
				if (((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled)
				{
					InitializeMdiTabForms(restore: false);
					CreateTabGroupControls();
					CreateSplitterControls();
					UpdateBorderStyle();
					PositionAndSizeTabGroups();
				}
				else
				{
					RemoveSplitterControls();
					RemoveTabGroupControls();
					InitializeMdiTabForms(restore: true);
					UpdateBorderStyle();
				}
				((EventHandler)((UltraComponentBase)this).Events[EventEnabledChanged])?.Invoke(this, new EventArgs());
				if (!((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).Enabled)
				{
					mdiClient.LayoutMdi(MdiLayout.Cascade);
				}
			}
		}

		public override void EndUpdate(bool invalidate)
		{
			((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).EndUpdate(invalidate);
			PositionAndSizeTabGroups();
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.ChangeInfo.PropId is TabbedMdiPropertyIds)
			{
				switch ((TabbedMdiPropertyIds)(object)e.ChangeInfo.PropId)
				{
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.SplitterAppearance:
					InvalidateControls(dirtyChildElements: false, invalidate: true, splitterControls: true, tabGroupControls: false);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiPropertyIds.ImageSize:
					if (HasTabGroups)
					{
						foreach (MdiTabGroup nestedTabGroup in TabGroups.GetNestedTabGroups(leafGroupsOnly: true))
						{
							for (int i = 0; i < nestedTabGroup.Tabs.Count; i++)
							{
								nestedTabGroup.Tabs[i].DisposeIconImage();
							}
						}
					}
					foreach (MdiTab hiddenTab in HiddenTabs)
					{
						hiddenTab.DisposeIconImage();
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			((UltraComponentBase)this).OnPropertyChanged(e);
		}

		protected override ComponentRole CreateComponentRole()
		{
			return (ComponentRole)(object)new UltraTabbedMdiManagerRole(this);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnterElement(UIElementEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!EventManager.CanFireEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.MouseEnterElement))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (e == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_50"));
			}
			EventManager.IncrementInProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.MouseEnterElement);
			try
			{
				((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).OnMouseEnterElement(e);
			}
			catch
			{
				throw;
			}
			finally
			{
				EventManager.DecrementInProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.MouseEnterElement);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeaveElement(UIElementEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!EventManager.CanFireEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds.MouseLeaveElement))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (e == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_50"));
			}
			EventManager.IncrementInProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.MouseLeaveElement);
			try
			{
				((UltraComponentControlManagerBase)this).OnMouseLeaveElement(e);
			}
			catch
			{
				throw;
			}
			finally
			{
				EventManager.DecrementInProgress(TabbedMdiEventIds.MouseLeaveElement);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnTabClosing(CancelableMdiTabEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_30"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, "LE_V2_Exception_53"));
			}
			((CancelableMdiTabEventHandler)((UltraComponentBase)this).Events[EventTabClosing])?.Invoke(this, e);
		}

		internal void InvokeEvent(TabbedMdiEventIds eventId, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!EventManager.CanFireEvent(eventId) || !Enum.IsDefined(typeof(TabbedMdiEventIds), eventId))
			{
				return;
			}
			EventManager.IncrementInProgress(eventId);
			try
			{
				switch (eventId)
				{
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.InitializeContextMenu:
					OnInitializeContextMenu(e as MdiTabContextMenuEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.InitializeTab:
					OnInitializeTab(e as MdiTabEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.InitializeTabGroup:
					OnInitializeTabGroup(e as MdiTabGroupEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.RestoreTab:
					OnRestoreTab(e as RestoreTabEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabClosing:
					OnTabClosing(e as CancelableMdiTabEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabClosed:
					OnTabClosed(e as MdiTabEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabDragging:
					OnTabDragging(e as CancelableMdiTabEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabDragOver:
					OnTabDragOver(e as MdiTabDragOverEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabDropped:
					OnTabDropped(e as MdiTabDroppedEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabGroupResizing:
					OnTabGroupResizing(e as MdiTabGroupResizingEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabGroupResized:
					OnTabGroupResized(e as MdiTabGroupResizedEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabGroupScrolling:
					OnTabGroupScrolling(e as MdiTabGroupScrollingEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabGroupScrolled:
					OnTabGroupScrolled(e as MdiTabGroupScrolledEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabSelecting:
					OnTabSelecting(e as CancelableMdiTabEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabSelected:
					OnTabSelected(e as MdiTabEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabDisplayed:
					OnTabDisplayed(e as MdiTabEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabDisplaying:
					OnTabDisplaying(e as MdiTabEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.SplitterDragged:
					OnSplitterDragged(e as MdiTabGroupEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.SplitterDragging:
					OnSplitterDragging(e as MdiSplitterDraggingEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabMoving:
					OnTabMoving(e as MdiTabMovingEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabMoved:
					OnTabMoved(e as MdiTabEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.StoreTab:
					OnStoreTab(e as StoreTabEventArgs);
					break;
				case TabbedMdiEventIds.TabActivated:
					OnTabActivated(e as MdiTabEventArgs);
					break;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				EventManager.DecrementInProgress(eventId);
			}
		}

		internal void OnTouchModeChanged()
		{
			TabGroupManager.DirtyAllTabGroups();
			PositionAndSizeTabGroups();
		}
	}
	internal class Utilities
	{
		private Utilities()
		{
		}

		internal static TabScrollButtons ToTabScrollButtons(DefaultableTabScrollButtons buttons)
		{
			return (TabScrollButtons)(buttons switch
			{
				DefaultableTabScrollButtons.None => 0, 
				DefaultableTabScrollButtons.Always => 1, 
				_ => 2, 
			});
		}

		internal static bool ShouldSetAppearanceProperty(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps, AppearancePropFlags appearanceProperty)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).HasPropertyBeenSet(appearanceProperty))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return (AppearancePropFlags)(requestedProps & appearanceProperty) == appearanceProperty;
		}

		internal static void ActivateForm(Form form)
		{
			try
			{
				form.Activate();
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				if (!form.Visible || !form.IsHandleCreated)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (form.IsMdiChild)
				{
					if (!(GetParent(form) is MdiClient mdiClient))
					{
						return;
					}
					try
					{
						NativeWindowMethods.SendMessageApi(mdiClient.Handle, 546, form.Handle, IntPtr.Zero);
						return;
					}
					catch (SecurityException)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				try
				{
					NativeWindowMethods.SetForegroundWindowApi(form.Handle);
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}
		}

		internal static bool FocusControl(Control control)
		{
			try
			{
				return control.Focus();
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				try
				{
					if (control.CanFocus)
					{
						NativeWindowMethods.SetFocusApi(control.Handle);
					}
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}
			return control.Focused;
		}

		internal static Form GetMdiParent(Form form)
		{
			try
			{
				UIPermission uIPermission = new UIPermission(UIPermissionWindow.AllWindows);
				if (Utilities.CanAssert)
				{
					uIPermission.Assert();
				}
				return form.MdiParent;
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				return null;
			}
		}

		internal static Control GetParent(Control control)
		{
			try
			{
				UIPermission uIPermission = new UIPermission(UIPermissionWindow.AllWindows);
				if (Utilities.CanAssert)
				{
					uIPermission.Assert();
				}
				return control.Parent;
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				return null;
			}
		}

		internal static bool IsVerticalTabOrientation(TabOrientation tabOrientation)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			switch (tabOrientation - 1)
			{
			default:
				return false;
			case 4:
			case 5:
			case 6:
			case 7:
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal static Form GetOwner(Form form)
		{
			try
			{
				UIPermission uIPermission = new UIPermission(UIPermissionWindow.AllWindows);
				if (Utilities.CanAssert)
				{
					uIPermission.Assert();
				}
				return form.Owner;
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				return null;
			}
		}
	}

namespace Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabbedMdi.Serialization
{
	public class Binder : Binder
	{
		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.Demand, SerializationFormatter = true)]
		public override Type BindToType(string assemblyName, string typeName)
		{
			if (assemblyName == null || !assemblyName.StartsWith("Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabbedMdi"))
			{
				return ((Binder)this).BindToType(assemblyName, typeName);
			}
			Type type = null;
			try
			{
				assemblyName = typeof(UltraTabbedMdiManager).Assembly.FullName;
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				assemblyName = "Infragistics4.Win.UltraWinTabbedMdi.v21.1";
			}
			type = Type.GetType(string.Format(null, "{0}, {1}", new object[2] { typeName, assemblyName }));
			if (type == null)
			{
				return ((Binder)this).BindToType(assemblyName, typeName);
			}
			return type;
		}
	}
}
namespace Infragistics.Shared
{
	internal sealed class SR
	{
		private static Infragistics.Shared.SR loader;

		private ResourceManager[] resources;

		private SR()
		{
			string text = "Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabbedMdi.strings";
			string[] array = text.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string text2 in array2)
			{
				if (text2 != null && text2.Length > 0)
				{
					num++;
				}
			}
			resources = new ResourceManager[num];
			Assembly assembly = GetType().Assembly;
			int num2 = 0;
			string[] array3 = array;
			foreach (string text3 in array3)
			{
				if (text3 != null && text3.Length > 0)
				{
					resources[num2++] = new ResourceManager(text3, assembly);
				}
			}
		}

		private static Infragistics.Shared.SR GetLoader()
		{
			if (loader == null)
			{
				lock (typeof(Infragistics.Shared.SR))
				{
					if (loader == null)
					{
						loader = new Infragistics.Shared.SR();
					}
				}
			}
			return loader;
		}

		public static string GetString(string name)
		{
			return GetString(null, name);
		}

		public static string GetString(string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return GetString(null, name, args);
		}

		public static string GetString(CultureInfo culture, string name)
		{
			return GetString(culture, name, null);
		}

		public static string GetString(CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return GetStringHelper(allowCustomizedStrings: true, culture, name, args);
		}

		private static string GetStringHelper(bool allowCustomizedStrings, CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
		{
			string text = null;
			if (allowCustomizedStrings)
			{
				text = global::ResourceCustomizerLocator.Customizer.GetCustomizedString(name);
			}
			if (text == null)
			{
				Infragistics.Shared.SR sR = GetLoader();
				if (sR == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				text = sR.GetString(name, culture);
				if (text == null)
				{
					text = string.Empty;
				}
			}
			if (args != null && args.Length != 0 && text.Length > 2)
			{
				try
				{
					text = string.Format(null, text, args);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		private string GetString(string name, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			ResourceManager[] array = resources;
			foreach (ResourceManager resourceManager in array)
			{
				string text = resourceManager.GetString(name, culture);
				if (text != null)
				{
					return text;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static string GetUncustomizedString(string name)
		{
			return GetUncustomizedString(null, name);
		}

		public static string GetUncustomizedString(string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return GetUncustomizedString(null, name, args);
		}

		public static string GetUncustomizedString(CultureInfo culture, string name)
		{
			return GetUncustomizedString(culture, name, null);
		}

		public static string GetUncustomizedString(CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return GetStringHelper(allowCustomizedStrings: false, culture, name, args);
		}

		public static object GetObject(string name)
		{
			return GetObject(null, name);
		}

		public static object GetObject(CultureInfo culture, string name)
		{
			return GetLoader()?.GetObject(name, culture);
		}

		private object GetObject(string name, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			ResourceManager[] array = resources;
			foreach (ResourceManager resourceManager in array)
			{
				object obj = resourceManager.GetObject(name, culture);
				if (obj != null)
				{
					return obj;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}

	internal sealed class LocalizedDescriptionAttribute : DescriptionAttribute
	{
		private bool loaded;

		public override string Description
		{
			get
			{
				if (!loaded)
				{
					loaded = true;
					base.DescriptionValue = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetUncustomizedString(null, base.Description);
				}
				return base.Description;
			}
		}

		public LocalizedDescriptionAttribute(string description)
			: base(description)
		{
		}
	}

		{

				}
				return displayName;

		}

		}
